{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_c888e18c5fb649ce9e2869b6e577132a|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2362260ec819d8102389fac270936","name":"read","arguments":{"path":"/tmp/issue81.md","offset":null,"limit":null}}],"api":"openai-codex-responses","provider":"openai-codex","model":"gpt-6.1-sol","usage":{"input":17057,"output":30,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"reasoning":0,"totalTokens":17087,"cost":{"input":0.034114,"output":0.00030000000000000003,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0.034414}},"stopReason":"toolUse","timestamp":1793209888109,"responseId":"resp_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236213530819d82bad6df4af104bc","rawStopReason":"completed","durationMs":2524,"thinkingLevel":"high"},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:30.644Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_c888e18c5fb649ce9e2869b6e577132a|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2362260ec819d8102389fac270936","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"<!-- omg-work-item:management-openapi -->\n\n## Objective\nPublish a machine-readable management contract grounded in actual handlers, authority and error/money types.\n\nParent tracking issue: #38.\n\n**Status:** Planned work item; specification and implementation plan require approval. Filing is not implementation authority or acceptance of held work.\n\n## Scope and acceptance\n- [ ] Document supported management operations with exact status-string error.code, machine error.reason and integer micro-USD strings.\n- [ ] CI detects handler/DTO/schema drift and unauthorized undocumented operations; inference-key denial and personal privacy are represented correctly.\n- [ ] Generate/validate the artifact from reviewed source without secret examples or invented routes; site publication stays separately authorized.\n\n## Verification\n- [ ] Add baseline/red regressions and positive controls; retain existing assertions, no skips or weakened gates.\n- [ ] `cargo test --workspace --all-features`\n- [ ] `npm run typecheck:web`\n- [ ] `npm run test:web`\n- [ ] Exercise this issue's actual API/runtime/browser path and negative authority cases; mock/fake-wire coverage is not live acceptance.\n\nDatabase gates require a fresh, explicitly selected disposable database and separately authorized serialized role/ACL changes. Frontend gates require a fresh lockfile install, not a held patched cache. No real provider calls without explicit cost approval. Listed commands are future gates, not completed results.\n\n## Dependencies\nBlocked by: None currently fixed; confirm conditional/recommended dependencies during specification.\n\n## Likely source areas\n- `apps/gateway/src/management/`\n- `docs/management-api.md`\n- `tests/ (new contract checks)`\n- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`\n\n## Decisions before implementation\nChoose generation/check approach without adding dependencies before approval; website/docs-site publication is deferred.\n\n## Delivery slices and checkpoint\n1. Approve the scoped spec, authority/compatibility contract and testable success criteria.\n2. Add regressions, then implement one complete path in small source/test slices (normally no more than about five files per implementation task).\n3. Run applicable complete gates and review runtime evidence before selecting the next slice.\n4. Parent-owned secret scan, scoped commit, exact-SHA CI and verified release before another issue starts.\n\n## Release and exclusions\nPatch if contract-only; minor if new API behavior is introduced. One implementation issue per branch and release; parent epics are tracking containers, not release bundles.\n\nNo automatic old-installation reset/migration, global inference limits, changed personal privacy, weakened tests, paid/live deployment, secrets-manager integration, website publication or unrelated dependency upgrades. Changes to APIs/schema/ACLs/dependencies/CI require explicit approval.\n\n\n=====COMMENT=====\n## Implementation specification: #81 drift-checked management OpenAPI 3.1\n\n\n## Decision and scope\n\nChoose **(c), a hybrid**: generate route, authorization and shared-error metadata from reviewed Rust source; hand-write schemas and validate them against DTOs and real handler responses.\n\nThis is smaller than converting SQL-built `Json<Value>` responses into derived types. Pure hand-maintenance risks duplicating authorization incorrectly. `Cargo.lock` contains schemars 0.9.0/1.2.2 and syn, but no utoipa or JSON Schema validator. Do not introduce utoipa/procedural macros: they would add build-time execution and supply-chain review without resolving dynamic response shapes.\n\nCover every declared `/api/v1/*` operation, including anonymous authentication/branding routes and authorized `410` installation-policy tombstones. Exclude inference (#82), SCIM, health and metrics. No API, database or authorization behavior changes are authorized.\n\n## Artifact and generation\n\nCommit OpenAPI **3.1.0 JSON** at `docs/openapi/management.openapi.json`, with reviewed schema/operation fragments under `docs/openapi/management/`.\n\nUse deterministic, test-only generation from compiled `ROUTES`, expanding space-separated methods and retaining exact parameter names. Combine generated metadata with fragments; reject duplicate operations, unmatched fragments, unresolved references and missing path parameters. Normal tests compare regeneration in memory against the committed artifact; writing requires an explicit developer regeneration mode. No production dependency or runtime specification endpoint.\n\nDocument implicit HEAD behavior without inventing separately registered operations.\n\n## Authority and privacy\n\nGenerate `x-omg-access` and token-policy extensions directly from `Access` and `TokenPolicy`.\n\nSecurity alternatives:\n\n- Browser: `omg_session` cookie; mutations additionally require `X-CSRF-Token` and exact configured `Origin`, represented as conjunctive header security requirements.\n- Admin token: HTTP bearer, only on individually `Scope(…)`-declared operations. Record the exact scope in an extension, not OAuth scopes on a bearer scheme.\n- Anonymous operations: explicit empty security requirements.\n\n`Denied` never permits tokens; `BrowserAdminOnly` requires a browser-session Platform Admin, including GET token inventory. Write scopes do not imply read scopes. Describe live owner/role checks, credential-family exclusivity, duplicate-header refusal and fail-closed undeclared routes. Never define inference keys as management credentials.\n\nAccess extensions describe minimum boundaries, not complete authority. Add tested operation-specific visibility rules: personal details owner-only; platform personal totals permitted; shared members’ own activity versus administrators’ workspace-wide activity; object ownership and hidden `404`s. Auditor platform authority is read-only, while independent User/membership entitlements remain usable. Define distinct redacted response variants, including webhook references and admission-ceiling identities, rather than optional sensitive fields everywhere.\n\n## Schema requirements\n\nRequest objects mirror serde names, defaults, requiredness, nullability and `deny_unknown_fields` through `additionalProperties:false`, recursively. Explicitly distinguish omission from null for custom deserializers: policy `nullable`/`supplied`/`present`, PATCH fields and reconciliation’s required-nullable `billing_usage`. Include nested types outside management, manually decoded webhook bodies, empty-body operations, query parameters, multipart uploads and binary responses.\n\nMoney uses exact integer micro-USD strings, never numbers or floats; preserve field-specific signedness/ranges and nullable unknown values. Counts and limits retain their actual wire types.\n\nGenerate shared error vocabulary from `REASONS`, `POLICY_REASONS` and inventoried detailed-error call sites. Validate serialization of `{error:{code,message,reason?}}`: `code` equals the HTTP status string. Document reason-specific details such as `period`, `limit`, `missing_meters` and retry information. Framework extraction errors retain their actual status/content type; do not falsely promise the envelope universally.\n\n## RED-first drift gates\n\n1. Add failing controls for omitted/extra operations, wrong method/template, changed access/scope, undocumented reasons and corrupted schemas. Preserve the independent source-route inventory and all existing authorization assertions.\n2. Reuse syn/source inventories to bind handlers and recursively reachable DTOs to schemas. Compare properties, types, requiredness, nullability and closure; fail on unsupported serde constructs rather than silently approximating. Custom deserializers require schema-versus-real-deserialization boundary vectors.\n3. Extend `configuration_matrix_tests.rs`: retain successful controls before unknown-field probes; validate both requests and responses. Add nested unknown-field, missing-field, wrong-type and null/omission cases, including manually decoded bodies.\n4. Validate actual status, headers, media type and response body in existing integration helpers before JSON fallback discards extraction failures. Require successful/nonempty fixtures and redacted variants for every operation, or its intentional tombstone response. No permissive placeholder schemas at completion.\n5. Extend served-router authz/token matrices to validate denial contracts while retaining inference-key, foreign-personal, Auditor, scope, CSRF and Origin checks. Schema validation supplements—not replaces—privacy assertions.\n\n## Tooling, delivery and effort\n\nPropose dev-only `jsonschema = \"=0.33.0\"` with default features disabled, explicit Draft 2020-12 and local-only reference resolution. Approval requires maintenance/advisory and transitive build-script/proc-macro review. Vendor checksum-pinned OpenAPI metaschemas/dialects; no schema fetching, package installation or upstream calls during validation.\n\nPR1 delivers route/auth/error skeleton and drift tests; subsequent small PRs complete schemas by area: identity/access, catalogs/setup, governance/pricing/reporting, then remaining settings/alerts/logs/files/batches. Intermediate work remains explicitly incomplete; acceptance requires exhaustive coverage.\n\nUse gateway release `info.version`, stable operation IDs and reviewed contract diffs; contract-only release is patch. Run existing all-feature Rust and web gates, plus real loopback/browser refusal paths on fresh disposable PostgreSQL. Publication remains separately authorized.\n\nEstimate **15–25 engineer-days plus reviews**. Main risks: dynamic SQL shapes, custom deserialization, redaction variants and incomplete positive fixtures.\n\n## Coordinator decisions (approved for implementation)\n\n1. **Approach (c) hybrid** as specified; no utoipa/proc-macro generators, no runtime spec endpoint.\n2. **Dev-only `jsonschema`** pinned exactly, default features off, local references only — approved subject to the PR1 supply-chain review (version, maintenance/advisories, transitive build scripts/proc macros recorded in the PR). Vendored metaschemas checksum-pinned; no network at test time.\n3. **Forcing function:** once PR1 merges, every PR that adds or changes a management route must update the fragments in the same PR (drift gates fail otherwise). Unmerged route-adding PRs (#102 scheduled reports, #48 cost reconciliation) rebase onto it.\n4. Slices: PR1 route/auth/error skeleton + drift gates (placeholder schemas explicitly marked incomplete and counted); then schemas by area. #81 closes only at exhaustive coverage with no placeholders. Publication on the docs site remains separately authorized.\n\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":5,"timestamp":1793209890642},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:30.644Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_2f669e7932ca47bda988554b236419e3|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23623dc64819d8dfda3878447e417","name":"read","arguments":{"path":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/docs/openapi/management/README.md","offset":null,"limit":null}},{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_fc049c098b484c398b44a5c9607f72bf|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236257a8c819d9e38882dc532e248","name":"grep","arguments":{"pattern":"management_openapi","path":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src","limit":100}},{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_c62fac82792349969bb426167d4eaa14|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236257a8c819db8a1f6c0ec021dd4","name":"ls","arguments":{"path":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/docs/openapi/management","limit":100}},{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_be48613b22e44990acd4eb54da528716|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236257a8c819d85d9aa14bf5459bb","name":"find","arguments":{"pattern":"**/*integration*","path":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway","limit":100}}],"api":"openai-codex-responses","provider":"openai-codex","model":"gpt-6.1-sol","usage":{"input":2186,"output":179,"cacheRead":16896,"cacheWrite":0,"reasoning":0,"totalTokens":19261,"cost":{"input":0.004372,"output":0.0017900000000000001,"cacheRead":0.0016896,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0.0078516}},"stopReason":"toolUse","timestamp":1793209890646,"responseId":"resp_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23622f7e8819da907555c7c5a9de0","rawStopReason":"completed","durationMs":2845,"thinkingLevel":"high"},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:33.537Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_2f669e7932ca47bda988554b236419e3|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23623dc64819d8dfda3878447e417","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# Management OpenAPI contract maintenance\n\n[management.openapi.json](../management.openapi.json) is the **incomplete PR1 skeleton** for #81, not a finished client-generation or body-validation contract. The test-only generator reads compiled `route_policy::ROUTES`, expands declared methods and includes exactly `/api/v1/*`. Inference, SCIM, health and metrics are excluded; anonymous authentication/branding operations and authorized installation-policy tombstones are included. Implicit HEAD follows GET policy without synthetic operations. `info.version` is the gateway package version.\n\nThe skeleton now has 251 operations, 40 shared error reasons and **502 incomplete request/response schema slots**. `management_openapi_reports_incomplete_schemas` reports and pins progress; later schema slices must deliberately reduce the baseline. #81 stays open until exhaustive schema and real-handler coverage is complete with no placeholders.\n\n## Run the gates\n\nFrom the repository root:\n\n```sh\ncargo test -p open-model-gateway --lib management_openapi -- --nocapture\n```\n\nNormal tests regenerate in memory and compare exact deterministic bytes with the committed artifact. To write it explicitly, after reviewing fragment changes:\n\n```sh\nOMG_REGENERATE_OPENAPI=1 cargo test -p open-model-gateway --lib management_openapi_artifact_matches_regeneration\n```\n\nWriting is forbidden whenever `CI` is set; there is no regeneration default, network access or production endpoint. Regeneration validates the document before writing. Review and commit the JSON diff; never regenerate in CI to conceal drift.\n\n## Reviewed fragments\n\nEvery `.json` file in this directory has the following shape (this example is not a complete inventory):\n\n```json\n{\n  \"operations\": {\n    \"GET /api/v1/me\": {\n      \"access\": \"Browser\",\n      \"token_policy\": \"Denied\",\n      \"scope\": null,\n      \"operation\": {}\n    }\n  },\n  \"schemas\": {}\n}\n```\n\nThe initial `operations.json` registers every operation. When adding/changing a management route, update its exact method/template and access/token/scope snapshot here **in the same PR**. Route-only changes fail even with artifact regeneration. These snapshots are review acknowledgements, not a second authorization source: extensions and security requirements always come from compiled `Access`/`TokenPolicy`. Missing/unmatched entries, duplicate operation/schema fragments (including duplicate top-level map keys), unknown fragment fields and overrides of generated authority/IDs are errors.\n\n`operation` supplies hand-written OpenAPI fields such as summaries, descriptions, responses and request bodies. Generated path parameters are immutable; fragments may append inline non-path parameters. Schema fragments go in `schemas`; they may replace a generated placeholder by its exact component name. The shared `ErrorEnvelope` is generated and cannot be overridden.\n\nBody slots are referenced by operation extensions `x-omg-request-schema` and `x-omg-response-schema`. Each initially points to a distinct component marked `x-omg-schema-status: incomplete`. They deliberately do **not** fabricate request-body presence, JSON media types, successful statuses or universally enveloped errors; the default response is descriptive only. Later slices add real `requestBody`/`responses`, body absence, media types, query parameters, DTO constraints and privacy-specific variants. Replacing a slot must remove its incomplete marker only after the corresponding behavior is tested. Placeholders are never acceptance validators for actual requests/responses.\n\nAccess extensions express minimum boundaries, not complete authority. Live ownership, membership, platform roles, hidden 404s and personal/shared-workspace visibility remain enforced by handlers. The shared error schema documents the handler envelope and generated reason vocabulary, not every framework extraction response or every reason-specific detail. Existing source-route and authorization tests are unchanged.\n\n## Offline schema safety\n\nThe dev-only validator uses explicit Draft 2020-12 and the linear-time `regex` backend with a compilation-size limit. Invalid/unsupported regexes (including look-around/backreferences) fail schema compilation; RED controls exercise both `pattern` and `patternProperties`, including under `not`. Every component, including unused fragments, and inline Schema Object is compiled before the artifact can be written. Artifact references must be local `#/...` JSON Pointers; nested `$id` rebasing is forbidden.\n\nThe [vendored metaschemas](../vendor/README.md) are SHA-256 checked before loading. Only their exact identifiers resolve to checked-in bytes; unknown URI retrieval is refused, with HTTP/file/default features disabled. The tests check OpenAPI structure, schema syntax, references, path parameters, authority, reason coverage, fragment matching and artifact drift. They do not yet bind all DTOs or validate every real handler response; those remain later #81 slices.\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":14,"timestamp":1793209893534},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:33.537Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_5176781a86c14c97a31e165bca663f18|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23628f77c819d937265359cd39bbd","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Every JSON management write gets a successful control before the same\n//! body plus an unknown configuration field. The source inventory below makes\n//! newly added Json extractors fail until they have a real HTTP case here.\nuse super::*;\nuse std::collections::BTreeSet;\nuse syn::visit::{self, Visit};\n\nfn json_handlers() -> BTreeSet<String> {\n    struct Handlers {\n        module: String,\n        names: BTreeSet<String>,\n    }\n    impl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for Handlers {\n        fn visit_item_mod(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ItemMod) {\n            if !crate::configuration_source_tests::test_only(&node.attrs) {\n                visit::visit_item_mod(self, node);\n            }\n        }\n        fn visit_item_fn(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ItemFn) {\n            if crate::configuration_source_tests::test_only(&node.attrs) {\n                return;\n            }\n            // `Json<T>`, or a handler that inspects the rejection itself\n            // (`Result<Json<T>, JsonRejection>`, `Option<Json<T>>`) so it can\n            // refuse without echoing input: both are JSON write extractors.\n            // A helper generic over the body type (`fn f<T>(Result<Json<T>, _>)`)\n            // is not a route handler: Axum handlers extract a concrete DTO.\n            let generic: BTreeSet<String> = node\n                .sig\n                .generics\n                .type_params()\n                .map(|t| t.ident.to_string())\n                .collect();\n            fn is_json(ty: &syn::Type, generic: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {\n                let syn::Type::Path(p) = ty else {\n                    return false;\n                };\n                let Some(last) = p.path.segments.last() else {\n                    return false;\n                };\n                if last.ident == \"Json\" {\n                    let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(args) = &last.arguments else {\n                        return true;\n                    };\n                    return !matches!(\n                        args.args.first(),\n                        Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(syn::Type::Path(inner)))\n                            if inner.path.get_ident().is_some_and(|i| generic.contains(&i.to_string()))\n                    );\n                }\n                if last.ident != \"Result\" && last.ident != \"Option\" {\n                    return false;\n                }\n                let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(args) = &last.arguments else {\n                    return false;\n                };\n                matches!(args.args.first(), Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(inner)) if is_json(inner, generic))\n            }\n            let json = node.sig.inputs.iter().any(|arg| match arg {\n                syn::FnArg::Typed(p) => is_json(&p.ty, &generic),\n                _ => false,\n            });\n            if json {\n                self.names\n                    .insert(format!(\"{}::{}\", self.module, node.sig.ident));\n            }\n            visit::visit_item_fn(self, node);\n        }\n    }\n    let mut names = BTreeSet::new();\n    for source in crate::configuration_source_tests::production_sources() {\n        let Some(module) = source.name.strip_prefix(\"management/\") else {\n            continue;\n        };\n        let mut visitor = Handlers {\n            module: module.trim_end_matches(\".rs\").replace('/', \"::\"),\n            names: BTreeSet::new(),\n        };\n        visitor.visit_file(&source.ast);\n        names.extend(visitor.names);\n    }\n    names\n}\n\n#[sqlx::test(migrations = \"./enterprise_migrations\")]\nasync fn every_json_management_write_rejects_unknown_fields_after_valid_control(pool: PgPool) {\n    use crate::providers::secrets::EnvSecrets;\n    let mut f = fixture(&pool).await;\n    f.s = f.s.with_secret_allowlists(\n        EnvSecrets::from_lookup([\"OPENAI_API_KEY\".into()], |_| None),\n        EnvSecrets::default(),\n    );\n    let ws = f.team;\n    let model_id = model(&pool, \"matrix-existing\").await;\n    let connection = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let deployment = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let catalog = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let account = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let mapping = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let price_id = Uuid::new_v4();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO provider_connections(id,name,provider,credential_ref,enabled) VALUES($1,'Matrix','openai','env:OPENAI_API_KEY',true)\").bind(connection).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO deployments(id,model_id,provider_connection_id,upstream_model,enabled) VALUES($1,$2,$3,'existing',true)\").bind(deployment).bind(model_id).bind(connection).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO deployment_prices(id,deployment_id,input_microusd_per_million,output_microusd_per_million,input_token_limit,output_token_limit,pricing_version) VALUES($1,$2,1000000,1000000,100,100,1)\").bind(price_id).bind(deployment).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO catalogs(id,name) VALUES($1,'Matrix')\")\n        .bind(catalog)\n        .execute(&pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO catalog_models(catalog_id,model_id) VALUES($1,$2)\")\n        .bind(catalog)\n        .bind(model_id)\n        .execute(&pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO workspace_type_catalogs(kind,catalog_id) VALUES('team',$1)\")\n        .bind(catalog)\n        .execute(&pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO service_accounts(id,workspace_id,name) VALUES($1,$2,'Existing')\")\n        .bind(account)\n        .bind(ws)\n        .execute(&pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO oidc_group_mappings(id,issuer,group_value,target_kind,platform_role,enabled) VALUES($1,'https://idp.example.test','existing','platform','user',false)\").bind(mapping).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    let (status, k) = key(&f, &f.owner, ws, Value::Null).await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let key_id = id(&k);\n    let (status, reconciliation_key) = key(&f, &f.owner, ws, Value::Null).await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let reconciliation_key_id = id(&reconciliation_key);\n    let accepting = user(&pool, \"accepting\", \"user\").await;\n    let (status, invitation) = call(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.owner,\n        \"POST\",\n        &format!(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/invitations\"),\n        json!({\"email\":accepting.email,\"role\":\"member\"}),\n    )\n    .await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let execution = Uuid::new_v4();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO inference_executions(id,workspace_id,api_key_id,deployment_id,public_model,provider,streamed,state,root_request_id,attempt_number) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,'matrix-existing','openai',false,'succeeded',$1,1)\").bind(execution).bind(ws).bind(reconciliation_key_id).bind(deployment).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO governance_reservations(execution_id,workspace_id,api_key_id,deployment_id,admitted_at,minute_start,month_start,lease_expires_at,state,held_microusd,unbounded_cost,price_id) SELECT $1,$2,$3,$4,e.started_at,date_trunc('minute',e.started_at,'UTC'),date_trunc('month',e.started_at,'UTC'),now()-interval '1 day','unknown',10,false,$5 FROM inference_executions e WHERE e.id=$1\").bind(execution).bind(ws).bind(reconciliation_key_id).bind(deployment).bind(price_id).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    let cost_center = Uuid::new_v4();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO cost_centers(id,name,code) VALUES($1,'Existing','existing')\")\n        .bind(cost_center)\n        .execute(&pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    let (status, rule) = call(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.admin,\n        \"POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/alerts/rules\",\n        json!({\"name\":\"Existing\",\"kind\":\"batch_failed\",\"notify_platform_admins\":true}),\n    )\n    .await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let rule_id = id(&rule);\n    let (status, rule) = call(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.owner,\n        \"POST\",\n        &format!(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/alerts/rules\"),\n        json!({\"name\":\"Existing\",\"kind\":\"batch_failed\",\"notify_workspace_admins\":true}),\n    )\n    .await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let workspace_rule = id(&rule);\n    let (status, schedule) = call(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.admin,\n        \"POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules\",\n        json!({\"cadence\":\"weekly\",\"timezone\":\"UTC\",\"delivery_time\":\"09:00\",\"state\":\"paused\",\"recipient_user_ids\":[f.admin.user_id]}),\n    )\n    .await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let platform_schedule = id(&schedule);\n    let (status, schedule) = call(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.owner,\n        \"POST\",\n        &format!(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules\"),\n        json!({\"cadence\":\"weekly\",\"timezone\":\"UTC\",\"delivery_time\":\"09:00\",\"state\":\"paused\",\"recipient_user_ids\":[f.owner.user_id]}),\n    )\n    .await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let workspace_schedule = id(&schedule);\n    let policy = json!({\"requests_per_minute\":null,\"tokens_per_minute\":null,\"concurrent_requests\":null,\"budgets\":[]});\n    let price = json!({\"input_microusd_per_million\":\"1000000\",\"output_microusd_per_million\":\"1000000\",\"input_token_limit\":100,\"output_token_limit\":100});\n    let platform = \"/api/v1/platform\";\n    let workspace = format!(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}\");\n    struct Case<'a> {\n        handler: &'static str,\n        method: &'static str,\n        path: String,\n        actor: &'a BrowserPrincipal,\n        body: Value,\n    }\n    let mut cases = Vec::new();\n    macro_rules! case {\n        ($handler:literal,$method:literal,$path:expr,$actor:expr,$body:expr) => {\n            cases.push(Case {\n                handler: $handler,\n                method: $method,\n                path: $path.into(),\n                actor: $actor,\n                body: $body,\n            });\n        };\n    }\n    sqlx::query(\"UPDATE workspaces SET discoverable=true WHERE id=$1\")\n        .bind(f.team)\n        .execute(&pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    case!(\n        \"access_requests::create\",\n        \"POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/me/access-requests\",\n        &f.outsider,\n        json!({\"workspace_id\":f.team,\"idempotency_key\":\"configuration-matrix\",\"reason\":\"Join team\"})\n    );\n    case!(\n        \"access_requests::set_discoverable\",\n        \"PUT\",\n        format!(\"{platform}/workspaces/{ws}/discoverable\"),\n        &f.admin,\n        json!({\"discoverable\":true})\n    );\n    case!(\n        \"resources::create_provider\",\n        \"POST\",\n        format!(\"{platform}/providers\"),\n        &f.admin,\n        json!({\"name\":\"New connection\",\"provider\":\"openai\",\"credential_ref\":\"env:OPENAI_API_KEY\",\"enabled\":false})\n    );\n\n[508 more lines in file. Use offset=241 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":1,"timestamp":1793209897042},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:37.044Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

Test-only management contract: no runtime endpoint or production dependency.\nmod generator;\nmod validation;\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::{generator as g, validation as v};\n    use serde_json::{Value, json};\n\n    fn generated() -> Value {\n        g::generate(&g::load_fragments().unwrap()).unwrap()\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_artifact_matches_regeneration() {\n        let document = generated();\n        g::verify(&document).unwrap();\n        v::validate_document(&document).unwrap();\n        let bytes = g::encode(&document);\n        if std::env::var_os(\"OMG_REGENERATE_OPENAPI\").is_some() {\n            assert_eq!(std::env::var(\"OMG_REGENERATE_OPENAPI\").unwrap(), \"1\");\n            assert!(\n                std::env::var_os(\"CI\").is_none(),\n                \"regeneration forbidden in CI\"\n            );\n            std::fs::write(g::artifact_path(), &bytes).unwrap();\n        }\n        assert_eq!(\n            std::fs::read(g::artifact_path()).unwrap(),\n            bytes,\n            \"management OpenAPI drift: update reviewed fragments, then explicitly regenerate\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_reports_incomplete_schemas() {\n        let document = generated();\n        let operations = g::operation_count(&document);\n        let placeholders = g::placeholder_count(&document);\n        eprintln!(\n            \"management OpenAPI: {operations} operations; {placeholders} incomplete request/response schemas remaining (#81)\"\n        );\n        // Intentionally not acceptance of #81: later slices must reduce this count to zero.\n        assert_eq!(\n            operations, 251,\n            \"review route additions and update this progress baseline\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            placeholders, 502,\n            \"review schema completion and update this progress baseline\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_rejects_operation_and_authority_drift() {\n        let good = generated();\n        let path = \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}\";\n        for mutation in [\n            \"omitted\",\n            \"extra\",\n            \"method\",\n            \"template\",\n            \"access\",\n            \"scope\",\n            \"token\",\n            \"security\",\n            \"parameter\",\n        ] {\n            let mut bad = good.clone();\n            match mutation {\n                \"omitted\" => {\n                    bad[\"paths\"][path].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove(\"get\");\n                }\n                \"extra\" => {\n                    bad[\"paths\"][\"/api/v1/not-declared\"] =\n                        json!({\"get\":good[\"paths\"][path][\"get\"]});\n                }\n                \"method\" => {\n                    let op = bad[\"paths\"][path]\n                        .as_object_mut()\n                        .unwrap()\n                        .remove(\"get\")\n                        .unwrap();\n                    bad[\"paths\"][path][\"post\"] = op;\n                }\n                \"template\" => {\n                    let op = bad[\"paths\"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove(path).unwrap();\n                    bad[\"paths\"][\"/api/v1/workspaces/{workspace}\"] = op;\n                }\n                \"access\" => {\n                    bad[\"paths\"][path][\"get\"][\"x-omg-access\"] = json!(\"Anonymous\");\n                }\n                \"scope\" => {\n                    bad[\"paths\"][path][\"get\"][\"x-omg-required-scope\"] = json!(\"settings:write\");\n                }\n                \"token\" => {\n                    bad[\"paths\"][path][\"get\"][\"x-omg-token-policy\"] = json!(\"Denied\");\n                }\n                \"security\" => {\n                    bad[\"paths\"][path][\"get\"][\"security\"] = json!([]);\n                }\n                \"parameter\" => {\n                    bad[\"paths\"][path][\"get\"][\"parameters\"] = json!([]);\n                }\n                _ => unreachable!(),\n            }\n            assert!(g::verify(&bad).is_err(), \"accepted {mutation} drift\");\n        }\n        g::verify(&good).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_requires_matching_reviewed_fragments() {\n        let good = g::load_fragments().unwrap();\n        let key = \"GET /api/v1/workspaces/{ws}\";\n        for mutation in [\"missing\", \"extra\", \"access\", \"scope\", \"override\"] {\n            let mut bad = good.clone();\n            match mutation {\n                \"missing\" => {\n                    bad.operations.remove(key);\n                }\n                \"extra\" => {\n                    bad.operations\n                        .insert(\"GET /api/v1/unknown\".into(), bad.operations[key].clone());\n                }\n                \"access\" => {\n                    bad.operations.get_mut(key).unwrap().access = \"Anonymous\".into();\n                }\n                \"scope\" => {\n                    bad.operations.get_mut(key).unwrap().scope = Some(\"settings:write\".into());\n                }\n                \"override\" => {\n                    bad.operations\n                        .get_mut(key)\n                        .unwrap()\n                        .operation\n                        .insert(\"security\".into(), json!([]));\n                }\n                _ => unreachable!(),\n            }\n            assert!(g::generate(&bad).is_err(), \"accepted {mutation} fragment\");\n        }\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&good).unwrap();\n        std::fs::write(dir.path().join(\"a.json\"), &bytes).unwrap();\n        std::fs::write(dir.path().join(\"b.json\"), &bytes).unwrap();\n        assert!(\n            g::load_fragments_from(dir.path()).is_err(),\n            \"duplicate operation fragments\"\n        );\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let entry = serde_json::to_string(&good.operations[key]).unwrap();\n        let duplicate = format!(\"{{\\\"operations\\\":{{\\\"{key}\\\":{entry},\\\"{key}\\\":{entry}}}}}\");\n        std::fs::write(dir.path().join(\"duplicate.json\"), duplicate).unwrap();\n        assert!(\n            g::load_fragments_from(dir.path()).is_err(),\n            \"duplicate key in one fragment file\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_rejects_unresolved_and_external_references() {\n        let good = generated();\n        for reference in [\n            \"#/components/schemas/Missing\",\n            \"https://example.invalid/schema\",\n            \"file:///etc/passwd\",\n        ] {\n            assert!(\n                v::compile_schema(&json!({\"$ref\":reference})).is_err(),\n                \"retriever accepted unresolved or external reference {reference}\"\n            );\n            let mut bad = good.clone();\n            bad[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][\"Probe\"] = json!({\"$ref\":reference});\n            assert!(\n                v::validate_document(&bad).is_err(),\n                \"accepted ref {reference}\"\n            );\n        }\n        v::validate_document(&good).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_rejects_response_links() {\n        let good = generated();\n        let (path, item) = good[\"paths\"].as_object().unwrap().iter().next().unwrap();\n        let (method, operation) = item.as_object().unwrap().iter().next().unwrap();\n        let status = operation[\"responses\"]\n            .as_object()\n            .unwrap()\n            .keys()\n            .next()\n            .unwrap()\n            .clone();\n        let mut bad = good.clone();\n        bad[\"paths\"][path][method][\"responses\"][&status][\"links\"] =\n            json!({\"Next\":{\"operationId\":\"doesNotExist\"}});\n        assert!(\n            v::validate_document(&bad).is_err(),\n            \"accepted a dangling link\"\n        );\n        let mut components = good.clone();\n        components[\"components\"][\"links\"] = json!({\"Next\":{\"operationId\":\"doesNotExist\"}});\n        assert!(v::validate_document(&components).is_err());\n        v::validate_document(&good).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_rejects_undocumented_reasons() {\n        let mut bad = generated();\n        bad[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][\"ErrorEnvelope\"][\"properties\"][\"error\"][\"properties\"][\"reason\"][\"enum\"]\n            .as_array_mut().unwrap().pop();\n        assert!(g::verify(&bad).is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn management_openapi_shared_error_serialization() {\n        use crate::management::{ApiError, POLICY_REASONS, REASONS};\n        use axum::{http::StatusCode, response::IntoResponse};\n\n        let document = generated();\n        let validator =\n            v::compile_schema(&document[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][\"ErrorEnvelope\"]).unwrap();\n        for (message, reason) in REASONS\n            .iter()\n            .map(|(m, r)| (*m, *r))\n            .chain(POLICY_REASONS.iter().map(|(m, r, _, _)| (*m, *r)))\n        {\n            let response = ApiError(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, message).into_response();\n            assert_eq!(response.status(), StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);\n            let bytes = axum::body::to_bytes(response.into_body(), 16_384)\n                .await\n                .unwrap();\n            let body: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap();\n            assert_eq!(body[\"error\"][\"code\"], \"400\");\n            assert_eq!(body[\"error\"][\"reason\"], reason);\n            validator.validate(&body).unwrap();\n        }\n        let response = ApiError(StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, \"Not found\").into_response();\n        let bytes = axum::body::to_bytes(response.into_body(), 16_384)\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let body: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap();\n        assert!(body[\"error\"].get(\"reason\").is_none());\n        validator.validate(&body).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_metaschema_and_schema_controls() {\n        let good = generated();\n        v::validate_document(&good).unwrap();\n        for mutation in [\"openapi\", \"responses\", \"schema\"] {\n            let mut bad = good.clone();\n            match mutation {\n                \"openapi\" => bad[\"openapi\"] = json!(\"3.0.3\"),\n                \"responses\" => bad[\"paths\"][\"/api/v1/me\"][\"get\"][\"responses\"] = json!(false),\n                \"schema\" => {\n                    bad[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][\"ErrorEnvelope\"][\"type\"] = json!(\"not-a-type\")\n                }\n                _ => unreachable!(),\n            }\n            assert!(\n                v::validate_document(&bad).is_err(),\n                \"accepted {mutation} corruption\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_regex_errors_fail_closed() {\n        // The production gate uses a linear-time backend: unsupported look-around,\n        // invalid regexes and excessive compilation complexity are errors, never non-matches.\n        for schema in [\n            json!({\"pattern\":\"[\"}),\n            json!({\"patternProperties\":{\"[\":false}}),\n            json!({\"not\":{\"pattern\":\"(?=a)a\"}}),\n            json!({\"patternProperties\":{\"(?=a)a\":false}}),\n        ] {\n            assert!(\n                v::compile_schema(&schema).is_err(),\n                \"accepted unexecutable regex {schema}\"\n            );\n            let mut fragments = g::load_fragments().unwrap();\n            fragments.schemas.insert(\"InvalidRegex\".into(), schema);\n            assert!(\n                g::generate(&fragments)\n                    .and_then(|doc| v::validate_document(&doc))\n                    .is_err()\n            );\n        }\n        let validator = v::compile_schema(&json!({\"patternProperties\":{\"^a\":false}})).unwrap();\n        assert!(validator.validate(&json!({\"b\":1})).is_ok());\n        assert!(validator.validate(&json!({\"a\":1})).is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_security_alternatives_are_exact() {\n        let doc = generated();\n        assert_eq!(\n            doc[\"paths\"][\"/api/v1/auth/config\"][\"get\"][\"security\"],\n            json!([])\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            doc[\"paths\"][\"/api/v1/auth/logout\"][\"post\"][\"security\"],\n            json!([{\"browserSession\":[],\"csrfToken\":[],\"origin\":[]} ])\n        );\n        let admin = &doc[\"paths\"][\"/api/v1/platform/admin-tokens\"];\n        assert_eq!(admin[\"get\"][\"x-omg-token-policy\"], \"BrowserAdminOnly\");\n        assert_eq!(admin[\"get\"][\"security\"], json!([{\"browserSession\":[]} ]));\n        let scoped = &doc[\"paths\"][\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}\"][\"get\"];\n        assert_eq!(\n            scoped[\"security\"],\n            json!([{\"browserSession\":[]},{\"adminToken\":[]} ])\n        );\n        assert_eq!(scoped[\"x-omg-required-scope\"], \"platform:read\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn management_openapi_vendor_checksums_are_enforced() {\n        let mut files = v::vendor_files().unwrap();\n        v::verify_vendor(&files).unwrap();\n        files.values_mut().next().unwrap().push(b' ');\n        assert!(v::verify_vendor(&files).is_err());\n    }\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":0,"timestamp":1793209900162},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:40.163Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_f653bebd99064d6ba56c609ef8848bda|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2362c20e0819d89b220dd689cdc69","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"use std::{\n    collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet},\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n};\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result, ensure};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse serde_json::{Map, Value, json};\nuse syn::visit::Visit;\n\nuse crate::route_policy::{Access, ROUTES, TokenPolicy};\n\n#[derive(Clone, Deserialize, Serialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\npub(super) struct Fragment {\n    pub access: String,\n    pub token_policy: String,\n    pub scope: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub operation: Map<String, Value>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Default, Deserialize, Serialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\npub(super) struct Fragments {\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"unique_map\")]\n    pub operations: BTreeMap<String, Fragment>,\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"unique_map\")]\n    pub schemas: BTreeMap<String, Value>,\n}\n\n// serde's default map decoding silently accepts duplicate keys. Reviewed fragments must not.\nfn unique_map<'de, D, T>(deserializer: D) -> std::result::Result<BTreeMap<String, T>, D::Error>\nwhere\n    D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,\n    T: Deserialize<'de>,\n{\n    struct Unique<T>(std::marker::PhantomData<T>);\n    impl<'de, T: Deserialize<'de>> serde::de::Visitor<'de> for Unique<T> {\n        type Value = BTreeMap<String, T>;\n        fn expecting(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {\n            f.write_str(\"an object without duplicate fragment names\")\n        }\n        fn visit_map<A: serde::de::MapAccess<'de>>(\n            self,\n            mut map: A,\n        ) -> std::result::Result<Self::Value, A::Error> {\n            let mut result = BTreeMap::new();\n            while let Some((key, value)) = map.next_entry::<String, T>()? {\n                if result.insert(key.clone(), value).is_some() {\n                    return Err(serde::de::Error::custom(format!(\n                        \"duplicate fragment {key}\"\n                    )));\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(result)\n        }\n    }\n    deserializer.deserialize_map(Unique(std::marker::PhantomData))\n}\n\npub(super) fn root() -> PathBuf {\n    Path::new(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\")).join(\"../..\")\n}\npub(super) fn artifact_path() -> PathBuf {\n    root().join(\"docs/openapi/management.openapi.json\")\n}\npub(super) fn load_fragments() -> Result<Fragments> {\n    load_fragments_from(&root().join(\"docs/openapi/management\"))\n}\npub(super) fn load_fragments_from(directory: &Path) -> Result<Fragments> {\n    let mut paths = std::fs::read_dir(directory)?\n        .map(|entry| entry.map(|e| e.path()))\n        .collect::<std::io::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;\n    paths.sort();\n    let mut result = Fragments::default();\n    for path in paths {\n        if path.extension().is_none_or(|ext| ext != \"json\") {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let fragment: Fragments = serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(&path)?)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"fragment {}\", path.display()))?;\n        for (key, value) in fragment.operations {\n            ensure!(\n                result.operations.insert(key.clone(), value).is_none(),\n                \"duplicate operation fragment {key}\"\n            );\n        }\n        for (key, value) in fragment.schemas {\n            ensure!(\n                result.schemas.insert(key.clone(), value).is_none(),\n                \"duplicate schema fragment {key}\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(result)\n}\n\nstruct Declaration {\n    method: String,\n    path: &'static str,\n    access: &'static str,\n    token_policy: &'static str,\n    scope: Option<&'static str>,\n    security: Value,\n}\nfn access_name(access: Access) -> &'static str {\n    match access {\n        Access::Anonymous => \"Anonymous\",\n        Access::Health => \"Health\",\n        Access::InferenceKey => \"InferenceKey\",\n        Access::Scim => \"Scim\",\n        Access::Browser => \"Browser\",\n        Access::BrowserLogout => \"BrowserLogout\",\n        Access::PlatformRead => \"PlatformRead\",\n        Access::PlatformWrite => \"PlatformWrite\",\n        Access::WorkspaceMetadata => \"WorkspaceMetadata\",\n        Access::WorkspaceMember => \"WorkspaceMember\",\n        Access::WorkspaceKey => \"WorkspaceKey\",\n        Access::WorkspaceKeyRotate => \"WorkspaceKeyRotate\",\n        Access::WorkspaceAdmin => \"WorkspaceAdmin\",\n        Access::WorkspaceReportAdmin => \"WorkspaceReportAdmin\",\n        Access::WorkspaceAdminOrReader => \"WorkspaceAdminOrReader\",\n        Access::WorkspacePlatformWrite => \"WorkspacePlatformWrite\",\n    }\n}\nfn inventory() -> Result<BTreeMap<String, Declaration>> {\n    let mut inventory = BTreeMap::new();\n    for route in ROUTES\n        .iter()\n        .filter(|route| route.path.starts_with(\"/api/v1/\"))\n    {\n        for method in route.methods.split_ascii_whitespace() {\n            ensure!(\n                [\n                    \"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\", \"OPTIONS\", \"HEAD\", \"TRACE\"\n                ]\n                .contains(&method),\n                \"unsupported management method {method}\"\n            );\n            let (token_policy, scope) = match route.token_policy {\n                TokenPolicy::Denied => (\"Denied\", None),\n                TokenPolicy::BrowserAdminOnly => (\"BrowserAdminOnly\", None),\n                TokenPolicy::Scope(scope) => (\"Scope\", Some(scope.as_str())),\n            };\n            let mut security = Vec::new();\n            if route.access != Access::Anonymous {\n                let browser = if [\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"].contains(&method) {\n                    json!({\"browserSession\":[]})\n                } else {\n                    json!({\"browserSession\":[],\"csrfToken\":[],\"origin\":[]})\n                };\n                security.push(browser);\n                if scope.is_some() {\n                    security.push(json!({\"adminToken\":[]}));\n                }\n            } else {\n                ensure!(\n                    scope.is_none(),\n                    \"anonymous operation unexpectedly admits admin tokens\"\n                );\n            }\n            let declaration = Declaration {\n                method: method.to_ascii_lowercase(),\n                path: route.path,\n                access: access_name(route.access),\n                token_policy,\n                scope,\n                security: json!(security),\n            };\n            let key = format!(\"{method} {}\", route.path);\n            ensure!(\n                inventory.insert(key.clone(), declaration).is_none(),\n                \"duplicate route {key}\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(inventory)\n}\nfn operation_id(declaration: &Declaration) -> String {\n    let mut id = declaration.method.clone();\n    for segment in declaration.path.split('/').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) {\n        id.push('_');\n        if segment.starts_with('{') {\n            id.push_str(\"by_\");\n        }\n        id.push_str(&segment.trim_matches(['{', '}']).replace('-', \"_\"));\n    }\n    id\n}\nfn path_parameters(path: &str) -> Vec<Value> {\n    path.split('/')\n        .filter_map(|segment| segment.strip_prefix('{')?.strip_suffix('}'))\n        .map(|name| json!({\"name\":name,\"in\":\"path\",\"required\":true,\"schema\":{\"type\":\"string\"}}))\n        .collect()\n}\nfn placeholder(description: &str) -> Value {\n    json!({\"description\":description,\"x-omg-schema-status\":\"incomplete\"})\n}\n\npub(super) fn generate(fragments: &Fragments) -> Result<Value> {\n    let inventory = inventory()?;\n    ensure!(\n        inventory.keys().eq(fragments.operations.keys()),\n        \"missing or unmatched operation fragments (exact METHOD template required)\"\n    );\n    let mut paths = Map::new();\n    let mut schemas = Map::new();\n    let mut ids = BTreeSet::new();\n    for (key, declaration) in &inventory {\n        let fragment = &fragments.operations[key];\n        ensure!(\n            fragment.access == declaration.access\n                && fragment.token_policy == declaration.token_policy\n                && fragment.scope.as_deref() == declaration.scope,\n            \"authority fragment drift for {key}\"\n        );\n        let id = operation_id(declaration);\n        ensure!(ids.insert(id.clone()), \"duplicate operationId {id}\");\n        let request_name = format!(\"{id}Request\");\n        let response_name = format!(\"{id}Response\");\n        schemas.insert(request_name.clone(), placeholder(\"Request body presence, media types and schema have not been inventoried. This is not a promise that a body is accepted.\"));\n        schemas.insert(response_name.clone(), placeholder(\"Response statuses, headers, media types and body schemas have not been inventoried. This is not a promise of a JSON response.\"));\n        let mut operation = json!({\n            \"operationId\":id,\n            \"x-omg-access\":declaration.access,\n            \"x-omg-token-policy\":declaration.token_policy,\n            \"security\":declaration.security,\n            \"parameters\":path_parameters(declaration.path),\n            \"x-omg-request-schema\":{\"$ref\":format!(\"#/components/schemas/{request_name}\")},\n            \"x-omg-response-schema\":{\"$ref\":format!(\"#/components/schemas/{response_name}\")},\n            \"responses\":{\"default\":{\"description\":\"Incomplete response contract; see x-omg-response-schema. Framework extraction errors can differ from the shared ErrorEnvelope.\"}},\n        });\n        if let Some(scope) = declaration.scope {\n            operation[\"x-omg-required-scope\"] = json!(scope);\n        }\n        for (field, value) in &fragment.operation {\n            ensure!(\n                ![\n                    \"operationId\",\n                    \"security\",\n                    \"x-omg-access\",\n                    \"x-omg-token-policy\",\n                    \"x-omg-required-scope\",\n                    \"x-omg-request-schema\",\n                    \"x-omg-response-schema\"\n                ]\n                .contains(&field.as_str()),\n                \"fragment overrides generated {field} for {key}\"\n            );\n            if field == \"parameters\" {\n                let parameters = value\n                    .as_array()\n                    .context(\"fragment parameters must be an array\")?;\n                ensure!(\n                    parameters\n                        .iter()\n                        .all(|p| p[\"in\"] != \"path\" && p.get(\"$ref\").is_none()),\n                    \"path parameters are generated; fragments may add only inline non-path parameters\"\n                );\n                operation[\"parameters\"]\n                    .as_array_mut()\n                    .unwrap()\n                    .extend(parameters.iter().cloned());\n            } else {\n                operation[field] = value.clone();\n            }\n        }\n        paths.entry(declaration.path).or_insert_with(|| json!({}))[&declaration.method] = operation;\n    }\n    schemas.insert(\"ErrorEnvelope\".into(), error_schema()?);\n    for (name, schema) in &fragments.schemas {\n        ensure!(\n            name != \"ErrorEnvelope\",\n            \"shared error vocabulary is generated\"\n        );\n        schemas.insert(name.clone(), schema.clone());\n    }\n    Ok(json!({\n        \"openapi\":\"3.1.0\",\n        \"jsonSchemaDialect\":\"https://spec.openapis.org/oas/3.1/dialect/base\",\n        \"info\":{\"title\":\"Open Model Gateway management API\",\"version\":env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\"),\n            \"description\":\"INCOMPLETE contract skeleton (#81 PR1). Access extensions are minimum boundaries, not complete authority. Live owner, role, membership, personal-workspace and object-visibility checks remain authoritative. Personal details are owner-only; platform totals do not grant personal detail access. Shared members see their own activity unless an administrator or the object's visibility permits more; ownership checks can hide objects with 404. Auditor platform authority is read-only, independent User/membership entitlements remain usable. Credential families are exclusive, duplicate Authorization headers are refused, inference keys never authorize management, undeclared routes fail closed, and write scopes do not imply read scopes. BrowserAdminOnly includes Platform Admin even on GET. HEAD implicitly follows GET unless separately declared; no synthetic HEAD operations are listed. Request/response placeholders live in x-omg-request-schema and x-omg-response-schema until actual body presence, status and media types are reviewed; they are not permissive acceptance schemas. No universally enveloped error response is promised.\"},\n        \"paths\":paths,\n        \"components\":{\"schemas\":schemas,\"securitySchemes\":{\n            \"browserSession\":{\"type\":\"apiKey\",\"in\":\"cookie\",\"name\":\"omg_session\",\"description\":\"Browser session; live role, workspace and owner checks still apply.\"},\n            \"csrfToken\":{\"type\":\"apiKey\",\"in\":\"header\",\"name\":\"X-CSRF-Token\",\"description\":\"Conjunctive with session and Origin on browser mutations.\"},\n            \"origin\":{\"type\":\"apiKey\",\"in\":\"header\",\"name\":\"Origin\",\"description\":\"Must exactly equal the server-configured Origin; conjunctive with session and CSRF on browser mutations.\"},\n            \"adminToken\":{\"type\":\"http\",\"scheme\":\"bearer\",\"description\":\"Admin API token, never an inference key. Only Scope operations admit this alternative; x-omg-required-scope is exact, not an OAuth scope. Live token and owner authority is rechecked.\"}\n        }}\n    }))\n}\n\npub(super) fn verify(document: &Value) -> Result<()> {\n    let inventory = inventory()?;\n    let paths = document[\"paths\"]\n        .as_object()\n        .context(\"paths object missing\")?;\n    let mut actual = BTreeSet::new();\n    let mut ids = BTreeSet::new();\n\n[263 more lines in file. Use offset=301 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":0,"timestamp":1793209900162},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:40.163Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

\"ux_tests.rs\"]\nmod ux_tests;\n#[path = \"alerts/webhook_tests.rs\"]\nmod webhook_tests;\n\nstruct Fixture {\n    s: Store,\n    admin: BrowserPrincipal,\n    auditor: BrowserPrincipal,\n    owner: BrowserPrincipal,\n    member: BrowserPrincipal,\n    outsider: BrowserPrincipal,\n    team: Uuid,\n    project: Uuid,\n    personal: Uuid,\n}\nasync fn user(pool: &PgPool, name: &str, role: &str) -> BrowserPrincipal {\n    let id = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let email = format!(\"{name}@example.test\");\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO users(id,email) VALUES($1,$2)\")\n        .bind(id)\n        .bind(&email)\n        .execute(pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO platform_role_grants(user_id,role,source) VALUES($1,$2,'manual')\")\n        .bind(id)\n        .bind(role)\n        .execute(pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    BrowserPrincipal {\n        user_id: id,\n        email,\n        platform_admin: role == \"admin\",\n        platform_auditor: matches!(role, \"admin\" | \"auditor\"),\n    }\n}\nasync fn workspace(pool: &PgPool, name: &str, kind: &str, owner: Uuid) -> Uuid {\n    let id = Uuid::new_v4();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO workspaces(id,name,kind,owner_user_id) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4)\")\n        .bind(id)\n        .bind(name)\n        .bind(kind)\n        .bind(if kind == \"personal\" {\n            Some(owner)\n        } else {\n            None\n        })\n        .execute(pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    member(pool, id, owner, \"owner\").await;\n    id\n}\nasync fn member(pool: &PgPool, ws: Uuid, user: Uuid, role: &str) {\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO workspace_membership_grants(workspace_id,user_id,role,source) VALUES($1,$2,$3,'manual')\").bind(ws).bind(user).bind(role).execute(pool).await.unwrap();\n}\nasync fn fixture(pool: &PgPool) -> Fixture {\n    let admin = user(pool, \"admin\", \"admin\").await;\n    let auditor = user(pool, \"auditor\", \"auditor\").await;\n    let owner = user(pool, \"owner\", \"user\").await;\n    let member_user = user(pool, \"member\", \"user\").await;\n    let outsider = user(pool, \"outsider\", \"user\").await;\n    let team = workspace(pool, \"Team\", \"team\", owner.user_id).await;\n    let project = workspace(pool, \"Project\", \"project\", owner.user_id).await;\n    let personal = workspace(pool, \"Personal\", \"personal\", owner.user_id).await;\n    member(pool, team, member_user.user_id, \"member\").await;\n    Fixture {\n        s: Store::new(pool.clone()),\n        admin,\n        auditor,\n        owner,\n        member: member_user,\n        outsider,\n        team,\n        project,\n        personal,\n    }\n}\nasync fn call(\n    s: &Store,\n    u: &BrowserPrincipal,\n    method: &str,\n    path: &str,\n    body: Value,\n) -> (StatusCode, Value) {\n    let response = routes()\n        .merge(super::provider_costs::upload_routes())\n        // Both extensions: a real browser session's middleware inserts both\n        // (identity::authenticate_management), and this bypasses that\n        // middleware entirely to call a handler directly.\n        .layer(Extension(ManagementPrincipal::Browser(u.clone())))\n        .layer(Extension(u.clone()))\n        .with_state(s.clone())\n        .oneshot(\n            Request::builder()\n                .method(method)\n                .uri(path)\n                .header(\"content-type\", \"application/json\")\n                .body(Body::from(body.to_string()))\n                .unwrap(),\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    let status = response.status();\n    let bytes = to_bytes(response.into_body(), 1024 * 1024).await.unwrap();\n    (\n        status,\n        serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap_or(Value::Null),\n    )\n}\n#[sqlx::test(migrations = \"./enterprise_migrations\")]\nasync fn me_exposes_inference_base_url_to_regular_users(pool: PgPool) {\n    let f = fixture(&pool).await;\n    let (status, unset) = call(&f.s, &f.member, \"GET\", \"/api/v1/me\", Value::Null).await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    assert_eq!(\n        unset[\"installation\"].get(\"inference_base_url\"),\n        Some(&Value::Null)\n    );\n    let response = routes()\n        .layer(Extension(f.member))\n        .layer(Extension(\n            crate::config::InferenceBaseUrl::parse(\n                Some(\"https://api.bitop.dev/v1/\"),\n                crate::config::Environment::Production,\n            )\n            .unwrap(),\n        ))\n        .with_state(f.s)\n        .oneshot(\n            Request::builder()\n                .uri(\"/api/v1/me\")\n                .body(Body::empty())\n                .unwrap(),\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(response.status(), StatusCode::OK);\n    let body: Value =\n        serde_json::from_slice(&to_bytes(response.into_body(), 1024 * 1024).await.unwrap())\n            .unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(\n        body[\"installation\"][\"inference_base_url\"],\n        \"https://api.bitop.dev/v1\"\n    );\n}\n\nfn id(v: &Value) -> Uuid {\n    Uuid::parse_str(v[\"id\"].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap()\n}\nasync fn model(pool: &PgPool, name: &str) -> Uuid {\n    let id = Uuid::new_v4();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO models(id,public_name) VALUES($1,$2)\")\n        .bind(id)\n        .bind(name)\n        .execute(pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    id\n}\nasync fn direct(pool: &PgPool, ws: Uuid, model: Uuid) {\n    sqlx::query(\n        \"INSERT INTO workspace_model_grants(workspace_id,model_id,source) VALUES($1,$2,'direct')\",\n    )\n    .bind(ws)\n    .bind(model)\n    .execute(pool)\n    .await\n    .unwrap();\n}\nasync fn key(f: &Fixture, u: &BrowserPrincipal, ws: Uuid, models: Value) -> (StatusCode, Value) {\n    call(\n\n[211 {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_c522cef738ba4ffdb43f69f2ac18f244|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2362c20e0819da69bfeae194278fc","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Exercise the served router, including live cookies/entitlement/CSRF, not injected principals.\nuse super::*;\nuse crate::{\n    identity::tests::MockProvider,\n    route_policy::{Access, ROUTES},\n};\nuse sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\n\n#[path = \"authz_admin_token_tests.rs\"]\nmod admin_token_tests;\n#[path = \"authz_middleware_tests.rs\"]\nmod isolated;\n#[path = \"key_issuance_contention_tests.rs\"]\nmod key_issuance_contention_tests;\n#[path = \"provider_cost_auth_tests.rs\"]\nmod provider_cost_auth_tests;\n#[path = \"authz_matrix_fixtures.rs\"]\nmod targets;\nconst ORIGIN: &str = \"http://127.0.0.1:3000\";\nstruct Session {\n    cookie: String,\n    csrf: String,\n}\nasync fn session(pool: &PgPool, u: &BrowserPrincipal) -> Session {\n    let cookie = crate::entropy::hex_token().unwrap();\n    let csrf = crate::entropy::hex_token().unwrap();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO browser_sessions(token_hash,user_id,csrf_hash,expires_at,verified_email,last_seen_at) VALUES($1,$2,$3,now()+interval '1 hour',$4,now())\")\n        .bind(Sha256::digest(cookie.as_bytes()).as_slice()).bind(u.user_id)\n        .bind(Sha256::digest(csrf.as_bytes()).as_slice()).bind(&u.email)\n        .execute(pool).await.unwrap();\n    Session { cookie, csrf }\n}\nasync fn request(\n    app: &Router,\n    method: &str,\n    path: &str,\n    session: Option<&Session>,\n    bearer: Option<&str>,\n    csrf: bool,\n    origin: &str,\n) -> StatusCode {\n    let mut req = Request::builder()\n        .method(method)\n        .uri(path)\n        .header(\"content-type\", \"application/json\")\n        .header(\"origin\", origin);\n    if let Some(s) = session {\n        req = req.header(\"cookie\", format!(\"omg_session={}\", s.cookie));\n        if csrf {\n            req = req.header(\"x-csrf-token\", &s.csrf);\n        }\n    }\n    if let Some(b) = bearer {\n        req = req.header(\"authorization\", format!(\"Bearer {b}\"));\n    }\n    app.clone()\n        .oneshot(req.body(Body::from(\"{}\")).unwrap())\n        .await\n        .unwrap()\n        .status()\n}\n\n/// Every declared workspace route must hide a foreign personal workspace just\n/// like an unknown UUID, before handler extraction (including mutations).\n#[sqlx::test(migrations = \"./enterprise_migrations\")]\nasync fn every_workspace_route_hides_foreign_personal_like_unknown(pool: PgPool) {\n    let f = fixture(&pool).await;\n    let owner = session(&pool, &f.owner).await;\n    let (status, key_value) = key(&f, &f.owner, f.team, Value::Null).await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    let targets = targets::Targets::new(&pool, &f, id(&key_value), &owner).await;\n    let mock = MockProvider::start(None).await;\n    let identity = mock.state(pool.clone()).await;\n    let engine = crate::inference::Engine::new(\n        std::sync::Arc::new(f.s.clone()),\n        crate::providers::ProviderRegistry::default(),\n        crate::inference::EngineLimits::default(),\n    )\n    .unwrap();\n    let app = crate::http::router_with_identity(f.s.clone(), None, engine, identity);\n    let unknown = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let sessions = [\n        (session(&pool, &f.outsider).await, \"user\"),\n        (session(&pool, &f.auditor).await, \"auditor\"),\n        (session(&pool, &f.admin).await, \"admin\"),\n    ];\n    let mut checked = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();\n    let mut failures = Vec::new();\n    for route in ROUTES.iter().filter(|r| {\n        r.path == \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}\" || r.path.starts_with(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/\")\n    }) {\n        let concrete = targets.concrete(route.path, &f);\n        let personal_path = concrete.replace(&f.team.to_string(), &f.personal.to_string());\n        let unknown_path = concrete.replace(&f.team.to_string(), &unknown.to_string());\n        assert!(!personal_path.contains('{'), \"unexpanded {}\", route.path);\n        for method in route.methods.split_whitespace().flat_map(|m| {\n            if m == \"GET\" {\n                vec![\"GET\", \"HEAD\"]\n            } else {\n                vec![m]\n            }\n        }) {\n            for (session, label) in &sessions {\n                let mut replies = Vec::new();\n                for path in [&personal_path, &unknown_path] {\n                    let response = app\n                        .clone()\n                        .oneshot(\n                            Request::builder()\n                                .method(method)\n                                .uri(path)\n                                .header(\"content-type\", \"application/json\")\n                                .header(\"origin\", ORIGIN)\n                                .header(\"cookie\", format!(\"omg_session={}\", session.cookie))\n                                .header(\"x-csrf-token\", &session.csrf)\n                                .body(Body::from(\"{}\"))\n                                .unwrap(),\n                        )\n                        .await\n                        .unwrap();\n                    let status = response.status();\n                    let body = to_bytes(response.into_body(), 1024 * 1024).await.unwrap();\n                    replies.push((status, body));\n                }\n                // Compare the complete body, not only its keys: a distinguishing\n                // message or reason would leave the existence oracle intact.\n                // Preserve the existing role-first refusal without turning any\n                // other unknown-workspace answer into 403 to equalize statuses.\n                let expected_status =\n                    if route.access == Access::WorkspacePlatformWrite && *label != \"admin\" {\n                        StatusCode::FORBIDDEN\n                    } else {\n                        StatusCode::NOT_FOUND\n                    };\n                if replies[0] != replies[1] || replies[1].0 != expected_status {\n                    failures.push(format!(\n                        \"{method} {} {label}: personal={:?}, unknown={:?}\",\n                        route.path, replies[0], replies[1]\n                    ));\n                }\n                if method == \"HEAD\" {\n                    assert!(replies.iter().all(|(_, body)| body.is_empty()));\n                } else {\n                    for (_, body) in &replies {\n                        let error: Value = serde_json::from_slice(body).unwrap();\n                        assert!(\n                            error[\"error\"].is_object(),\n                            \"{method} {} {label}: {error}\",\n                            route.path\n                        );\n                    }\n                }\n                checked.insert((route.path, method, *label));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    let expected: usize = ROUTES\n        .iter()\n        .filter(|r| {\n            r.path == \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}\" || r.path.starts_with(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/\")\n        })\n        .map(|r| {\n            r.methods\n                .split_whitespace()\n                .map(|m| if m == \"GET\" { 2 } else { 1 })\n                .sum::<usize>()\n        })\n        .sum();\n    // Positive controls: the same real middleware still admits the personal\n    // owner for a representative read and a write, not a blanket refusal.\n    assert_eq!(\n        request(\n            &app,\n            \"GET\",\n            &format!(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{}\", f.personal),\n            Some(&owner),\n            None,\n            true,\n            ORIGIN\n        )\n        .await,\n        StatusCode::OK\n    );\n    let response = app\n        .oneshot(\n            Request::builder()\n                .method(\"POST\")\n                .uri(format!(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{}/keys\", f.personal))\n                .header(\"content-type\", \"application/json\")\n                .header(\"origin\", ORIGIN)\n                .header(\"cookie\", format!(\"omg_session={}\", owner.cookie))\n                .header(\"x-csrf-token\", &owner.csrf)\n                .body(Body::from(\n                    json!({\"name\":\"Owner control\", \"expires_in_days\":1}).to_string(),\n                ))\n                .unwrap(),\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(response.status(), StatusCode::OK);\n    assert!(expected > 0, \"workspace route inventory must not be empty\");\n    assert_eq!(checked.len(), expected * sessions.len());\n    assert!(\n        failures.is_empty(),\n        \"workspace existence oracle:\\n{}\",\n        failures.join(\"\\n\")\n    );\n}\n\n#[sqlx::test(migrations = \"./enterprise_migrations\")]\nasync fn deployment_preview_browser_only_origin_csrf_and_live_roles(pool: PgPool) {\n    let f = fixture(&pool).await;\n    let mock = MockProvider::start(None).await;\n    let identity = mock.state(pool.clone()).await;\n    let engine = crate::inference::Engine::new(\n        std::sync::Arc::new(f.s.clone()),\n        crate::providers::ProviderRegistry::default(),\n        crate::inference::EngineLimits::default(),\n    )\n    .unwrap();\n\n[714 more lines in file.
