{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_ebb6ad18b1c0402b835983595beeb2a6|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2362c20e0819db14aa56a4c2356d9","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Exact route-template/method authorization declarations.\n//! Workspace entries declare the minimum boundary; handlers retain object ownership,\n//! activity filtering, personal/shared-kind constraints and transactional live rechecks.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub(crate) enum Access {\n    Anonymous,\n    Health,\n    InferenceKey,\n    Scim,\n    Browser,\n    BrowserLogout,\n    PlatformRead,\n    PlatformWrite,\n    WorkspaceMetadata,\n    WorkspaceMember,\n    WorkspaceKey,\n    WorkspaceKeyRotate,\n    WorkspaceAdmin,\n    /// Actual admin membership; foreign personal scopes are missing (#102/#282).\n    WorkspaceReportAdmin,\n    WorkspaceAdminOrReader,\n    WorkspacePlatformWrite,\n}\n/// The five admin-token scopes (#37 PR2), one per [`crate::admin_tokens::SCOPES`]\n/// entry. A dedicated enum (rather than reusing the scope strings directly in\n/// every route declaration) makes a typo a compile error instead of a silent\n/// mismatch, and keeps this module the single source of truth for the\n/// string spelling (`TokenScope::as_str`).\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub(crate) enum TokenScope {\n    PlatformRead,\n    CatalogWrite,\n    AccessWrite,\n    GovernanceWrite,\n    SettingsWrite,\n}\nimpl TokenScope {\n    #[cfg(test)]\n    pub(crate) const ALL: [Self; 5] = [\n        Self::PlatformRead,\n        Self::CatalogWrite,\n        Self::AccessWrite,\n        Self::GovernanceWrite,\n        Self::SettingsWrite,\n    ];\n    pub(crate) const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            Self::PlatformRead => \"platform:read\",\n            Self::CatalogWrite => \"catalog:write\",\n            Self::AccessWrite => \"access:write\",\n            Self::GovernanceWrite => \"governance:write\",\n            Self::SettingsWrite => \"settings:write\",\n        }\n    }\n}\n/// What an admin API token may do with a route, independent of (and applied\n/// in addition to) the browser-session [`Access`] boundary above. Every\n/// route declares exactly one: there is no wildcard or class-wide default,\n/// so an addition without an explicit decision fails to compile (the\n/// `route!` macro requires a fourth argument) and a route missing from\n/// [`ROUTES`] entirely denies tokens by construction ([`token_policy`]\n/// returns `None`, mapped to a refusal by the caller).\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub(crate) enum TokenPolicy {\n    /// No admin token may ever call this route, regardless of scope\n    /// (workspace-member/key/inference/SCIM classes, and every browser-only\n    /// dashboard route).\n    Denied,\n    /// Browser session only, and only a currently effective Platform Admin\n    /// (admin-token CRUD itself: creating, listing or revoking tokens is\n    /// never delegable to a token).\n    BrowserAdminOnly,\n    /// Requires exactly this scope on the presented token, re-checked live\n    /// on every request; every other authority/object check in [`Access`]\n    /// still applies on top.\n    Scope(TokenScope),\n}\npub(crate) struct Route {\n    pub(crate) methods: &'static str,\n    pub(crate) path: &'static str,\n    pub(crate) access: Access,\n    pub(crate) token_policy: TokenPolicy,\n}\nuse Access::*;\nconst DENIED: TokenPolicy = TokenPolicy::Denied;\nconst BROWSER_ADMIN_ONLY: TokenPolicy = TokenPolicy::BrowserAdminOnly;\nconst PLATFORM_READ: TokenPolicy = TokenPolicy::Scope(TokenScope::PlatformRead);\nconst CATALOG_WRITE: TokenPolicy = TokenPolicy::Scope(TokenScope::CatalogWrite);\nconst ACCESS_WRITE: TokenPolicy = TokenPolicy::Scope(TokenScope::AccessWrite);\nconst GOVERNANCE_WRITE: TokenPolicy = TokenPolicy::Scope(TokenScope::GovernanceWrite);\nconst SETTINGS_WRITE: TokenPolicy = TokenPolicy::Scope(TokenScope::SettingsWrite);\nmacro_rules! route {\n    ($methods:literal, $path:literal, $access:ident, $token:expr) => {\n        Route {\n            methods: $methods,\n            path: $path,\n            access: $access,\n            token_policy: $token,\n        }\n    };\n}\npub(crate) const ROUTES: &[Route] = &[\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}/runs\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}/runs\",\n        WorkspaceReportAdmin,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}/csv\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}/csv\",\n        WorkspaceReportAdmin,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}\",\n        WorkspaceReportAdmin,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules\",\n        PlatformWrite,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"PATCH DELETE\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}\",\n        PlatformWrite,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules\",\n        WorkspaceReportAdmin,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET PATCH DELETE\",\n        \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}\",\n        WorkspaceReportAdmin,\n        DENIED\n    ),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/auth/config\", Anonymous, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/auth/login\", Anonymous, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/auth/callback\", Anonymous, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/api/v1/auth/logout\", BrowserLogout, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/branding/logo\", Anonymous, DENIED),\n    // Unauthenticated, optional separate listener; never mounted on the public router.\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/metrics\", Anonymous, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/health/live\", Health, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/health/ready\", Health, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/v1/models\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/chat/completions\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/responses\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    // Also /v1/messages?beta=true: queries never widen this exact POST template.\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/messages\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/embeddings\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/realtime/client_secrets\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/realtime/calls\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/images/generations\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/rerank\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/systemone\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/audio/transcriptions\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/audio/speech\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET POST\", \"/v1/videos\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET DELETE\", \"/v1/videos/{id}\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/v1/videos/{id}/content\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET POST\", \"/v1/files\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET DELETE\", \"/v1/files/{id}\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/v1/files/{id}/content\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET POST\", \"/v1/batches\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/v1/batches/{id}\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"POST\", \"/v1/batches/{id}/cancel\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/v1/realtime\", InferenceKey, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/scim/v2/ServiceProviderConfig\", Scim, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", \"/scim/v2/ResourceTypes\", Scim, DENIED),\n    route!(\"GET\", Offline validation: all trusted external identifiers resolve only to checked-in,\n//! checksum-verified bytes. Contract references themselves must be local pointers.\nuse std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result, ensure};\nuse jsonschema::{Draft, PatternOptions, Retrieve, Uri, Validator};\nuse serde::Deserialize;\nuse serde_json::{Value, json};\nuse sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\n\nuse super::generator::root;\n\nconst OPENAPI_META: &str = \"https://spec.openapis.org/oas/3.1/schema-base/2022-10-07\";\n\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\nstruct Manifest {\n    files: Vec<Vendored>,\n}\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\nstruct Vendored {\n    file: String,\n    uri: String,\n    sha256: String,\n}\nfn manifest() -> Result<Manifest> {\n    Ok(serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(\n        root().join(\"docs/openapi/vendor/manifest.json\"),\n    )?)?)\n}\npub(super) fn vendor_files() -> Result<BTreeMap<String, Vec<u8>>> {\n    let mut files = BTreeMap::new();\n    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(root().join(\"docs/openapi/vendor\"))? {\n        let path = entry?.path();\n        let name = path\n            .file_name()\n            .context(\"vendor filename missing\")?\n            .to_str()\n            .context(\"non-UTF8 vendor filename\")?;\n        if path.extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == \"json\") && name != \"manifest.json\" {\n            files.insert(name.to_owned(), std::fs::read(&path)?);\n        }\n    }\n    verify_vendor(&files)?;\n    Ok(files)\n}\npub(super) fn verify_vendor(files: &BTreeMap<String, Vec<u8>>) -> Result<()> {\n    let entries = manifest()?.files;\n    let expected = entries\n        .iter()\n        .map(|e| e.file.clone())\n        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();\n    ensure!(\n        entries.len() == expected.len(),\n        \"duplicate vendored filename\"\n    );\n    ensure!(\n        expected.iter().eq(files.keys()),\n        \"missing or extra vendored schemas\"\n    );\n    let mut uris = BTreeSet::new();\n    for entry in entries {\n        ensure!(\n            !entry.file.contains('/') && !entry.file.contains('\\\\'),\n            \"vendor filename must be local\"\n        );\n        ensure!(uris.insert(entry.uri), \"duplicate vendor URI\");\n        let checksum = hex::encode(Sha256::digest(&files[&entry.file]));\n        ensure!(\n            checksum == entry.sha256,\n            \"vendored checksum mismatch: {}\",\n            entry.file\n        );\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\nstruct LocalSchemas(BTreeMap<String, Value>);\nimpl Retrieve for LocalSchemas {\n    fn retrieve(\n        &self,\n        uri: &Uri<String>,\n    ) -> std::result::Result<Value, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {\n        self.0\n            .get(uri.as_str())\n            .cloned()\n            .ok_or_else(|| format!(\"non-vendored reference refused: {uri}\").into())\n    }\n}\nfn local_schemas() -> Result<LocalSchemas> {\n    let files = vendor_files()?;\n    let mut schemas = BTreeMap::new();\n    for entry in manifest()?.files {\n        let schema: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&files[&entry.file])?;\n        ensure!(\n            schema[\"$id\"] == entry.uri,\n            \"vendor id differs from approved retrieval URI\"\n        );\n        schemas.insert(entry.uri, schema);\n    }\n    Ok(LocalSchemas(schemas))\n}\n\npub(super) fn compile_schema(schema: &Value) -> Result<Validator> {\n    // No runtime backtracking: look-around/backreferences, malformed patterns and\n    // compilation exhaustion fail at construction. The RED tests cover both regex\n    // keywords, including underneath `not` (where treating errors as false is unsafe).\n    jsonschema::options()\n        .with_draft(Draft::Draft202012)\n        .with_retriever(local_schemas()?)\n        .with_pattern_options(PatternOptions::regex().size_limit(1 << 20))\n        .build(schema)\n        .map_err(|error| anyhow::anyhow!(\"schema compilation failed: {error}\"))\n}\n\nfn local_references(value: &Value, document: &Value) -> Result<()> {\n    match value {\n        Value::Object(map) => {\n            for keyword in [\"$ref\", \"$dynamicRef\", \"$recursiveRef\"] {\n                if let Some(reference) = map.get(keyword) {\n                    let reference = reference.as_str().context(\"reference must be a string\")?;\n                    ensure!(\n                        reference.starts_with(\"#/\"),\n                        \"only local JSON Pointer references are allowed: {reference}\"\n                    );\n                    ensure!(\n                        document.pointer(&reference[1..]).is_some(),\n                        \"unresolved reference: {reference}\"\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n            // Do not let a local reference change meaning under a nested base URI.\n            ensure!(\n                !map.contains_key(\"$id\"),\n                \"contract schemas must not rebase local references with $id\"\n            );\n            for child in map.values() {\n                local_references(child, document)?;\n            }\n        }\n        Value::Array(array) => {\n            for child in array {\n                local_references(child, document)?;\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\nfn pointer_segment(value: &str) -> String {\n    value.replace('~', \"~0\").replace('/', \"~1\")\n}\n/// RFC 6901 section 6: a JSON Pointer used as a URI fragment must\n/// percent-encode every byte outside RFC 3986 `fragment` characters\n/// (for example the braces of templated path keys such as `{session}`).\nfn fragment(pointer: &str) -> String {\n    let mut out = String::with_capacity(pointer.len());\n    for byte in pointer.bytes() {\n        let allowed = byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b\"-._~!$&'()*+,;=:@/?\".contains(&byte);\n        if allowed {\n            out.push(char::from(byte));\n        } else {\n            out.push_str(&format!(\"%{byte:02X}\"));\n        }\n    }\n    out\n}\nfn inline_schema_pointers(value: &Value, pointer: &str, result: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {\n    match value {\n        Value::Object(map) => {\n            for (key, child) in map {\n                let child_pointer = format!(\"{pointer}/{}\", pointer_segment(key));\n                if key == \"schema\" {\n                    result.insert(child_pointer.clone());\n                }\n                inline_schema_pointers(child, &child_pointer, result);\n            }\n        }\n        Value::Array(array) => {\n            for (i, child) in array.iter().enumerate() {\n                inline_schema_pointers(child, &format!(\"{pointer}/{i}\"), result);\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n}\n/// Response Links name operations by `operationId`/`operationRef`, which the\n/// reference checks above do not resolve. None are needed yet, so refuse them\n/// outright rather than let a fragment point at a nonexistent operation.\nfn no_links(document: &Value) -> Result<()> {\n    ensure!(\n        document.pointer(\"/components/links\").is_none(),\n        \"response links are not supported in the management contract\"\n    );\n    for (path, item) in document[\"paths\"].as_object().context(\"paths missing\")? {\n        for (method, operation) in item.as_object().into_iter().flatten() {\n            for (status, response) in operation[\"responses\"].as_object().into_iter().flatten() {\n                ensure!(\n                    response.get(\"links\").is_none(),\n                    \"response links are not supported: {method} {path} {status}\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\npub(super) fn validate_document(document: &Value) -> Result<()> {\n    local_references(document, document)?;\n    no_links(document)?;\n    let validator = compile_schema(&json!({\"$ref\":OPENAPI_META}))?;\n    validator\n        .validate(document)\n        .map_err(|error| anyhow::anyhow!(\"OpenAPI 3.1 metaschema: {error}\"))?;\n    ensure!(document[\"openapi\"] == \"3.1.0\", \"expected OpenAPI 3.1.0\");\n    // The OAS metaschema validates schema keyword shapes, but does not compile\n    // patterns/refs in each body schema. Compile every component (even unused\n    // fragments) and inline Schema Object, with the full document as pointer root.\n    let mut pointers = BTreeSet::new();\n    for name in document[\"components\"][\"schemas\"]\n        .as_object()\n        .context(\"schemas missing\")?\n        .keys()\n    {\n        pointers.insert(format!(\"/components/schemas/{}\", pointer_segment(name)));\n    }\n    inline_schema_pointers(document, \"\", &mut pointers);\n    for pointer in pointers {\n        let mut schema = document.clone();\n        schema[\"$ref\"] = json!(format!(\"#{}\", fragment(&pointer)));\n        compile_schema(&schema).with_context(|| format!(\"compile contract schema at {pointer}\"))?;\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod fragment_tests {\n    #[test]\n    fn templated_path_pointers_become_valid_uri_fragments() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            super::fragment(\"/paths/~1api~1v1~1me~1sessions~1{session}/delete\"),\n            \"/paths/~1api~1v1~1me~1sessions~1%7Bsession%7D/delete\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(super::fragment(\"/a b/%/\\\"/<>\"), \"/a%20b/%25/%22/%3C%3E\");\n    }\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":2,"timestamp":1793209908865},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:48.867Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_5d13715fdd6145fa81d7a9eb57f6bcdc|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23634d2b0819dbb9a01e54bf8da49","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"    let mut ids = BTreeSet::new();\n    for (path, item) in paths {\n        ensure!(path.starts_with(\"/api/v1/\"), \"non-management path {path}\");\n        for (method, operation) in item.as_object().context(\"invalid path item\")? {\n            let key = format!(\"{} {path}\", method.to_ascii_uppercase());\n            let declaration = inventory\n                .get(&key)\n                .with_context(|| format!(\"extra operation {key}\"))?;\n            ensure!(\n                method == &declaration.method,\n                \"noncanonical method {method}\"\n            );\n            actual.insert(key.clone());\n            ensure!(\n                operation[\"operationId\"] == operation_id(declaration),\n                \"operationId drift for {key}\"\n            );\n            ensure!(\n                ids.insert(\n                    operation[\"operationId\"]\n                        .as_str()\n                        .context(\"operationId missing\")?\n                ),\n                \"duplicate operationId\"\n            );\n            ensure!(\n                operation[\"x-omg-access\"] == declaration.access\n                    && operation[\"x-omg-token-policy\"] == declaration.token_policy\n                    && operation\n                        .get(\"x-omg-required-scope\")\n                        .and_then(Value::as_str)\n                        == declaration.scope,\n                \"access/token/scope drift for {key}\"\n            );\n            ensure!(\n                declaration.scope.is_some() || operation.get(\"x-omg-required-scope\").is_none(),\n                \"scope on non-Scope operation\"\n            );\n            ensure!(\n                operation[\"security\"] == declaration.security,\n                \"security drift for {key}\"\n            );\n            let parameters = operation[\"parameters\"]\n                .as_array()\n                .context(\"parameters missing\")?;\n            let actual_path = parameters\n                .iter()\n                .filter(|p| p[\"in\"] == \"path\")\n                .cloned()\n                .collect::<Vec<_>>();\n            ensure!(\n                actual_path == path_parameters(path),\n                \"path parameters drift for {key}\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n    ensure!(actual.iter().eq(inventory.keys()), \"omitted operations\");\n    ensure!(\n        document[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][\"ErrorEnvelope\"] == error_schema()?,\n        \"undocumented error vocabulary or envelope drift\"\n    );\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n// Compiled constants are authoritative for common/policy reasons. Detailed error\n// call sites and the manually rendered upload envelope are additionally inventoried\n// from parsed source, failing on unrecognized reason expressions rather than guessing.\nfn error_reasons() -> Result<BTreeSet<String>> {\n    let mut reasons = crate::management::REASONS\n        .iter()\n        .map(|(_, r)| (*r).to_owned())\n        .chain(\n            crate::management::POLICY_REASONS\n                .iter()\n                .map(|(_, r, _, _)| (*r).to_owned()),\n        )\n        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();\n    let mut files = Vec::new();\n    source_files(\n        &Path::new(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\")).join(\"src/management\"),\n        &mut files,\n    )?;\n    for file in files {\n        let source = syn::parse_file(&std::fs::read_to_string(&file)?)?;\n        let constants = source\n            .items\n            .iter()\n            .filter_map(|item| match item {\n                syn::Item::Const(item) => {\n                    string_literal(&item.expr).map(|value| (item.ident.to_string(), value))\n                }\n                _ => None,\n            })\n            .collect();\n        let mut visitor = DetailedReasons {\n            constants,\n            reasons: &mut reasons,\n            errors: Vec::new(),\n        };\n        visitor.visit_file(&source);\n        ensure!(\n            visitor.errors.is_empty(),\n            \"{}: {}\",\n            file.display(),\n            visitor.errors.join(\"; \")\n        );\n    }\n    Ok(reasons)\n}\nfn source_files(directory: &Path, files: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) -> Result<()> {\n    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(directory)? {\n        let path = entry?.path();\n        let name = path.file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy();\n        if name.contains(\"test\") {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if path.is_dir() {\n            source_files(&path, files)?;\n        } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == \"rs\") {\n            files.push(path);\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\nfn string_literal(expr: &syn::Expr) -> Option<String> {\n    if let syn::Expr::Lit(syn::ExprLit {\n        lit: syn::Lit::Str(s),\n        ..\n    }) = expr\n    {\n        Some(s.value())\n    } else {\n        None\n    }\n}\nstruct DetailedReasons<'a> {\n    constants: BTreeMap<String, String>,\n    reasons: &'a mut BTreeSet<String>,\n    errors: Vec<String>,\n}\nimpl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for DetailedReasons<'_> {\n    fn visit_item_mod(&mut self, item: &'ast syn::ItemMod) {\n        if item\n            .attrs\n            .iter()\n            .any(|attr| attr.path().is_ident(\"cfg\") && quote_cfg_test(attr))\n        {\n            return;\n        }\n        syn::visit::visit_item_mod(self, item);\n    }\n    fn visit_expr_call(&mut self, call: &'ast syn::ExprCall) {\n        if let syn::Expr::Path(path) = &*call.func\n            && path\n                .path\n                .segments\n                .last()\n                .is_some_and(|s| s.ident == \"detailed\")\n        {\n            let reason = call.args.iter().nth(1).and_then(|expr| {\n                string_literal(expr).or_else(|| {\n                    if let syn::Expr::Path(path) = expr {\n                        self.constants\n                            .get(&path.path.get_ident()?.to_string())\n                            .cloned()\n                    } else {\n                        None\n                    }\n                })\n            });\n            if let Some(reason) = reason {\n                self.reasons.insert(reason);\n            } else {\n                self.errors\n                    .push(\"unsupported detailed-error reason; inventory it explicitly\".into());\n            }\n        }\n        syn::visit::visit_expr_call(self, call);\n    }\n    fn visit_item_fn(&mut self, function: &'ast syn::ItemFn) {\n        if function.sig.ident == \"dashboard_upload_error\" {\n            struct Upload<'a>(&'a mut BTreeSet<String>);\n            impl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for Upload<'_> {\n                fn visit_expr_tuple(&mut self, tuple: &'ast syn::ExprTuple) {\n                    if tuple.elems.len() == 3\n                        && let Some(reason) = string_literal(&tuple.elems[1])\n                    {\n                        self.0.insert(reason);\n                    }\n                    syn::visit::visit_expr_tuple(self, tuple);\n                }\n            }\n            Upload(&mut *self.reasons).visit_block(&function.block);\n        }\n        syn::visit::visit_item_fn(self, function);\n    }\n}\nfn quote_cfg_test(attr: &syn::Attribute) -> bool {\n    // Parse the cfg tokens, rather than substring matching comments or identifiers.\n    attr.parse_args::<syn::Meta>().is_ok_and(|meta| match meta {\n        syn::Meta::Path(path) => path.is_ident(\"test\"),\n        syn::Meta::List(list) => list\n            .tokens\n            .into_iter()\n            .any(|token| matches!(token, proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) if i == \"test\")),\n        _ => false,\n    })\n}\nfn error_schema() -> Result<Value> {\n    Ok(\n        json!({\"type\":\"object\",\"required\":[\"error\"],\"additionalProperties\":false,\n            \"description\":\"Shared management handler envelope, not a universal framework response. code equals the HTTP status string. Selected reasons add period, limit, missing_meters or retry details. Reason-specific details are not yet exhaustive (#81).\",\n            \"properties\":{\"error\":{\"type\":\"object\",\"required\":[\"code\",\"message\"],\"additionalProperties\":true,\n                \"properties\":{\n                    \"code\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"pattern\":\"^[1-5][0-9]{2}$\"},\n                    \"message\":{\"type\":\"string\"},\"reason\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":error_reasons()?},\n                    \"period\":{\"type\":\"string\"},\"limit\":{\"type\":\"string\"},\n                    \"missing_meters\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\"}},\n                    \"retry_after_seconds\":{\"type\":\"integer\",\"minimum\":0}\n                }}}\n        }),\n    )\n}\npub(super) fn operation_count(document: &Value) -> usize {\n    document[\"paths\"]\n        .as_object()\n        .unwrap()\n        .values()\n        .map(|v| v.as_object().unwrap().len())\n        .sum()\n}\npub(super) fn placeholder_count(value: &Value) -> usize {\n    match value {\n        Value::Object(map) => {\n            usize::from(\n                map.get(\"x-omg-schema-status\")\n                    .is_some_and(|v| v == \"incomplete\"),\n            ) + map.values().map(placeholder_count).sum::<usize>()\n        }\n        Value::Array(array) => array.iter().map(placeholder_count).sum(),\n        _ => 0,\n    }\n}\npub(super) fn encode(document: &Value) -> Vec<u8> {\n    // Explicit canonical insertion order remains deterministic even if another crate\n    // enables serde_json/preserve_order through feature unification.\n    fn canonical(value: &Value) -> Value {\n        match value {\n            Value::Object(map) => Value::Object(\n                map.iter()\n                    .map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), canonical(v)))\n                    .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>()\n                    .into_iter()\n                    .collect(),\n            ),\n            Value::Array(array) => Value::Array(array.iter().map(canonical).collect()),\n            value => value.clone(),\n        }\n    }\n    let mut bytes = serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&canonical(document)).unwrap();\n    bytes.push(b'\\n');\n    bytes\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":2,"timestamp":1793209908866},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:48.867Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

 sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO provider_connections(id,name,provider,credential_ref) VALUES($1,'Cost matrix','anthropic','env:PLACEHOLDER')\")\n        .bind(cost_connection).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    let cost_mapping = super::provider_cost_tests::mapping(cost_connection, Some(ws));\n    let (status, cost_map) = call(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.admin,\n        \"POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/provider-cost-mappings\",\n        cost_mapping.clone(),\n    )\n    .await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK, \"{cost_map}\");\n    let mut next_mapping = cost_mapping;\n    next_mapping[\"supersedes\"] = cost_map[\"id\"].clone();\n    case!(\n        \"provider_costs::import\",\n        \"POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/provider-cost-imports\",\n        &f.admin,\n        cost_upload\n    );\n    case!(\n        \"provider_costs::create_mapping\",\n        \"POST\",\n }\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":3,"timestamp":1793209908866},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:48.867Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_07de17a9a91c4bf883dfbe07445acfde|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23634d2b0819db8dd8d0d5b57b8dd","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! #37 PR2: admin-token authentication, scope/IP enforcement and the\n//! per-attempt audit trail, on the real served router and real middleware\n//! (not injected principals) wherever the test needs the full stack, and on\n//! `isolated::stub_routes()` (every declared route, the same two middleware\n//! layers, no handler logic) for the exhaustive route/scope matrix.\nuse super::*;\nuse crate::{\n    admin_tokens,\n    governance::locks,\n    identity::{self, throttle},\n    route_policy::{TokenPolicy, TokenScope},\n};\nuse isolated::stub_routes;\n\n/// One admin token for `owner` (who must already be a currently effective\n/// Platform Admin), with exactly `scopes` and optionally an IP allowlist.\n/// Mirrors the browser handler's own contract: the owner's user-scope\n/// authority lock exclusive, before the first write.\nasync fn mint(\n    pool: &PgPool,\n    owner: Uuid,\n    scopes: &[&str],\n    allowlist: Option<&[&str]>,\n) -> (Uuid, String) {\n    let mut tx = crate::db::begin(pool).await.unwrap();\n    locks::exclusive(&mut tx, [locks::Scope::User(owner)])\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    let scopes: Vec<String> = scopes.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();\n    let allow: Option<Vec<String>> = allowlist.map(|a| a.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect());\n    let created = admin_tokens::create_admin_token(\n        &mut tx,\n        owner,\n        \"Test token\",\n        &scopes,\n        30,\n        allow.as_deref(),\n    )\n    .await\n    .unwrap();\n    tx.commit().await.unwrap();\n    (created.id, created.token)\n}\n\n/// The latest `start` attempt recorded for this exact token/route (never\n/// ambiguous across probes made one at a time by these tests).\nasync fn last_outcome(\n    pool: &PgPool,\n    token_id: Uuid,\n    method: &str,\n    template: &str,\n) -> Option<String> {\n    sqlx::query_scalar(\n        \"SELECT outcome FROM admin_token_attempts WHERE token_id=$1 AND phase='start' AND route_method=$2 AND route_template=$3 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1\",\n    )\n    .bind(token_id)\n    .bind(method)\n    .bind(template)\n    .fetch_optional(pool)\n    .await\n    .unwrap()\n}\n\nfn concrete_path(path: &str, team: Uuid) -> String {\n    let filler = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();\n    path.replace(\"{ws}\", &team.to_string())\n        .replace(\"{kind}\", \"team\")\n        .replace(\"{role}\", \"user\")\n        .replace(\"{root}\", \"root-1\")\n        .replace(\"{*rest}\", \"unknown\")\n        .replace(\"{user}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{key}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{id}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{model}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{deployment}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{execution}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{hold}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{session}\", &filler)\n        .replace(\"{token}\", &filler)\n}\n\n/// A synthetic, distinct-per-call client address (#37 PR2 finding 2\n/// regression guard): the *existing*, unrelated per-IP admin-token limiter\n/// in `identity.rs` buckets every in-process request with no `ConnectInfo`\n/// under one shared \"unknown peer\" bucket, and the expanded matrix below\n/// issues far more requests than that bucket's burst allows. Each probe\n/// gets its own address so this test exercises the token-policy matrix,\n/// not an unrelated rate limiter it isn't about.\nfn synthetic_peer(seq: u32) -> std::net::SocketAddr {\n    std::net::SocketAddr::new(\n        std::net::IpAddr::V4(std::net::Ipv4Addr::from(0x0A00_0001u32.wrapping_add(seq))),\n        0,\n    )\n}\n\n/// Like [`authz_matrix_tests::request`](super::request), but for an admin\n/// token only, with its own synthetic peer address per call (see\n/// [`synthetic_peer`]).\nasync fn probe(app: &Router, method: &str, path: &str, token: &str, seq: u32) -> StatusCode {\n    let mut req = Request::builder()\n        .method(method)\n        .uri(path)\n        .header(\"content-type\", \"application/json\")\n        .header(\"origin\", ORIGIN)\n        .header(\"authorization\", format!(\"Bearer {token}\"))\n        .body(Body::from(\"{}\"))\n        .unwrap();\n    req.extensions_mut()\n        .insert(axum::extract::ConnectInfo(synthetic_peer(seq)));\n    app.clone().oneshot(req).await.unwrap().status()\n}\n\nfn stub_app(s: &Store, identity: identity::IdentityState) -> Router {\n    stub_routes()\n        .route_layer(middleware::from_fn_with_state(\n            s.clone(),\n            authorization::require_route_access,\n        ))\n        .route_layer(middleware::from_fn_with_state(\n            identity,\n            authenticate_management,\n        ))\n        .with_state(s.clone())\n}\n\n/// TEST-FIRST (RED before this PR's authentication/authorization layer\n/// existed, every one of these requests 404'd or 401'd with no scope\n/// concept at all): every declared route's token policy is enforced\n/// exactly as declared, for every route in the inventory -- not a sample,\n/// and not only against one \"a wrong scope\" probe (finding 2: the original\n/// version of this test took only the *first* mismatching scope via\n/// `Iterator::find`, so a route that happened to also admit, say, the\n/// second-tried scope by mistake would never have been caught). For a\n/// `Scope(required)` route this now probes, individually, every one of the\n/// five single-scope tokens (exactly one must admit it), a token holding\n/// all five scopes together (still admitted: extra scopes never revoke the\n/// one that matters), and a token holding every *other* scope but not the\n/// required one (still refused: holding several wrong scopes at once must\n/// not accidentally satisfy the check). `Denied`/`BrowserAdminOnly` routes\n/// refuse all of that -- every single-scope token and the all-five token\n/// alike -- regardless of how many scopes the token holds.\n///\n/// Two independent oracles, not one: the per-attempt audit row\n/// (`admin_token_attempts.outcome`, the authorization decision alone) and\n/// the actual HTTP response (which also depends on route readiness --\n/// finding 1's HEAD/501 bug was invisible to the audit-only oracle alone,\n/// since the outcome is decided before the readiness check runs).\n#[sqlx::test(migrations = \"./enterprise_migrations\")]\nasync fn every_route_enforces_its_declared_token_policy(pool: PgPool) {\n    let f = fixture(&pool).await;\n    let mock = MockProvider::start(None).await;\n    let identity = mock.state(pool.clone()).await;\n    let app = stub_app(&f.s, identity);\n    let all_scope_strings: Vec<&str> = TokenScope::ALL.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();\n    let mut tokens = std::collections::HashMap::new();\n    for scope in TokenScope::ALL {\n        let (id, token) = mint(&pool, f.admin.user_id, &[scope.as_str()], None).await;\n        tokens.insert(scope.as_str(), (id, token));\n    }\n    let (all_five_id, all_five_token) =\n        mint(&pool, f.admin.user_id, &all_scope_strings, None).await;\n    // Keyed by the *excluded* scope: every other scope present, the\n    // required one deliberately absent.\n    let mut missing_one = std::collections::HashMap::new();\n    for excluded in TokenScope::ALL {\n        let rest: Vec<&str> = all_scope_strings\n            .iter()\n            .copied()\n            .filter(|s| *s != excluded.as_str())\n            .collect();\n        missing_one.insert(\n            excluded.as_str(),\n            mint(&pool, f.admin.user_id, &rest, None).await,\n        );\n    }\n    let mut checked = 0;\n    for route in ROUTES {\n        for method in route.methods.split_whitespace().flat_map(|m| {\n            if m == \"*\" {\n                vec![\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\"]\n\n[1582 more lines in file. Use offset=181 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":2,"timestamp":1793209908866},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:48.867Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

Implemented by both principal types so every\n/// shared preflight/transaction helper below (`resources::audit`,\n/// `catalog_tx`, `workspace_access` and friends) accepts either without the\n/// ~180 existing `Extension<BrowserPrincipal>` handlers needing to change at\n/// all (#37 PR2): `BrowserPrincipal` already implements this trait, so every\n/// call site that still passes one keeps compiling unchanged, and\n/// `route_policy::TokenPolicy::Denied`/`BrowserAdminOnly` refuse an admin\n/// token before any handler using a generic helper is ever reached by one.\npub(crate) trait Actor {\n    fn user_id(&self) -> Uuid;\n    fn token_id(&self) -> Option<Uuid> {\n        None\n    }\n}\nimpl Actor for BrowserPrincipal {\n    fn user_id(&self) -> Uuid {\n        self.user_id\n    }\n}\nimpl Actor for ManagementPrincipal {\n    fn user_id(&self) -> Uuid {\n        ManagementPrincipal::user_id(self)\n    }\n    fn token_id(&self) -> Option<Uuid> {\n        ManagementPrincipal::token_id(self)\n    }\n}\nuse axum::{\n    Extension, Json, Router,\n    extract::{Path, Query, State},\n    http::StatusCode,\n    middleware,\n    response::{IntoResponse, Response},\n    routing::{get, patch, post},\n};\nuse resources::audit;\nuse serde::Deserialize;\nuse serde_json::{Value, json};\nuse sqlx::{Postgres, Transaction};\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\ntype ApiResult = Result<Json<Value>, ApiError>;\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub(crate) struct ApiError(pub(crate) StatusCode, pub(crate) &'static str);\n/// Stable machine-readable reasons for selected errors (additive `error.reason`).\nconst REASONS: &[(&str, &str)] = &[\n    (PERSONAL_NAME_FIXED, \"personal_workspace_name_fixed\"),\n    (\n        PERSONAL_VISIBILITY_FIXED,\n        \"personal_workspace_visibility_fixed\",\n    ),\n    (PERSONAL_LIMITS, \"personal_limits_platform_controlled\"),\n    (STACKED_LEGACY, \"stacked_budgets_require_budgets_field\"),\n    (KEY_REVOKED, \"key_revoked\"),\n    (KEY_DISABLED, \"key_disabled\"),\n    (MEMBER_VISIBILITY, \"workspace_wide_visibility_required\"),\n    (settings::KEY_LIFETIME, \"key_lifetime_exceeds_maximum\"),\n    (settings::SETTING_LOCKED, \"setting_locked_by_environment\"),\n    (\n        settings::REFERENCE_NOT_ALLOWED,\n        \"credential_reference_not_allowed\",\n    ),\n    (settings::PLAINTEXT_REMOTE, \"plaintext_requires_loopback\"),\n    (settings::EMAIL_NOT_CONFIGURED, \"email_not_configured\"),\n    (settings::EMAIL_TEST_LIMIT, \"email_test_rate_limited\"),\n    (\n        settings::storage::STORAGE_OFF,\n        \"file_storage_not_configured\",\n    ),\n    (\n        settings::storage::STORAGE_UNHEALTHY,\n        \"file_storage_unhealthy\",\n    ),\n    (\n        settings::storage::STORAGE_TEST_LIMIT,\n        \"storage_test_rate_limited\",\n    ),\n    (branding::LOGO_TYPE, \"logo_unsupported_type\"),\n    (branding::LOGO_TOO_LARGE, \"logo_too_large\"),\n    (branding::LOGO_INVALID, \"logo_invalid_image\"),\n    (branding::LOGO_DIMENSIONS, \"logo_dimensions\"),\n    (alerts::PERSONAL_BUILTIN_ONLY, \"personal_alerts_built_in\"),\n\n[757 more lines in file. {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_5d4e3a4bec744962b1cf879dbf44ba94|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236384528819d83a90ab1f26bc13a","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Shared source inventory: filenames never confer test-only status.\nuse std::{\n    collections::BTreeSet,\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n};\n\npub(crate) fn test_only(attrs: &[syn::Attribute]) -> bool {\n    fn requires_test(meta: &syn::Meta) -> bool {\n        match meta {\n            syn::Meta::Path(p) => p.is_ident(\"test\"),\n            syn::Meta::List(list) if list.path.is_ident(\"all\") || list.path.is_ident(\"any\") => {\n                let items = list\n                    .parse_args_with(\n                        syn::punctuated::Punctuated::<syn::Meta, syn::Token![,]>::parse_terminated,\n                    )\n                    .unwrap();\n                if list.path.is_ident(\"all\") {\n                    items.iter().any(requires_test)\n                } else {\n                    !items.is_empty() && items.iter().all(requires_test)\n                }\n            }\n            _ => false,\n        }\n    }\n    attrs.iter().any(|attr| {\n        attr.path().is_ident(\"cfg\")\n            && attr\n                .parse_args::<syn::Meta>()\n                .is_ok_and(|meta| requires_test(&meta))\n    })\n}\n\npub(crate) struct Source {\n    pub(crate) name: String,\n    pub(crate) ast: syn::File,\n}\n\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct Modules {\n    production: BTreeSet<PathBuf>,\n    tests: BTreeSet<PathBuf>,\n}\nimpl Modules {\n    fn load(&mut self, path: &Path, gated: bool) {\n        let path = std::fs::canonicalize(path)\n            .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!(\"unresolved module {}\", path.display()));\n        let seen = if gated {\n            &mut self.tests\n        } else {\n            &mut self.production\n        };\n        if !seen.insert(path.clone()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        let ast = syn::parse_file(&std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap()).unwrap();\n        let parent = path.parent().unwrap();\n        let stem = path.file_stem().unwrap().to_str().unwrap();\n        let module_dir = if matches!(stem, \"lib\" | \"main\" | \"mod\") {\n            parent.to_owned()\n        } else {\n            parent.join(stem)\n        };\n        self.items(&ast.items, &module_dir, parent, gated);\n    }\n    fn items(&mut self, items: &[syn::Item], module_dir: &Path, path_dir: &Path, gated: bool) {\n        for item in items {\n            let syn::Item::Mod(module) = item else {\n                continue;\n            };\n            let gated = gated || test_only(&module.attrs);\n            let name = module.ident.to_string();\n            if let Some((_, items)) = &module.content {\n                let dir = module_dir.join(&name);\n                self.items(items, &dir, &dir, gated);\n                continue;\n            }\n            let explicit = module.attrs.iter().find_map(|attr| {\n                if !attr.path().is_ident(\"path\") {\n                    return None;\n                }\n                let syn::Meta::NameValue(value) = &attr.meta else {\n                    panic!(\"invalid module path\");\n                };\n                let syn::Expr::Lit(value) = &value.value else {\n                    panic!(\"nonliteral module path\");\n                };\n                let syn::Lit::Str(value) = &value.lit else {\n                    panic!(\"nonstring module path\");\n                };\n                Some(path_dir.join(value.value()))\n            });\n            let path = explicit.unwrap_or_else(|| {\n                let flat = module_dir.join(format!(\"{name}.rs\"));\n                let nested = module_dir.join(&name).join(\"mod.rs\");\n                assert_ne!(\n                    flat.exists(),\n                    nested.exists(),\n                    \"missing or ambiguous module {name} in {}\",\n                    module_dir.display()\n                );\n                if flat.exists() { flat } else { nested }\n            });\n            self.load(&path, gated);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\npub(crate) fn sources_at(root: &Path) -> Vec<Source> {\n    fn files(dir: &Path, out: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) {\n        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir).unwrap() {\n            let path = entry.unwrap().path();\n            if path.is_dir() {\n                files(&path, out);\n            } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == \"rs\") {\n                out.push(path);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    let root = std::fs::canonicalize(root).unwrap();\n    let mut modules = Modules::default();\n    for name in [\"lib.rs\", \"main.rs\"] {\n        let path = root.join(name);\n        if path.exists() {\n            modules.load(&path, false);\n        }\n    }\n    let mut all = Vec::new();\n    files(&root, &mut all);\n    all.sort();\n    all.into_iter()\n        .filter(|path| !modules.tests.contains(path) || modules.production.contains(path))\n        .map(|path| Source {\n            name: path\n                .strip_prefix(&root)\n                .unwrap()\n                .to_string_lossy()\n                .replace('\\\\', \"/\"),\n            ast: syn::parse_file(&std::fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap()).unwrap(),\n        })\n        .collect()\n}\n\npub(crate) fn production_sources() -> Vec<Source> {\n    sources_at(&Path::new(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\")).join(\"src\"))\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn exclusions_follow_module_gates_not_filenames_or_directory_names() {\n    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n    for (name, source) in [\n        (\n            \"lib.rs\",\n            \"mod harmless_tests; mod shared; #[cfg(test)] #[path=\\\"shared.rs\\\"] mod also_test; #[cfg(all(test, feature=\\\"integration-tests\\\"))] #[path=\\\"odd.rs\\\"] mod gated;\",\n        ),\n        (\"harmless_tests.rs\", \"fn production() {}\"),\n        (\"shared.rs\", \"fn production() {}\"),\n        (\"odd.rs\", \"mod child;\"),\n        (\"odd/child.rs\", \"fn test_helper() {}\"),\n        (\"tests.rs\", \"fn orphan() {}\"),\n        (\"tests/payload.rs\", \"fn orphan() {}\"),\n    ] {\n        let path = dir.path().join(name);\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();\n        std::fs::write(path, source).unwrap();\n    }\n    let names: BTreeSet<_> = sources_at(dir.path()).into_iter().map(|s| s.name).collect();\n    assert_eq!(\n        names,\n        [\n            \"lib.rs\",\n            \"harmless_tests.rs\",\n            \"shared.rs\",\n            \"tests.rs\",\n            \"tests/payload.rs\"\n        ]\n        .map(str::to_owned)\n        .into_iter()\n        .collect()\n    );\n    let module: syn::ItemMod = syn::parse_str(\n        \"#[cfg(any(test, feature=\\\"integration-tests\\\"))] mod usable_in_production;\",\n    )\n    .unwrap();\n    assert!(!test_only(&module.attrs));\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":2,"timestamp":1793209914162},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:54.163Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_ccfee80cacdd4d4591e307be8a03651c|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2363a15a0819d89b9d8b27fa380de","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Installation logo (Admin › Settings › General › Logo; migration 0023).\n//!\n//! An uploaded PNG, JPEG or WebP image (never SVG), at most 512 KiB, stored\n//! encrypted in the file store (purpose `branding`, installation scope, never\n//! expires). The magic bytes must match the declared type, the container\n//! structure must parse to its end (no trailing data) and the dimensions are\n//! read from the image header. Markup anywhere in the file is refused, so a\n//! polyglot never becomes a branding object.\n//!\n//! The current logo is served same-origin and without a session at\n//! `GET /api/v1/branding/logo` (the sign-in page needs it before login), with\n//! `nosniff`, `default-src 'none'`, an ETag and a short public cache. Replacing\n//! or removing the logo deletes the previous object after the settings change\n//! commits. Admin writes (audited), Auditor reads.\nuse super::*;\nuse crate::filestore::{FileError, FileStoreRuntime, NewFile, Purpose, files::FileStorage};\nuse axum::{\n    body::{Body, Bytes},\n    http::{HeaderMap, HeaderValue, header},\n    routing::put,\n};\nuse futures::StreamExt;\n\n/// The public, unauthenticated logo endpoint.\npub(crate) const LOGO_PATH: &str = \"/api/v1/branding/logo\";\npub(crate) const MAX_BYTES: usize = 512 * 1024;\nconst SIDES: std::ops::RangeInclusive<u32> = 16..=4096;\npub(super) const LOGO_TYPE: &str = \"The logo must be a PNG, JPEG or WebP image\";\npub(super) const LOGO_TOO_LARGE: &str = \"The logo must be 512 KiB or smaller\";\npub(super) const LOGO_INVALID: &str = \"The logo file is not a valid image\";\npub(super) const LOGO_DIMENSIONS: &str = \"The logo must be 16 to 4096 pixels on each side\";\nconst STORE_UNAVAILABLE: &str = \"File storage unavailable\";\n\n/// `{url, updated_at}` of the current logo over `installation_settings s`.\n/// The URL carries a version so a replaced logo is never served from cache.\npub(crate) const PUBLIC_LOGO_SQL: &str = \"CASE WHEN s.branding_logo_file_id IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE jsonb_build_object('url','/api/v1/branding/logo?v='||left(replace(s.branding_logo_file_id::text,'-',''),12),'updated_at',s.branding_logo_updated_at) END\";\n/// Settings view: also the stored dimensions.\nconst SETTINGS_LOGO_SQL: &str = \"CASE WHEN s.branding_logo_file_id IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE jsonb_build_object('url','/api/v1/branding/logo?v='||left(replace(s.branding_logo_file_id::text,'-',''),12),'updated_at',s.branding_logo_updated_at,'width',s.branding_logo_width,'height',s.branding_logo_height) END\";\n\npub(super) fn routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new().route(\n        \"/api/v1/platform/settings/general/logo\",\n        put(upload).delete(remove),\n    )\n}\n/// Mounted without the session layer.\npub(super) fn public_routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new().route(LOGO_PATH, get(serve))\n}\n\nfn runtime(ext: Option<Extension<FileStoreRuntime>>) -> FileStoreRuntime {\n    ext.map(|Extension(r)| r).unwrap_or_default()\n}\n\n/// The current logo for `/me` and the sign-in config: `{url, updated_at}`,\n/// or null when none is set or the file store is off (nothing could be served).\npub(crate) async fn public_logo<'e>(\n    executor: impl sqlx::PgExecutor<'e>,\n    rt: &FileStoreRuntime,\n) -> Result<Value, sqlx::Error> {\n    if rt.store().is_none() {\n        return Ok(Value::Null);\n    }\n    sqlx::query_scalar(&format!(\n        \"SELECT {PUBLIC_LOGO_SQL} FROM installation_settings s WHERE s.singleton\"\n    ))\n    .fetch_one(executor)\n    .await\n    .map(|v: Option<Value>| v.unwrap_or(Value::Null))\n}\n\n/// Sign-in page branding: `(logo, installation_name)`. The display name is\n/// only included with a logo (it is the logo's alt text); otherwise null.\npub(crate) async fn sign_in_branding(\n    pool: &sqlx::PgPool,\n    rt: &FileStoreRuntime,\n) -> Result<(Value, Value), sqlx::Error> {\n    let logo = public_logo(pool, rt).await?;\n    if logo.is_null() {\n        return Ok((logo, Value::Null));\n    }\n    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT name FROM installation WHERE singleton\")\n        .fetch_one(pool)\n        .await?;\n    Ok((logo, Value::String(name)))\n}\n\n/// The settings view of the logo and what an upload accepts.\npub(super) async fn settings_json(\n    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,\n    rt: &FileStoreRuntime,\n) -> Result<(Value, Value), ApiError> {\n    let logo: Option<Value> = sqlx::query_scalar(&format!(\n        \"SELECT {SETTINGS_LOGO_SQL} FROM installation_settings s WHERE s.singleton\"\n    ))\n    .fetch_one(&mut **tx)\n    .await?;\n    let upload = json!({\n        \"available\": rt.store().is_some(),\n        \"max_bytes\": MAX_BYTES,\n        \"content_types\": ImageKind::ALL.map(ImageKind::content_type),\n        \"min_side\": SIDES.start(),\n        \"max_side\": SIDES.end(),\n        \"recommended_side\": 64,\n    });\n    Ok((logo.unwrap_or(Value::Null), upload))\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub(crate) enum ImageKind {\n    Png,\n    Jpeg,\n    Webp,\n}\nimpl ImageKind {\n    const ALL: [Self; 3] = [Self::Png, Self::Jpeg, Self::Webp];\n    pub(crate) fn content_type(self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            Self::Png => \"image/png\",\n            Self::Jpeg => \"image/jpeg\",\n            Self::Webp => \"image/webp\",\n        }\n    }\n    fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            Self::Png => \"png\",\n            Self::Jpeg => \"jpeg\",\n            Self::Webp => \"webp\",\n        }\n    }\n    fn from_content_type(value: &str) -> Option<Self> {\n        let essence = crate::filestore::files::normalize_content_type(value)?;\n        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|k| k.content_type() == essence)\n    }\n    fn sniff(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<Self> {\n        if bytes.starts_with(&PNG_SIGNATURE) {\n            Some(Self::Png)\n        } else if bytes.starts_with(&[0xFF, 0xD8, 0xFF]) {\n            Some(Self::Jpeg)\n        } else if bytes.len() >= 12 && &bytes[..4] == b\"RIFF\" && &bytes[8..12] == b\"WEBP\" {\n            Some(Self::Webp)\n        } else {\n            None\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub(crate) struct LogoImage {\n    pub(crate) kind: ImageKind,\n    pub(crate) width: u32,\n    pub(crate) height: u32,\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub(crate) enum LogoError {\n    /// Not declared as, or not actually, PNG/JPEG/WebP (SVG included).\n    Type,\n    TooLarge,\n    /// Malformed structure, trailing data, animation or embedded markup.\n    Invalid,\n    Dimensions,\n}\nimpl From<LogoError> for ApiError {\n    fn from(e: LogoError) -> Self {\n        match e {\n            LogoError::Type => ApiError(StatusCode::UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE, LOGO_TYPE),\n            LogoError::TooLarge => ApiError(StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE, LOGO_TOO_LARGE),\n            LogoError::Invalid => ApiError(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, LOGO_INVALID),\n            LogoError::Dimensions => ApiError(StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, LOGO_DIMENSIONS),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nconst PNG_SIGNATURE: [u8; 8] = [0x89, b'P', b'N', b'G', 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A];\n/// Markup that would make the bytes meaningful to a browser as a document.\nconst MARKUP: [&[u8]; 9] = [\n    b\"<svg\",\n    b\"<script\",\n    b\"<html\",\n\n[563 more lines in file. Use offset=181 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":2,"timestamp":1793209914162},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:51:54.164Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

}\n    fn range() -> String {\n        let date = Utc::now().date_naive();\n        format!(\n            \"start_date={}&end_date={}\",\n            date.with_day(1).unwrap(),\n .merge(crate::management::logs::routes())\n            .route(\n                \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/keys/{key}\",\n                axum::routing::get(crate::management::keys::key),\n            )\n            .route(\n                \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/keys/{key}/stats\",\n                axum::routing::get(crate::management::keys::key_stats),\n            )\n r.status();\n        let bytes = to_bytes(r.into_body(), 8 * 1024 * 1024).await.unwrap();\n        (\n            status,\n            serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap_or(Value::Null),\n        )\n    }\n    async fn member_key(f: &Fixture) -> Uuid {\n        let k = NewApiKey::generate().unwrap();\n        sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO api_keys(id,workspace_id,issued_to_user_id,name,secret_hash) {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_a226cd0562954257aa7d58383ba95489|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2364079a4819d900750b400f91cba","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Installation logo (0023): authority, the store-off gate, upload validation\n//! (types, SVG and polyglots, size, dimensions), the public endpoint's headers,\n//! replace/remove deleting the previous object, auditing and `/me`.\nuse super::*;\nuse crate::filestore::{FileStoreRuntime, ObjectKey};\nuse branding::fixtures::{jpeg, png, webp_lossless};\n\nconst LOGO: &str = \"/api/v1/platform/settings/general/logo\";\nconst GENERAL: &str = \"/api/v1/platform/settings/general\";\nconst PUBLIC: &str = \"/api/v1/branding/logo\";\n\nasync fn send(\n    s: &Store,\n    u: &BrowserPrincipal,\n    rt: Option<&FileStoreRuntime>,\n    method: &str,\n    path: &str,\n    content_type: &str,\n    body: Vec<u8>,\n) -> (StatusCode, Value) {\n    let mut router = routes()\n        .layer(Extension(crate::identity::ManagementPrincipal::Browser(\n            u.clone(),\n        )))\n        .layer(Extension(u.clone()));\n    if let Some(rt) = rt {\n        router = router.layer(Extension(rt.clone()));\n    }\n    let response = router\n        .with_state(s.clone())\n        .oneshot(\n            Request::builder()\n                .method(method)\n                .uri(path)\n                .header(\"content-type\", content_type)\n                .body(Body::from(body))\n                .unwrap(),\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    let status = response.status();\n    let bytes = to_bytes(response.into_body(), 1024 * 1024).await.unwrap();\n    (\n        status,\n        serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap_or(Value::Null),\n    )\n}\n\n/// The public endpoint through the full router (request middleware included).\nasync fn public(\n    s: &Store,\n    rt: Option<&FileStoreRuntime>,\n    etag: Option<&str>,\n) -> axum::http::Response<Body> {\n    let mut router = crate::http::router(s.clone());\n    if let Some(rt) = rt {\n        router = router.layer(Extension(rt.clone()));\n    }\n    let mut request = Request::builder().uri(format!(\"{PUBLIC}?v=abc\"));\n    if let Some(etag) = etag {\n        request = request.header(\"if-none-match\", etag);\n    }\n    router\n        .oneshot(request.body(Body::empty()).unwrap())\n        .await\n        .unwrap()\n}\n\nasync fn audit_count(pool: &PgPool, action: &str) -> i64 {\n    sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT count(*) FROM audit_events WHERE action=$1\")\n        .bind(action)\n        .fetch_one(pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap()\n}\n\nasync fn object_exists(pool: &PgPool, rt: &FileStoreRuntime, id: Uuid) -> bool {\n    let key: String = sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT object_key FROM stored_files WHERE id=$1\")\n        .bind(id)\n        .fetch_one(pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    rt.store()\n        .unwrap()\n        .head(&ObjectKey::parse(&key).unwrap())\n        .await\n        .unwrap()\n        .is_some()\n}\n\nasync fn current(pool: &PgPool) -> Option<Uuid> {\n    sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT branding_logo_file_id FROM installation_settings WHERE singleton\")\n        .fetch_one(pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap()\n}\n\n#[sqlx::test(migrations = \"./enterprise_migrations\")]\nasync fn logo_is_admin_write_auditor_read(pool: PgPool) {\n    let f = fixture(&pool).await;\n    let rt = FileStoreRuntime::memory();\n    for user in [&f.auditor, &f.owner, &f.member] {\n        for method in [\"PUT\", \"DELETE\"] {\n            let (status, _) = send(\n                &f.s,\n                user,\n                Some(&rt),\n                method,\n                LOGO,\n                \"image/png\",\n                png(64, 64),\n            )\n            .await;\n            assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, \"{method}\");\n        }\n    }\n    let (status, v) = send(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.admin,\n        Some(&rt),\n        \"PUT\",\n        LOGO,\n        \"image/png\",\n        png(64, 64),\n    )\n    .await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK, \"{v}\");\n    assert_eq!(v[\"logo\"][\"width\"], 64);\n    assert!(\n        v[\"logo\"][\"url\"]\n            .as_str()\n            .unwrap()\n            .starts_with(\"/api/v1/branding/logo?v=\")\n    );\n    // Auditors read the logo and upload limits; Users don't see settings.\n    let (status, v) = send(\n        &f.s,\n        &f.auditor,\n        Some(&rt),\n        \"GET\",\n\n[312 more lines in file. Use offset=141 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":1,"timestamp":1793209920611},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:52:00.612Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_dadbf455e44a4769aad97aca72a7272d|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2364079a4819db2f3f5d4bde6594d","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"\n    // Exercises the public auth routes, signed token exchange, persisted session,\n    // production authenticate_management middleware and actual management handlers.\n    async fn http_sign_in(app: &Router, mock: &MockProvider) -> (String, String) {\n        let (session, csrf, _) = http_sign_in_from(app, mock, \"/api/v1/auth/login\").await;\n        (session, csrf)\n    }\n\n    /// Signs in through `login` (which may carry `return_to`); also returns the callback's Location.\n    async fn http_sign_in_from(\n        app: &Router,\n        mock: &MockProvider,\n        login: &str,\n    ) -> (String, String, String) {\n        let response = app\n            .clone()\n            .oneshot(Request::builder().uri(login).body(Body::empty()).unwrap())\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(response.status(), StatusCode::SEE_OTHER);\n        let url = Url::parse(response.headers()[header::LOCATION].to_str().unwrap()).unwrap();\n        let params: HashMap<_, _> = url\n            .query_pairs()\n            .map(|(k, v)| (k.into_owned(), v.into_owned()))\n            .collect();\n        mock.claims.lock().unwrap()[\"nonce\"] = json!(params[\"nonce\"]);\n        let browser = response_cookie(&response, BROWSER);\n        let response = app\n            .clone()\n            .oneshot(\n                Request::builder()\n                    .uri(format!(\n                        \"/api/v1/auth/callback?state={}&code=good\",\n                        params[\"state\"]\n                    ))\n                    .header(header::COOKIE, format!(\"{BROWSER}={browser}\"))\n                    // Recorded as the session's device label (#79).\n                    .header(header::USER_AGENT, FIREFOX_LINUX)\n                    .body(Body::empty())\n                    .unwrap(),\n            )\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(response.status(), StatusCode::SEE_OTHER);\n        (\n            response_cookie(&response, SESSION),\n            response_cookie(&response, CSRF),\n            response.headers()[header::LOCATION]\n                .to_str()\n                .unwrap()\n                .to_owned(),\n        )\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn return_paths_are_same_origin_dashboard_paths_only() {\n        for (input, expected) in [\n            (\"/workspaces/abc/keys\", Some(\"/workspaces/abc/keys\")),\n            (\n                \"/workspaces/abc/requests?status=failed&q=3cbf\",\n                Some(\"/workspaces/abc/requests?status=failed&q=3cbf\"),\n            ),\n            (\n                \"/admin/models/x?tab=routes\",\n                Some(\"/admin/models/x?tab=routes\"),\n            ),\n            (\"/home\", Some(\"/home\")),\n            (\"/a/../admin\", Some(\"/admin\")),\n        ] {\n            assert_eq!(safe_return_path(input).as_deref(), expected, \"{input}\");\n        }\n        for bad in [\n            \"\",\n            \"home\",\n            \"//evil.example/x\",\n            \"/\\\\evil.example\",\n            \"/\\\\/evil.example\",\n            \"https://evil.example/\",\n            \"javascript:alert(1)\",\n            \"/api/v1/me\",\n            \"/api\",\n            \"/v1/chat/completions\",\n            \"/health/ready\",\n            \"/a/../api/v1/me\",\n            \"/x\\ny\",\n            \"/x\\u{7}\",\n        ] {\n            assert_eq!(safe_return_path(bad), None, \"{bad:?}\");\n        }\n        assert_eq!(safe_return_path(&format!(\"/{}\", \"a\".repeat(2048))), None);\n    }\n\n Exact expectations for the two management middleware layers, independent of handlers.\nuse super::*;\nuse axum::routing::{MethodFilter, MethodRouter, any, on};\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]\n#[repr(usize)]\nenum Actor {\n    Anonymous,\n    Inference,\n    Unentitled,\n    User,\n    Auditor,\n    Admin,\n    NonMember,\n    KeyHolder,\n    OtherMember,\n    WorkspaceAdmin,\n    Owner,\n    PersonalOwner,\n    PersonalAuditor,\n    PersonalAdmin,\n    // An otherwise-authorized principal for this Access, with the token omitted.\n    NoCsrf,\n}\nconst ACTORS: [Actor; 15] = [\n    Actor::Anonymous,\n    Actor::Inference,\n    Actor::Unentitled,\n    Actor::User,\n    Actor::Auditor,\n    Actor::Admin,\n    Actor::NonMember,\n    Actor::KeyHolder,\n    Actor::OtherMember,\n    Actor::WorkspaceAdmin,\n    Actor::Owner,\n    Actor::PersonalOwner,\n    Actor::PersonalAuditor,\n    Actor::PersonalAdmin,\n    Actor::NoCsrf,\n];\n// Each cell is (GET/HEAD, mutation). 204 proves the stub was reached.\nconst A: (u16, u16) = (204, 204);\nconst U: (u16, u16) = (401, 401);\nconst F: (u16, u16) = (403, 403);\nconst N: (u16, u16) = (404, 404);\nconst C: (u16, u16) = (204, 403);\n// One independently written Access × Actor table. External route classes are\n// intentionally forbidden if accidentally mounted behind management middleware;\n// their real routers' credential families remain covered by the handler matrix.\nconst EXPECTED: &[(Access, [(u16, u16); 15])] = &[\n    (\n        Access::Anonymous,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::Health,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::InferenceKey,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F],\n    ),\n    (Access::Scim, [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F]),\n    (\n        Access::BrowserLogout,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, F],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::Browser,\n        [U, U, U, A, A, A, A, A, A, A, A, A, A, A, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::PlatformRead,\n        [U, U, U, F, A, A, F, F, F, F, F, F, A, A, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::PlatformWrite,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, A, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, A, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspaceMetadata,\n        [U, U, U, F, A, A, F, A, A, A, A, A, N, N, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspaceMember,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, A, A, A, A, A, N, N, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspaceAdmin,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, F, F, A, A, A, N, N, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspaceReportAdmin,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, F, F, A, A, A, N, N, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspaceAdminOrReader,\n        [U, U, U, F, A, A, F, F, F, A, A, A, N, N, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspacePlatformWrite,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, A, F, F, F, F, F, F, F, N, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspaceKey,\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, A, N, A, A, A, N, N, C],\n    ),\n    (\n        Access::WorkspaceKeyRotate,\n        // Workspace admins and owners see every key: refused rotation is 403.\n        [U, U, U, F, F, F, F, A, N, F, F, A, N, N, C],\n    ),\n];\nfn expected(access: Access, actor: Actor, method: &str) -> StatusCode {\n    let row = &EXPECTED\n        .iter()\n        .find(|(a, _)| *a == access)\n        .expect(\"missing expected Access row\")\n        .1;\n    let (safe, mutation) = row[actor as usize];\n    StatusCode::from_u16(if matches!(method, \"GET\" | \"HEAD\") {\n        safe\n    } else {\n        mutation\n    })\n    .unwrap()\n}\nfn methods(route: &crate::route_policy::Route) -> Vec<&str> {\n    route\n\n[431 more lines in file. Use offset=131 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":1,"timestamp":1793209920611},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:52:00.612Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

\"/api/v1/platform/installation/policy\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        PLATFORM_READ\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"PUT\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/installation/policy\",\n route!(\n        \"PUT\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/workspace-types/{kind}/policy\",\n        PlatformWrite,\n        GOVERNANCE_WRITE\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/workspaces/{ws}/policy\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        PLATFORM_READ\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"PUT DELETE\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/workspaces/{ws}/policy\",\n route!(\n        \"PUT\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/deployments/{deployment}/routing\",\n        PlatformWrite,\n        CATALOG_WRITE\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/cost-report\",\n        PlatformRead,\n        PLATFORM_READ\n    ),\n    // #37 PR2: admin API token CRUD. Browser session (Platform Admin) only,\n    // never delegable to a token, regardless of any scope it holds.\n    route!(\n        \"GET POST\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/admin-tokens\",\n        PlatformWrite,\n        BROWSER_ADMIN_ONLY\n    ),\n    route!(\n        \"DELETE\",\n        \"/api/v1/platform/admin-tokens/{token}\",\n        PlatformWrite,\n        BROWSER_ADMIN_ONLY\n    ),\n];\n\nfn find(method: &str, path: &str) -> Option<&'static Route> {\n    let exact = |method| {\n        ROUTES\n            .iter()\n            .find(|r| r.path == path && r.methods.split_whitespace().any(|m| m == method))\n    };\n    // Explicit HEAD, if registered, takes precedence over GET's implicit HEAD.\n    exact(method)\n        .or_else(|| (method == \"HEAD\").then(|| exact(\"GET\")).flatten())\n        .or_else(|| exact(\"*\"))\n}\n\npub(crate) fn lookup(method: &str, path: &str) -> Option<Access> {\n    find(method, path).map(|r| r.access)\n}\n\n/// The admin-token decision for this exact method/template, or `None` for a\n/// route not declared in [`ROUTES]` at all (denies by construction: the\n/// caller must treat an absent entry as [`TokenPolicy::Denied`]).\npub(crate) fn token_policy(method: &str, path: &str) -> Option<TokenPolicy> {\n    find(method, path).map(|r| r.token_policy)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    /// Every declared route has an explicit token policy (the type system\n    /// already guarantees this at compile time; this is a readability\n    /// cross-check that `token_policy` and `lookup` agree on every entry).\n    #[test]\n    fn every_route_has_an_explicit_token_policy() {\n        for route in ROUTES {\n            assert_eq!(\n                token_policy(route.methods.split_whitespace().next().unwrap(), route.path),\n                Some(route.token_policy),\n                \"{} {}\",\n                route.methods,\n                route.path\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn an_undeclared_route_denies_tokens() {\n        assert_eq!(token_policy(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/platform/not-a-route\"), None);\n        assert_eq!(lookup(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/platform/not-a-route\"), None);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn scope_strings_match_admin_tokens_scopes() {\n        for scope in [\n            TokenScope::PlatformRead,\n            TokenScope::CatalogWrite,\n            TokenScope::AccessWrite,\n            TokenScope::GovernanceWrite,\n            TokenScope::SettingsWrite,\n        ] {\n            assert!(\n                crate::admin_tokens::SCOPES.contains(&scope.as_str()),\n                \"{scope:?}\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":5,"timestamp":1793209936184},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:52:16.185Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
