{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_59314d9032174c049a77d40c09b6b5f0|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236501a9c819d9b385238245d630b","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Saved platform receivers only. No network I/O or secret resolution here.\nuse super::*;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\n\npub(super) fn routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new()\n        .route(\"/api/v1/platform/alerts/webhooks\", get(list).post(create))\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/alerts/webhooks/{id}\",\n            get(item).patch(update).delete(remove),\n        )\n        .route(\"/api/v1/platform/alerts/webhooks/{id}/test\", post(test))\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/alerts/webhooks/{id}/deliveries\",\n            get(deliveries),\n        )\n}\nconst STATUS: &str = \"jsonb_build_object('id',d.id,'label',d.label,'enabled',d.enabled,'created_at',d.created_at,'updated_at',d.updated_at,'signing_configured',true,'overlap_active',coalesce(d.previous_secret_until>clock_timestamp(),false),'previous_secret_until',d.previous_secret_until,'waiting',(SELECT count(*) FROM alert_webhook_outbox q WHERE q.destination_id=d.id AND q.state='waiting' AND q.destination_generation=d.enable_generation),'exhausted',(SELECT count(*) FROM alert_webhook_outbox q WHERE q.destination_id=d.id AND q.state='exhausted'),'last_success_at',(SELECT completed_at FROM alert_webhook_outbox q WHERE q.destination_id=d.id AND q.state='sent' ORDER BY completed_at DESC LIMIT 1),'last_error',(SELECT last_error FROM alert_webhook_outbox q WHERE q.destination_id=d.id AND q.last_error IS NOT NULL ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1)) || CASE WHEN $1 THEN jsonb_build_object('url',d.url,'current_secret_ref',d.current_secret_ref,'previous_secret_ref',d.previous_secret_ref) ELSE '{}'::jsonb END\";\nasync fn read_tx(\n    s: &Store,\n    u: &ManagementPrincipal,\n) -> Result<(Transaction<'static, Postgres>, bool), ApiError> {\n    // Token revalidation must be inside a lock-capable transaction, catalog first.\n    let mut tx = crate::db::begin(&s.pool).await?;\n    resources::catalog_lock(&mut tx, false).await?;\n    resources::platform_read(&mut tx, u).await?;\n    let admin = resources::platform_role(&mut tx, u.user_id()).await? == \"admin\"\n        && u.as_admin_token()\n            .is_none_or(|token| token.scopes.iter().any(|scope| scope == \"settings:write\"));\n    Ok((tx, admin))\n}\nasync fn status(\n    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,\n    id: Uuid,\n    admin: bool,\n) -> Result<Value, ApiError> {\n    sqlx::query_scalar(&format!(\n        \"SELECT {STATUS} FROM alert_webhook_destinations d WHERE d.id=$2 AND d.deleted_at IS NULL\"\n    ))\n    .bind(admin)\n    .bind(id)\n    .fetch_optional(&mut **tx)\n    .await?\n    .ok_or_else(missing)\n}\nasync fn list(State(s): State<Store>, Extension(u): Extension<ManagementPrincipal>) -> ApiResult {\n    let (mut tx, admin) = read_tx(&s, &u).await?;\n    let data: Vec<Value> = sqlx::query_scalar(&format!(\"SELECT {STATUS} FROM alert_webhook_destinations d WHERE d.deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY d.created_at,d.id\"))\n        .bind(admin).fetch_all(&mut *tx).await?;\n    tx.commit().await?;\n    Ok(Json(json!({\"data\":data})))\n}\nasync fn item(\n    State(s): State<Store>,\n    Extension(u): Extension<ManagementPrincipal>,\n    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,\n) -> ApiResult {\n    let (mut tx, admin) = read_tx(&s, &u).await?;\n    let value = status(&mut tx, id, admin).await?;\n    tx.commit().await?;\n    Ok(Json(value))\n}\n/// Saved receivers only. The installation management lock in write_tx\n/// serializes the audit-log allowance across replicas, including token callers.\nasync fn test(\n    State(s): State<Store>,\n    Extension(u): Extension<ManagementPrincipal>,\n    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,\n    body: axum::body::Bytes,\n) -> ApiResult {\n    let mut tx = super::super::settings::write_tx(&s, &u).await?;\n    super::super::settings::no_configuration(&body)?;\n    let saved: Option<(i64,)> = sqlx::query_as(\"SELECT enable_generation FROM alert_webhook_destinations d WHERE id=$1 AND enabled AND deleted_at IS NULL AND url IS NOT NULL FOR SHARE OF d\")\n        .bind(id).fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;\n    let (generation,) = saved.ok_or(ApiError(\n        StatusCode::CONFLICT,\n        \"An enabled saved destination is required\",\n    ))?;\n    let (mine,destination,all): (i64,i64,i64) = sqlx::query_as(\"SELECT count(*) FILTER(WHERE actor_user_id=$1 AND created_at>clock_timestamp()-interval '1 minute'),count(*) FILTER(WHERE resource_id=$2 AND created_at>clock_timestamp()-interval '1 minute'),count(*) FROM audit_events WHERE action='alert_webhook.tested' AND created_at>clock_timestamp()-interval '1 hour'\")\n        .bind(u.user_id()).bind(id).fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;\n    if mine >= 2 || destination >= 2 || all >= 20 {\n        return Err(ApiError(\n            StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,\n            \"Too many webhook tests; try again later\",\n        ));\n    }\n    let delivery = Uuid::new_v4();\n    sqlx::query(\"INSERT INTO alert_webhook_outbox(id,destination_id,destination_generation,test,payload) VALUES($1,$2,$3,true,convert_to(alert_webhook_payload($1,NULL,NULL),'UTF8'))\")\n        .bind(delivery).bind(id).bind(generation).execute(&mut *tx).await?;\n    audit(\n        &mut tx,\n        &u,\n        None,\n        \"alert_webhook.tested\",\n        \"alert_webhook\",\n        Some(id),\n        json!({}),\n    )\n    .await?;\n    tx.commit().await?;\n    Ok(Json(json!({\"id\":delivery,\"state\":\"waiting\"})))\n}\n\n/// Bounded metadata only, never payloads, incident ids, URLs or references.\nasync fn deliveries(\n    State(s): State<Store>,\n    Extension(u): Extension<ManagementPrincipal>,\n    Path(id): Path<Uuid>,\n) -> ApiResult {\n    let (mut tx, admin) = read_tx(&s, &u).await?;\n    let destination = status(&mut tx, id, admin).await?;\n    let counts: Value = sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT jsonb_object_agg(state,n) FROM (SELECT states.state,count(q.id) n FROM unnest(ARRAY['waiting','leased','sent','failed','exhausted','ineligible']) states(state) LEFT JOIN (alert_webhook_outbox q JOIN alert_webhook_destinations d ON d.id=q.destination_id) ON q.destination_id=$1 AND q.state=states.state AND (q.state<>'waiting' OR q.destination_generation=d.enable_generation) GROUP BY states.state) totals\")\n        .bind(id).fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;\n    let rows: Vec<Value> = sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT jsonb_build_object('id',q.id,'state',q.state,'test',q.test,'attempt_count',q.attempt_count,'created_at',q.created_at,'completed_at',q.completed_at,'next_attempt_at',CASE WHEN q.state='waiting' THEN q.next_attempt_at END,'last_error',q.last_error) FROM alert_webhook_outbox q JOIN alert_webhook_destinations d ON d.id=q.destination_id WHERE q.destination_id=$1 AND (q.state<>'waiting' OR q.destination_generation=d.enable_generation) ORDER BY q.created_at DESC,q.id DESC LIMIT 50\")\n        .bind(id).fetch_all(&mut *tx).await?;\n    tx.commit().await?;\n    Ok(Json(\n        json!({\"destination\":destination,\"counts\":counts,\"data\":rows}),\n    ))\n}\n\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\nstruct Create {\n    label: String,\n    url: String,\n    current_secret_ref: String,\n}\nfn reference(s: &Store, value: &str) -> Result<(), ApiError> {\n    let name = value.strip_prefix(\"env:\").ok_or_else(invalid)?;\n    if name.is_empty()\n        || name.len() > 128\n        || !name\n            .bytes()\n            .enumerate()\n            .all(|(i, c)| c == b'_' || c.is_ascii_uppercase() || (i > 0 && c.is_ascii_digit()))\n        || !s.alert_webhooks.secrets.allows(value)\n    {\n        return Err(invalid());\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\nfn label(value: &str) -> Result<String, ApiError> {\n    let value = value.trim();\n    if value.is_empty() || value.chars().count() > 120 || value.chars().any(char::is_control) {\n\n[202 more lines in file. {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_dacf1a86f6604f33b99cc318b195b120|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236517060819da2dc67c3bb24e253","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\"body\":\"## Summary\\n\\nThe management API is documented in prose (`docs/management-api.md`) but has no versioned machine-readable contract.\\n\\n## Scope\\n\\n- OpenAPI 3.1 for `/api/v1/*` (management) and a separate one for the inference surface's supported subset; generated from or checked against the Rust handlers so it can't drift (CI test).\\n- Error envelope (`error.code` HTTP status string, `error.reason` machine code) and micro-USD string money types described precisely.\\n- Published on the docs site; used by the Terraform provider and SDK generation.\\n\\n---\\n\\n_Filed from the post-v0.4.0 roadmap review, 2026-10-10. Source: roadmap R16, program phase 5._\\n\",\"title\":\"OpenAPI specification for the management API\"}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":449,"timestamp":1793209940303},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:52:20.312Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_09a4d772f033420e8cba4d24d72bf122|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23653b75c819db7cfab7d9cb20c25","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\"body\":\"<!-- omg-work-item:inference-openapi -->\\n\\n## Objective\\nDescribe the gateway's implemented inference subset separately from management and vendor-wide capabilities.\\n\\nParent tracking issue: #38.\\n\\n**Status:** Planned work item; specification and implementation plan require approval. Filing is not implementation authority or acceptance of held work.\\n\\n## Scope and acceptance\\n- [ ] Supported protocol/provider pairings, streaming/error contracts and unsupported capabilities match executable handlers/tests.\\n- [ ] Inference machine error codes stay distinct from management codes; usage, pricing estimates and unknown states are described honestly.\\n- [ ] Drift checks and SDK contract tests reject fabricated routes/options; examples contain placeholders only and no paid calls.\\n\\n## Verification\\n- [ ] Add baseline/red regressions and positive controls; retain existing assertions, no skips or weakened gates.\\n- [ ] `cargo test --workspace --all-features`\\n- [ ] `npm run typecheck:web`\\n- [ ] `npm run test:web`\\n- [ ] Exercise this issue's actual API/runtime/browser path and negative authority cases; mock/fake-wire coverage is not live acceptance.\\n\\nDatabase gates require a fresh, explicitly selected disposable database and separately authorized serialized role/ACL changes. Frontend gates require a fresh lockfile install, not a held patched cache. No real provider calls without explicit cost approval. Listed commands are future gates, not completed results.\\n\\n## Dependencies\\nBlocked by: None currently fixed; confirm conditional/recommended dependencies during specification.\\n\\n## Likely source areas\\n- `apps/gateway/src/protocols/`\\n- `docs/protocol-matrix.md`\\n- `tests/sdk-contracts.mjs`\\n- `tests/ (new contract checks)`\\n\\n## Decisions before implementation\\nApprove the scoped specification and compatibility/upgrade contract before implementation.\\n\\n## Delivery slices and checkpoint\\n1. Approve the scoped spec, authority/compatibility contract and testable success criteria.\\n2. Add regressions, then implement one complete path in small source/test slices (normally no more than about five files per implementation task).\\n3. Run applicable complete gates and review runtime evidence before selecting the next slice.\\n4. Parent-owned secret scan, scoped commit, exact-SHA CI and verified release before another issue starts.\\n\\n## Release and exclusions\\nPatch if contract-only; reassess new behavior separately. One implementation issue per branch and release; parent epics are tracking containers, not release bundles.\\n\\nNo automatic old-installation reset/migration, global inference limits, changed personal privacy, weakened tests, paid/live deployment, secrets-manager integration, website publication or unrelated dependency upgrades. Changes to APIs/schema/ACLs/dependencies/CI require explicit approval.\\n\",\"title\":\"API contracts: supported inference-subset OpenAPI 3.1\"}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":497,"timestamp":1793209940310},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:52:20.312Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_6464207395864e6f96062e907a8a778f|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae23653b75c819d909208bab7f41c26","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Live platform policy, pinned pricing and privacy-scoped accounting reports.\nuse super::*;\nuse crate::{\n    billing::{CachePricing, CostComponents},\n    inference::{error::InferenceError, types::Usage},\n};\nmod holds;\nmod policies;\npub(super) use policies::{\n    BudgetInput, Limits, MAX_KEY_ISSUANCES, check_initial_key_limits, initial_key_limits,\n    json_budgets, json_limits, key_limits, key_usage, layers_with, lineage, lineage_budget_windows,\n    store_initial_key_limits, workspace_budget_windows,\n};\n#[cfg(all(test, feature = \"integration-tests\"))]\nmod holds_tests;\npub(super) mod prices;\npub(crate) mod reports;\npub(crate) mod suggestion;\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests;\npub(super) fn routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new()\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/policy\",\n            get(policies::workspace_policy).put(policies::put_workspace_policy),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/keys/{key}/policy\",\n            get(policies::key_policy).put(policies::put_key_policy),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/cost-report\",\n            get(reports::workspace_report),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/cost-summary\",\n            get(reports::cost_summary),\n        )\n        .route(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/costs\", get(reports::costs))\n        .route(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/holds\", get(holds::workspace))\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/usage-export\",\n            get(reports::usage_export),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/costs/{execution}/reconcile\",\n            post(reports::reconcile),\n        )\n}\npub(super) fn platform_routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new()\n        .route(\"/api/v1/platform/holds\", get(holds::platform))\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/installation/policy\",\n            get(policies::installation_policy_removed).put(policies::installation_policy_removed),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/workspace-types/{kind}/policy\",\n            get(policies::type_policy).put(policies::put_type_policy),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/workspaces/{ws}/policy\",\n            get(policies::platform_workspace_policy)\n                .put(policies::put_platform_workspace_policy)\n                .delete(policies::reset_platform_workspace_policy),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/deployments/{deployment}/prices\",\n            get(prices::prices).post(prices::create_price),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/deployments/{deployment}/price-suggestion\",\n            get(suggestion::price_suggestion),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/models/{model}/routing\",\n            get(prices::model_routing).put(prices::put_model_routing),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/deployments/{deployment}/routing\",\n            get(prices::deployment_routing).put(prices::put_deployment_routing),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/cost-report\",\n            get(reports::platform_report),\n        )\n}\nfn nullable<'de, D, T>(d: D) -> Result<Option<T>, D::Error>\nwhere\n    D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,\n    T: Deserialize<'de>,\n{\n    Option::deserialize(d)\n}\nfn positive_limit(v: i64) -> bool {\n    (1..=i64::from(i32::MAX)).contains(&v)\n}\nfn money(v: &str, positive: bool) -> Result<i64, ApiError> {\n    if v.is_empty() || v.len() > 19 || !v.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {\n        return Err(invalid());\n    }\n    let n: i64 = v.parse().map_err(|_| invalid())?;\n    if positive && n == 0 {\n        return Err(invalid());\n    }\n    Ok(n)\n}\nfn residency_valid(v: &str) -> bool {\n    (1..=64).contains(&v.len())\n        && v.bytes()\n            .next()\n            .is_some_and(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit())\n        && v.bytes().all(|b| {\n            b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || matches!(b, b'.' | b'_' | b'-')\n        })\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":4,"timestamp":1793209940310},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:52:20.312Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_7088db1466d44e38b7765cd0ca910edc|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2368c9ab0819d8adc8876759be418","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Regression guard for the audited configuration surface (#197), not a Rust sandbox.\nuse crate::configuration_source_tests::{production_sources, sources_at, test_only};\nuse syn::visit::{self, Visit};\n\nconst MUTATIONS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"set_var\",\n    \"remove_var\",\n    \"setenv\",\n    \"unsetenv\",\n    \"putenv\",\n    \"clearenv\",\n    \"_putenv\",\n    \"_wputenv\",\n    \"SetEnvironmentVariableA\",\n    \"SetEnvironmentVariableW\",\n];\nconst LOADERS: &[&str] = &[\n    \"libloading\",\n    \"dlopen2\",\n    \"dlopen\",\n    \"dlmopen\",\n    \"dlsym\",\n    \"LoadLibraryA\",\n    \"LoadLibraryW\",\n    \"LoadLibraryExA\",\n    \"LoadLibraryExW\",\n    \"GetProcAddress\",\n];\n// No production subprocesses exist. CLI commands are Rust subcommands, not OS\n// processes. Future exceptions must identify a function and a reviewed reason.\nconst PROCESS_SPAWN_ALLOWLIST: &[(&str, &str)] = &[];\n\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct Guard {\n    startup: bool,\n    main: bool,\n    /// OTLP transports get configuration from their caller, never the process.\n    otlp: bool,\n    in_main: bool,\n    importing: bool,\n    startup_mutations: usize,\n    file: String,\n    function: String,\n    violations: Vec<String>,\n}\nimpl Guard {\n    fn check_process(&mut self, path: &str) {\n        let path = path.replace(\"r#\", \"\");\n        let process = path == \"std::process\"\n            || path.starts_with(\"std::process::\")\n            || path == \"tokio::process\"\n            || path.starts_with(\"tokio::process::\")\n            || path == \"process::Command\"\n            || path.starts_with(\"process::Command::\")\n            || path.contains(\"::process::Command\")\n            || path == \"Command::new\"\n            || path.starts_with(\"Command::new::\");\n        if process\n            && !PROCESS_SPAWN_ALLOWLIST.iter().any(|(site, reason)| {\n                !reason.is_empty() && *site == format!(\"{}::{}\", self.file, self.function)\n            })\n        {\n            self.violations.push(path);\n        }\n    }\n    fn check(&mut self, word: &str) {\n        if self.otlp && word == \"env\" {\n            self.violations\n                .push(\"OTLP transport must not read process configuration\".into());\n        }\n        if MUTATIONS.contains(&word) {\n            if self.importing {\n                self.startup_mutations += 1;\n            } else {\n                self.violations.push(word.to_owned());\n            }\n        }\n        if LOADERS.contains(&word)\n            || (word == \"dotenvy\" && !self.in_main)\n            || (word == \"import_secret_files\" && !self.importing && !self.in_main)\n        {\n            self.violations.push(word.to_owned());\n        }\n    }\n    fn macro_tokens(&mut self, tokens: proc_macro2::TokenStream) {\n        // Parse item/statement-shaped macro fragments as well, so grouped use\n        // aliases cannot hide behind an opaque token group.\n        let block = proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(proc_macro2::Group::new(\n            proc_macro2::Delimiter::Brace,\n            tokens.clone(),\n        ));\n        if let Ok(block) = syn::parse2::<syn::Block>(block.into()) {\n            self.visit_block(&block);\n        }\n        let mut compact = String::new();\n        for token in tokens {\n            match token {\n                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) => {\n                    let name = i.to_string().trim_start_matches(\"r#\").to_owned();\n                    self.check(&name);\n                    compact.push_str(&name);\n                }\n                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Punct(p) => compact.push(p.as_char()),\n                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(g) => {\n                    self.macro_tokens(g.stream());\n                    compact.push(' ');\n                }\n                // Text in a string is data, not source tokens.\n                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Literal(_) => compact.push(' '),\n            }\n        }\n        for path in [\n            \"std::process\",\n            \"tokio::process\",\n            \"process::Command\",\n            \"Command::new\",\n        ] {\n            if compact.contains(path) {\n                self.check_process(path);\n            }\n        }\n        for alias in [\n            \"usestdas\",\n            \"usetokioas\",\n            \"usestd::*\",\n            \"usetokio::*\",\n            \"externcratestdas\",\n            \"externcratetokioas\",\n        ] {\n            if compact.contains(alias) {\n                self.violations.push(alias.to_owned());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nimpl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for Guard {\n    fn visit_item_mod(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ItemMod) {\n        if !test_only(&node.attrs) {\n            visit::visit_item_mod(self, node);\n        }\n    }\n    fn visit_item_fn(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ItemFn) {\n        if test_only(&node.attrs) {\n            return;\n        }\n        let previous = (self.importing, self.in_main, self.function.clone());\n        self.function = node.sig.ident.to_string();\n        self.importing = self.startup && node.sig.ident == \"import_secret_files\";\n        self.in_main = self.main && node.sig.ident == \"main\";\n        visit::visit_item_fn(self, node);\n        (self.importing, self.in_main, self.function) = previous;\n    }\n    fn visit_impl_item_fn(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ImplItemFn) {\n        if test_only(&node.attrs) {\n            return;\n        }\n        let previous = std::mem::replace(&mut self.function, node.sig.ident.to_string());\n        visit::visit_impl_item_fn(self, node);\n        self.function = previous;\n    }\n\n[181 more lines in file.    sqlx::query(\"UPDATE api_keys SET expires_at=now()+interval '3 days'\")\n        .execute(&pool)\n        .await\n        .unwrap();\n    let report = crate::alerts::evaluate_once(&f.s).await.unwrap().unwrap();\n    assert_eq!((report.fired, report.failed_rules), (3, 0));\n    let mock = MockProvider::start(None).await;\n    let identity = mock.state(pool.clone()).await;\n    let engine = crate::inference::Engine::new(\n        std::sync::Arc::new(f.s.clone()),\n        crate::providers::ProviderRegistry::default(),\n        crate::inference::EngineLimits::default(),\n    )\n    .unwrap();\n    let app = crate::http::router_with_identity(f.s.clone(), None, engine, identity);\n    // Exercise a running gateway over loopback as well as its full middleware.\n    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(\"127.0.0.1:0\").await.unwrap();\n    let address = listener.local_addr().unwrap();\n    let server = tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap() });\n    let app = format!(\"http://{address}\");\n    async fn get(app: &str, s: &Session, path: &str) -> (StatusCode, Value) {\n        let response = reqwest::Client::builder()\n            .no_proxy()\n            .redirect(reqwest::redirect::Policy::none())\n            .build()\n            .unwrap()\n            .get(format!(\"{app}{path}\"))\n            .header(\"cookie\", format!(\"omg_session={}\", s.cookie))\n            .send()\n            .await\n            .unwrap();\n        let status = response.status();\n        (status, response.json().await.unwrap())\n    }\n    let history = format!(\n        \"/api/v1/workspaces/{}/alerts/events?kind=key_expiring\",\n        f.team\n    );\n    let holder = session(&pool, &f.member).await;\n    assert_eq!(get(&app, &holder, &history).await.0, StatusCode::FORBIDDEN);\n    let (status, feed) = get(&app, &holder, \"/api/v1/me/notifications\").await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    assert_eq!(\n        feed[\"data\"].as_array().unwrap().len(),\n        1,\n        \"only the holder's own reminder\"\n    );\n    let holder_event: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT e.id FROM alert_events e JOIN api_keys k ON k.id=e.api_key_id WHERE k.issued_to_user_id=$1\")\n        .bind(f.member.user_id).fetch_one(&pool).await.unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(feed[\"data\"][0][\"id\"], holder_event.to_string());\n    let admin = session(&pool, &ws_admin).await;\n    let (status, history_rows) = get(&app, &admin, &history).await;\n    assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);\n    assert_eq!(\n        history_rows[\"data\"].as_array().unwrap().len(),\n        2,\n        \"workspace admin sees both shared reminders\"\n    );\n    for u in [&f.admin, &f.auditor] {\n        let s = session(&pool, u).await;\n        assert_eq!(\n            get(\n                &app,\n                &s,\n                &format!(\n                    \"/api/v1/workspaces/{}/alerts/events?kind=key_expiring\",\n                    f.personal\n                )\n            )\n            .await\n            .0,\n            StatusCode::NOT_FOUND\n        );\n        for path in [\n            \"/api/v1/me/notifications\",\n            \"/api/v1/platform/alerts/events?kind=key_expiring\",\n            &history,\n        ] {\n            let (status, rows) = get(&app, &s, path).await;\n            assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK, \"{path}: {rows}\");\n            assert_eq!(\n                rows[\"data\"],\n                json!([]),\n                \"platform authority never grants reminder visibility\"\n            );\n\n[455 more lines in file. Use offset=480 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":1,"timestamp":1793209997219},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:17.221Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

Optional for older clients: absent keeps the\n    /// stored value; explicit null clears it (tighten-only rules still apply).\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"supplied\")]\n    concurrent_jobs: Option<Option<i64>>,\n    /// \"Storage\" quota in bytes (0020): type default, platform override and\n    /// workspace local layers only. Absent keeps the stored value; explicit\n    /// null clears it (tighten-only rules still apply).\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"supplied\")]\n    storage_bytes: Option<Option<i64>>,\n    /// Key issuance budgets per rolling 24 hours (0053): one holder's, and the\n    /// workspace's. Workspace layers only, like `storage_bytes`: absent keeps\n    /// the stored value; explicit null clears it (tighten-only rules apply).\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"supplied\")]\n    key_issuances_per_holder: Option<Option<i64>>,\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"supplied\")]\n    key_issuances_per_workspace: Option<Option<i64>>,\n    /// Deprecated single budget (amount per `budget_period`). Required unless\n    /// `budgets` is supplied; then it must be absent.\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"supplied\")]\n    monthly_budget_microusd: Option<Option<String>>,\n    /// Deprecated; only with `monthly_budget_microusd`. Explicit null rejected.\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"present\")]\n    budget_period: Option<String>,\n    /// Full replacement set of stacked budgets (one per period).\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"present\")]\n    budgets: Option<Vec<BudgetInput>>,\n}\nfn present<'de, D, T>(d: D) -> Result<Option<T>, D::Error>\nwhere\n    D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,\n    T: Deserialize<'de>,\n{\n    T::deserialize(d).map(Some)\n}\nfn supplied<'de, D, T>(d: D) -> Result<Option<Option<T>>, D::Error>\nwhere\n    D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,\n    T: Deserialize<'de>,\n{\n    Option::<T>::deserialize(d).map(Some)\n}\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]\npub(crate) struct Limits {\n    pub(crate) requests_per_minute: Option<i64>,\n    pub(crate) tokens_per_minute: Option<i64>,\n    pub(crate) concurrent_requests: Option<i64>,\n    /// Concurrent active async jobs (video + batch).\n    pub(crate) concurrent_jobs: Option<i64>,\n    /// File store quota in bytes (workspace layers only; never key layers).\n    pub(crate) storage_bytes: Option<i64>,\n    /// Key creations and rotations one holder may make in a rolling 24 hours\n    /// (workspace layers only; never key layers).\n    pub(crate) key_issuances_per_holder: Option<i64>,\n    /// Key creations and rotations a workspace may make in a rolling 24 hours.\n    pub(crate) key_issuances_per_workspace: Option<i64>,\n    pub(crate) budgets: Budgets,\n}\ntype Row = (\n    Option<i64>,\n    Option<i64>,\n    Option<i64>,\n    Option<i64>,\n    Option<i64>,\n    Option<i64>,\n    Option<i64>,\n);\nconst COLS: &str = \"requests_per_minute,tokens_per_minute,concurrent_requests,concurrent_jobs\";\n/// Largest storage quota (1 PiB); rate limits stay within i32.\nconst MAX_STORAGE_BYTES: i64 = 1 << 50;\n/// Ceiling (and the value of a missing platform limit) of a key issuance budget.\npub(crate) const MAX_KEY_ISSUANCES: i64 = 10_000;\n/// The workspace-only columns of a layer table (storage and the key issuance\n/// budgets): real on workspace layers, NULL elsewhere.\nfn workspace_cols(scope: &Scope) -> &'static str {\n    match scope {\n        Scope::Type(_) | Scope::Override(_) | Scope::Local(_) => {\n            \"storage_bytes,key_issuances_per_holder,key_issuances_per_workspace\"\n        }\n        Scope::Key(..) => \"NULL::bigint,NULL::bigint,NULL::bigint\",\n    }\n}\nimpl Limits {\n    fn rates(&self) -> [Option<i64>; 7] {\n        [\n            self.requests_per_minute,\n            self.tokens_per_minute,\n            self.concurrent_requests,\n            self.concurrent_jobs,\n            self.storage_bytes,\n            self.key_issuances_per_holder,\n            self.key_issuances_per_workspace,\n        ]\n    }\n    /// Deprecated single-budget mirror: the smallest amount (ties prefer the shorter period).\n    pub(crate) fn legacy(&self) -> Option<(BudgetPeriod, i64)> {\n        self.budgets\n            .iter()\n            .min_by_key(|(p, a)| (**a, **p))\n            .map(|(p, a)| (*p, *a))\n    }\n    fn legacy_period(&self) -> Option<BudgetPeriod> {\n        (self.budgets.len() == 1)\n            .then(|| self.budgets.keys().next().copied())\n            .flatten()\n    }\n}\nfn rates(row: Option<Row>) -> Limits {\n    let (r, t, c, j, st, kh, kw) = row.unwrap_or_default();\n    Limits {\n        requests_per_minute: r,\n        tokens_per_minute: t,\n        concurrent_requests: c,\n        concurrent_jobs: j,\n        storage_bytes: st,\n        key_issuances_per_holder: kh,\n        key_issuances_per_workspace: kw,\n        budgets: Budgets::new(),\n    }\n}\n/// Storage scope of one policy layer.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug)]\n/// There is no installation layer (removed in 0026).\npub(crate) enum Scope {\n    Type(String),\n    Override(Uuid),\n    Local(Uuid),\n    Key(Uuid, Uuid),\n\n[1335 more lines in file. Use offset=151 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":1,"timestamp":1793209997219},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:17.221Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_c18fdbb830244725a8b740c191cc37d4|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2368d1fc8819d84de1bc37608540b","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Workspace › Files: the dashboard view of the gateway Files API store\n//! (docs/files-api.md). Same files as `GET /v1/files` for the workspace.\n//!\n//! Visibility is the Files API's (`crate::visibility`): workspace admins (and\n//! the personal owner) see every file; other members see every file when the\n//! workspace's object visibility is `workspace`, else the files they uploaded\n//! themselves (from the dashboard or with their own keys). Whoever sees a file\n//! may download it; deleting needs the creator or a workspace owner/admin\n//! (`crate::visibility::may_modify_sql`). Platform roles without membership\n//! see nothing.\n//! Uploads use the same pipeline as `POST /v1/files`; contents never reach\n//! audit or logs.\nuse super::*;\nuse crate::filestore::{\n    FileStoreRuntime, StoredFile,\n    files::{API_PURPOSES, FileList, FileStorage, parse_public_id, public_id},\n    upload::{self, UploadError, Uploader},\n};\nuse crate::visibility::{Viewer, sees_all};\nuse axum::{body::Body, http::HeaderMap, routing::delete};\n\npub(super) fn routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new()\n        .route(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/files\", get(list))\n        .route(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/files/{id}\", delete(remove))\n        .route(\"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/files/{id}/content\", get(content))\n}\n\n/// The upload route carries its own body cap (`GATEWAY_FILES_MAX_BYTES`), so\n/// it is mounted outside the shared 2 MiB management limit.\npub(super) fn upload_routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    let bytes = upload::limits().body_bytes();\n    Router::new().route(\n        \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/files/upload\",\n        post(upload_file)\n            .layer::<_, std::convert::Infallible>(axum::extract::DefaultBodyLimit::max(bytes))\n            .layer(tower_http::limit::RequestBodyLimitLayer::new(bytes)),\n    )\n}\n\nfn storage(s: &Store, ext: Option<Extension<FileStoreRuntime>>) -> FileStorage {\n    FileStorage::new(s.clone(), ext.map(|Extension(r)| r).unwrap_or_default())\n}\n\nfn render(f: &StoredFile, me: Uuid) -> Value {\n    json!({\n        \"id\": public_id(f.id),\n        \"filename\": f.filename.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| \"file\".into()),\n        \"purpose\": f.api_purpose,\n        \"bytes\": f.size_bytes,\n        \"content_type\": f.content_type,\n        \"created_at\": f.created_at,\n        \"expires_at\": f.expires_at,\n        \"status\": \"processed\",\n        \"mine\": f.created_by_user_id == Some(me),\n        \"via_key\": f.created_by_api_key_id.is_some(),\n    })\n}\n\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\npub(super) struct ListQuery {\n    purpose: Option<String>,\n    search: Option<String>,\n    limit: Option<i64>,\n    after: Option<String>,\n}\n\nconst STORE_UNAVAILABLE: &str = \"File storage unavailable\";\n\nfn store_error() -> ApiError {\n    ApiError(StatusCode::SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, STORE_UNAVAILABLE)\n}\n\nasync fn list(\n    State(s): State<Store>,\n    Extension(u): Extension<BrowserPrincipal>,\n    ext: Option<Extension<FileStoreRuntime>>,\n    Path(ws): Path<Uuid>,\n    Query(q): Query<ListQuery>,\n) -> ApiResult {\n    let limit = q.limit.unwrap_or(100);\n    if !(1..=200).contains(&limit)\n        || q.purpose\n            .as_deref()\n            .is_some_and(|p| !API_PURPOSES.contains(&p))\n        || q.search.as_deref().is_some_and(|t| t.chars().count() > 120)\n    {\n        return Err(invalid());\n    }\n\n[220 more lines in file. Use offset=91 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":1,"timestamp":1793209997220},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:17.221Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_fbf9bd3710444ff1a760eb1512b44487|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2368d1fc8819d9a0d978da0e458ce","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"[package]\nname = \"open-model-gateway\"\nversion = \"0.9.3\"\nedition = \"2024\"\nrust-version = \"1.88\"\nlicense = \"MIT\"\npublish = false\n\n[features]\ndefault = []\nintegration-tests = []\n\n[dependencies]\nanyhow = \"1\"\nasync-trait = \"0.1\"\nopenidconnect = { version = \"4\", default-features = false, features = [\"reqwest\", \"rustls-tls\"] }\naws-config = \"1\"\n# Without the default \"rustls\" feature: it pulls the legacy hyper 0.14 /\n# rustls 0.21 connector (unpatched h2 0.3 and rustls-webpki 0.101 advisories,\n# deny.toml). Requests use the gateway's own reqwest transport (providers/bedrock/transport.rs).\naws-sdk-bedrockruntime = { version = \"1\", default-features = false, features = [\"rt-tokio\", \"default-https-client\"] }\naws-smithy-types = { version = \"1\", features = [\"http-body-1-x\"] }\naws-smithy-runtime-api = { version = \"1\", features = [\"client\"] }\naws-smithy-eventstream = \"=0.60.18\"\naws-smithy-async = \"1\"\n# File store (docs/file-storage.md): S3-compatible backend. Same SDK generation as\n# Bedrock (shared smithy/runtime crates); request checksums \"when_required\" for MinIO/RustFS.\naws-sdk-s3 = { version = \"=1.122.0\", default-features = false, features = [\"rt-tokio\"] }\n# Mandatory streaming AES-256-GCM encryption of every stored object.\naes-gcm = { version = \"=0.11.1\", features = [\"zeroize\"] }\nbase64 = \"0.23\"\nbytes = \"1\"\nfutures = \"0.3\"\nlibc = \"0.2\"\nzeroize = \"1\"\nhttp-body = \"1\"\nhttp-body-util = \"0.1\"\nchrono = { version = \"0.4\", features = [\"serde\"] }\nasync-stream = \"0.3\"\nfutures-util = \"0.3\"\naxum = { version = \"0.8\", features = [\"ws\"] }\nclap = { version = \"4\", features = [\"derive\", \"env\"] }\ndotenvy = \"0.15\"\nhex = \"0.4\"\n# Batch scheduling time windows (IANA zones, DST): the bundled tz database, so\n# window evaluation never depends on the host's zoneinfo.\njiff = { version = \"=0.2.38\", default-features = false, features = [\"std\", \"tzdb-bundle-always\"] }\n# SMTP delivery (invitations, alerts): rustls, async, one connection per message (no pool).\nlettre = { version = \"=0.11.23\", default-features = false, features = [\"builder\", \"smtp-transport\", \"tokio1-rustls-tls\"] }\npercent-encoding = \"2\"\n# The already-locked reqwest transport's typed EOF/closed-connection errors;\n# never classify webhook retryability by attacker-controlled error text.\nhyper = { version = \"=1.11.1\", default-features = false }\n# Prometheus/OpenMetrics exposition on the optional GATEWAY_METRICS_ADDR listener.\nprometheus-client = \"=0.25.1\"\n# Secrets come only from the OS CSPRNG (rand::rngs::SysRng, fallible; src/entropy.rs).\n# No ThreadRng (it panics when the OS RNG fails); seeded StdRng only for routing's\n# deterministic, non-secret weighted choice.\nrand = { version = \"0.10\", default-features = false, features = [\"std\", \"sys_rng\", \"std_rng\"] }\nreqwest = { version = \"0.12\", default-features = false, features = [\"json\", \"stream\", \"rustls-tls\"] }\nserde = { version = \"1\", features = [\"derive\"] }\nserde_json = { version = \"1\", features = [\"raw_value\"] }\nsha2 = \"0.11\"\nsubtle = \"2\"\nsqlx = { version = \"0.8\", default-features = false, features = [\"runtime-tokio\", \"tls-rustls-ring\", \"postgres\", \"uuid\", \"migrate\", \"macros\", \"json\", \"chrono\"] }\ntokio = { version = \"1\", features = [\"macros\", \"rt-multi-thread\", \"net\", \"signal\", \"time\", \"sync\", \"process\", \"fs\", \"io-util\"] }\n# Realtime upstream WebSocket (same tungstenite as axum's `ws`); TLS uses an explicit\n# ring-backed rustls config with webpki roots; no proxies, redirects or retries.\ntokio-tungstenite = { version = \"=0.29.0\", default-features = false, features = [\"connect\", \"handshake\", \"rustls-tls-webpki-roots\"] }\nrustls = { version = \"=0.23.46\", default-features = false, features = [\"ring\", \"std\", \"tls12\"] }\nwebpki-roots = \"=1.0.10\"\n# Audit export over syslog/TLS (#107): the same ring-backed rustls, no new TLS stack.\ntokio-rustls = { version = \"=0.26.6\", default-features = false, features = [\"ring\", \"tls12\"] }\ntower = { version = \"0.5\", features = [\"util\"] }\ntower-http = { version = \"0.7\", features = [\"limit\", \"fs\"] }\ntracing = \"0.1\"\ntracing-subscriber = { version = \"0.3\", features = [\"env-filter\", \"json\"] }\nuuid = { version = \"1\", features = [\"v4\", \"v7\", \"serde\"] }\n\n[dev-dependencies]\n# Offline OpenAPI 3.1/Draft 2020-12 gates only (#81); no fetchers or schema-generator macros.\njsonschema = { version = \"=0.60.1\", default-features = false }\n# Deterministic paused-time coverage of the metrics worker's missed ticks.\ntokio = { version = \"1\", features = [\"test-util\"] }\nproc-macro2 = \"1\"\n# Parse source inventories for route declarations and the configuration surface.\nsyn = { version = \"3\", features = [\"full\", \"visit\"] }\ntempfile = \"3\"\nproptest = { version = \"=1.12.0\", default-features = false, features = [\"std\"] }\n"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":1,"timestamp":1793209997220},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:17.221Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_f438ca3935f442f586bd0f17e2d1b79b|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236949438819db1a13283f7f9af36","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"import assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport OpenAI, { toFile } from \"openai\";\nimport Anthropic from \"@anthropic-ai/sdk\";\n\nconst base = process.env.SDK_TEST_BASE_URL;\nconst key = process.env.SDK_TEST_API_KEY;\nassert.equal(new URL(base).hostname, \"127.0.0.1\", \"SDK contracts must use loopback fixtures\");\nconst openai = new OpenAI({ apiKey: key, baseURL: `${base}/v1`, maxRetries: 0 });\n// Omission-positive compatibility cases use the official client's ordinary\n// retry defaults, separately from the legacy capability/refusal fixtures.\nconst ordinaryOpenai = new OpenAI({ apiKey: key, baseURL: `${base}/v1` });\nconst anthropic = new Anthropic({ apiKey: key, baseURL: base, maxRetries: 0 });\nconst ordinaryAnthropic = new Anthropic({ apiKey: key, baseURL: base });\nconst messages = [{ role: \"user\", content: \"hello\" }];\nconst chat = await ordinaryOpenai.chat.completions.create({ model: \"company/smart\", messages });\nassert.equal(chat.choices[0].message.content, \"Hello from fixture\");\nlet text = \"\";\nfor await (const event of await ordinaryOpenai.chat.completions.create({ model: \"company/smart\", messages, stream: true })) {\n  text += event.choices[0]?.delta?.content ?? \"\";\n}\nassert.equal(text, \"Hello from fixture\");\n// Legacy `max_tokens` (still sent by many OpenAI-compatible clients) is an alias of max_completion_tokens.\nconst legacy = await openai.chat.completions.create({ model: \"company/smart\", messages, max_tokens: 7 });\nassert.equal(legacy.choices[0].message.content, \"Hello from fixture (max 7)\");\nconst both = await openai.chat.completions.create({ model: \"company/smart\", messages, max_tokens: 7, max_completion_tokens: 7 });\nassert.equal(both.choices[0].message.content, \"Hello from fixture (max 7)\");\nassert.equal((await openai.chat.completions.create({ model: \"company/smart\", messages, max_completion_tokens: 7 })).choices[0].message.content, \"Hello from fixture (max 7)\");\nawait assert.rejects(\n  openai.chat.completions.create({ model: \"company/smart\", messages, max_tokens: 7, max_completion_tokens: 8 }),\n  (e) => e.status === 400 && e.code === \"invalid_request_error\",\n);\nlet legacyText = \"\";\nfor await (const event of await openai.chat.completions.create({ model: \"company/smart\", messages, max_tokens: 7, stream: true })) {\n  legacyText += event.choices[0]?.delta?.content ?? \"\";\n}\nassert.equal(legacyText, \"Hello from fixture (max 7)\");\n// Responses keeps its own max_output_tokens.\nconst capped = await openai.responses.create({ model: \"company/smart\", input: \"hello\", store: false, max_output_tokens: 7 });\nassert.equal(capped.output_text, \"Hello from fixture (max 7)\");\nconst response = await ordinaryOpenai.responses.create({ model: \"company/smart\", input: \"hello\" });\nassert.equal(response.status, \"completed\");\nassert.equal(response.output_text, \"Hello from fixture\");\nconst responseStream = ordinaryOpenai.responses.stream({ model: \"company/smart\", input: \"hello\" });\nconst finalResponse = await responseStream.finalResponse();\nassert.equal(finalResponse.status, \"completed\");\nassert.equal(finalResponse.output_text, \"Hello from fixture\");\nlet responseText = \"\";\nlet terminalTextResponse;\nfor await (const event of await ordinaryOpenai.responses.create({ model: \"company/smart\", input: \"hello\", stream: true })) {\n  if (event.type === \"response.output_text.delta\") responseText += event.delta;\n  if (event.type === \"response.completed\") terminalTextResponse = event.response;\n}\nassert.equal(responseText, \"Hello from fixture\");\nassert.equal(terminalTextResponse.status, \"completed\");\nasync function terminalWireResponse(args) {\n  let terminal;\n  for await (const event of await ordinaryOpenai.responses.create({ ...args, stream: true })) {\n    if (event.type === \"response.completed\" || event.type === \"response.incomplete\") terminal = event.response;\n  }\n  assert.ok(terminal, \"stream must provide a terminal wire response\");\n  return terminal;\n}\n// Stateless followups reuse the gateway's actual output, including read-only\n// item metadata and empty annotations. No store/max/previous_response_id workaround.\n// Use the terminal event, not finalResponse()'s SDK-only parsed/parsed_arguments fields.\nfor (const [prior, streaming] of [[response, false], [terminalTextResponse, true]]) {\n  const args = {\n    model: \"company/smart\",\n    input: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"hello\" }, ...prior.output, { role: \"user\", content: \"continue\" }],\n  };\n  const followup = streaming\n    ? await ordinaryOpenai.responses.stream(args).finalResponse()\n    : await ordinaryOpenai.responses.create(args);\n  assert.equal(followup.output_text, \"Hello from fixture\");\n}\nfor (const streaming of [false, true]) {\n  const args = { model: \"company/smart\", input: \"call weather\" };\n  const prior = streaming\n    ? await terminalWireResponse(args)\n    : await ordinaryOpenai.responses.create(args);\n  assert.equal(prior.output[0].content[0].text, \"Checking weather\");\n  const call = prior.output[1];\n  assert.equal(call.type, \"function_call\");\n  assert.equal(call.call_id, \"semantic-call\");\n  assert.notEqual(call.id, call.call_id);\n  assert.equal(call.arguments, '{ \"city\": \"東京\" }');\n  const followupArgs = {\n    model: \"company/smart\",\n    input: [\n      { role: \"user\", content: \"call weather\" }, ...prior.output,\n      { type: \"function_call_output\", call_id: call.call_id, output: \"sun\", id: null, status: null },\n    ],\n  };\n  const followup = streaming\n    ? await ordinaryOpenai.responses.stream(followupArgs).finalResponse()\n    : await ordinaryOpenai.responses.create(followupArgs);\n  assert.equal(followup.output_text, \"Tool result received\");\n}\n// Negative controls still fail before any reservation or provider dispatch.\nfor (const input of [\n  [{ ...response.output[0], id: null }],\n  [{ ...response.output[0], status: null }],\n  [{ ...response.output[0], status: \"failed\" }],\n  [{ ...response.output[0], extra: true }],\n  [{ ...response.output[0], content: [{ type: \"output_text\", text: \"hi\", parsed: null }] }],\n  [{ type: \"function_call\", call_id: \"c\", name: \"weather\", arguments: \"{}\", parsed_arguments: null }],\n  [{ ...response.output[0], content: null }],\n  [{ ...response.output[0], content: [{ type: \"output_text\", text: \"hi\", annotations: null }] }],\n  [{ ...response.output[0], content: [{ type: \"output_text\", text: \"hi\", annotations: [{ type: \"url_citation\", url: \"https://example.invalid\", title: \"citation\", start_index: 0, end_index: 1 }] }] }],\n  [{ role: \"user\", content: [{ type: \"output_text\", text: \"hi\", annotations: [] }] }],\n\n[175 more lines in file. Use offset=111 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":4,"timestamp":1793210004655},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:24.657Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_9eda7b94e66a4f1dba1ce83f82a40fa9|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236949438819d9bd51795fa73029a","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# Protocol support\n\nCurrent inference routes share key authentication, global public model aliases, live workspace grants, key restrictions, routing and per-attempt accounting. Support means the bounded protocol subsets below (including native Anthropic Messages images/thinking), **not universal SDK/vendor option compatibility**. Model `supported_protocols` and request-specific profile checks may narrow each row further.\n\n| Adapter | Chat `/v1/chat/completions` | Responses `/v1/responses` | Messages `/v1/messages` | Embeddings `/v1/embeddings` | Rerank `/v1/rerank` | System One `/v1/systemone` |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| `openai` | Native Chat | Native Responses | Translated via Chat | String input; client float/base64 | No | No |\n| `anthropic` | Representable subset via Messages | No | Native Messages | No | No | No |\n| `bedrock` | Text/tools via Converse | No | Text/tools via Converse | No | No | No |\n| `openai_compatible` | Narrow compatible profile | No | Translated via Chat | OpenAI-wire subset; no dimensions | Jina/Cohere `/v1/rerank` | TypeSafe `/v1/systemone` |\n| `vllm` | Profile-specific compatible subset | No | Translated via Chat | OpenAI-wire subset, bounded dimensions | Jina/Cohere `/rerank` | No |\n| `sglang` | Profile-specific compatible subset | No | Translated via Chat | OpenAI-wire subset, bounded dimensions | Native `/v1/rerank`, mapped | No |\n| `ollama` | Compatible Chat subset | No | Translated via Chat | Native `/api/embed`, no dimensions | No | `/v1/systemone` (v0.35.0+) |\n| `openrouter` | Compatible Chat subset (stream/non-stream) | No | Translated via Chat | String input; client float/base64; fixed native widths only | Native `/rerank` | Native `/systemone` |\n\n### Speech routes\n\n| Adapter | Transcriptions `/v1/audio/transcriptions` | Speech `/v1/audio/speech` |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `openai` | Native multipart (gateway re-encoded) | Native, `mp3`/`wav`/`opus`/`pcm` |\n| `openrouter` | JSON base64 `input_audio`; no `prompt` | Native, `mp3`/`pcm` only |\n| Others | No | No |\n\n### Realtime\n\n| Adapter | Realtime `GET /v1/realtime` (WebSocket) |\n| --- | --- |\n| `openai` | GA interface over `wss://api.openai.com/v1/realtime`, bounded event allowlist |\n| Others | No |\n\nSee [realtime](#realtime-audio) below and [realtime](realtime.md).\n\n### Async jobs\n\n| Adapter | Videos `/v1/videos` | Native batches (`/v1/batches`) |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `openai` | **No longer offered.** OpenAI shut down the Sora 2 models and the Videos API on 2026-09-24. The `sora-*` wire code remains only for jobs created before then | OpenAI Batch: chat-like lines as Chat Completions, embeddings as Embeddings; the gateway uploads its copy, collects results into gateway files and deletes the provider's files |\n| `anthropic` | No | Message Batches for Chat Completions, Responses and Messages lines |\n| `openrouter` | Planned: an OpenRouter video adapter (another API shape) | No (no Batch API): gateway-run |\n| Others | No | No: gateway-run |\n\nAny model can be batched: batches without a native path run line by line through the gateway ([batches](batches.md)). Files are always gateway files ([Files API](files-api.md)).\n\nSee [async jobs](#async-jobs-video-and-batch) below and [async jobs](async-jobs.md).\n\nThis table is one source of truth in the gateway (`providers::capabilities`): every adapter's `supports_protocol`, the pre-admission `unsupported_capability` check, route validation (`400 route_unsupported_capability` when a route's connection serves none of its model's protocols, see [management API](management-api.md#infrastructure-and-catalogs)), readiness and the Add model form all read it.\n\nA model's protocols belong to one workload: Chat/Responses/Messages combine, while embeddings and each other kind stand alone. Images, audio, realtime, video, batch, Rerank and System One routes return **400 `unsupported_capability`** when the model exists but has no deployment that declares the protocol on an adapter supporting it. Chat/Responses/Embeddings keep 501 for unsupported capabilities. Messages uses Anthropic-shaped 400 refusals.\n\nPermanent `unsupported_capability` refusals carry `x-should-retry: false`, honored by the official OpenAI and Anthropic SDKs even on HTTP 501. Statuses and protocol-specific error bodies are unchanged. Transient rate/concurrency failures, upstream 502/504 errors and cooling-down 503s are not opted out of SDK retries; cooling-down responses retain `Retry-After`. An SDK retry is a new gateway HTTP request with its own admission and durable reservation if dispatched; unknown usage retains its hold. This is separate from the gateway's [explicit failover policy](routing.md): the gateway does not implicitly retry an upstream request or fail over after returning a stream.\n\nUnsupported combinations fail explicitly. Local labels do not mean every installed server/model supports all fields. Ollama native embedding follow-up has source and mocked tests, but this documentation refresh does not claim a fresh pass or live-server certification; consult [local profile notes](../apps/gateway/src/providers/local/README.md). [Bedrock](bedrock.md) documents its separate transport.\n\n## Client labels (Logs sessions and apps)\n\nEvery inference route accepts two optional, untrusted labels used only to group and display requests in Logs ([management API](management-api.md#logs)):\n\n- **Session id**: the `X-Session-Id` header; otherwise, in the body, OpenAI `metadata.session_id` then `user` (Chat Completions, Responses) or Anthropic `metadata.user_id` (Messages). Embeddings and the other workloads take the header only.\n- **App name**: the `X-Title` header.\n\nA label is recorded only when it is 1–128 (session) or 1–200 (app) characters, valid UTF-8, without control characters or leading/trailing whitespace, and sent once; anything else is silently not recorded and never fails the request. Labels are metadata, not authorization or attribution: they are stored on each attempt of the root request, shown to whoever may see that request, cleared by execution-detail retention, and are not authorization input. They are never forwarded upstream, including Messages `metadata.user_id`. Other protocols' `user` and `metadata` are not sent to providers (they do not change generation); OpenAI `metadata` keeps its documented bounds (at most 16 pairs, keys 1–64 and values up to 512 characters, else 400) and is not stored beyond `session_id`. Anthropic `metadata` accepts only `user_id` (at most 256 characters). Treat a session id that may identify an end user (such as `user`) as visible to workspace administrators and, for Team/Project workspaces, to Platform Admins and Auditors.\n\n## Chat Completions\n\nOne choice (`n` absent or 1); text-string messages with system/developer/user/assistant/tool roles; function tools and tool-call/results; `temperature`, `max_completion_tokens` (or its legacy alias `max_tokens`), streaming and optional `stream_options.include_usage`; `user` and `metadata` as [client labels](#client-labels-logs-sessions-and-apps) only. Multimodal arrays, structured output, reasoning, log probabilities and arbitrary extensions are rejected.\n\n**Nullable defaults.** Omitted or null `stream` means non-streaming; explicit `false`/`true` is unchanged. Existing nullable supported options (`temperature`, maxima, `n`, `stream_options`, `metadata`) also treat null as omission. This is field-specific: `tools`, message `tool_calls` and `stream_options.include_usage` are not nullable in OpenAI SDK 7.28 and remain strict. Assistant message `refusal:null` is public no-refusal metadata and is accepted without being forwarded; non-null refusal content remains unsupported, and null does not make an otherwise empty message valid. Other roles cannot send `refusal`, even null. Unknown fields such as `parsed:null` and unsupported options remain rejected even when null.\n\n**No-argument functions.** Omitting a Chat function's `parameters` defines an empty parameter list (OpenAI SDK contract). The gateway canonicalizes only omission to `{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{},\"additionalProperties\":false}`: only the empty object satisfies this schema, unlike the unconstrained schema `{}`. Explicit object schemas pass through unchanged; explicit null/nonobject parameters and unknown function fields remain invalid. Omitted `strict` stays absent, not `true`; explicit strict values retain each adapter's existing capability rules. This is schema normalization, not a new runtime argument validator or strict-generation guarantee.\n\n**Output maximum.** `max_tokens` (still sent by many OpenAI-compatible clients and older SDK code) is accepted as an alias of `max_completion_tokens`. Sending both is accepted only when they are equal; different values (or 0) are 400 `invalid_request_error`. The one value is the admission reservation's output bound and the upstream maximum: OpenAI and OpenRouter receive `max_completion_tokens`; local profiles (`openai_compatible`, `vllm`, `sglang`, `ollama`) receive `max_tokens`; Anthropic receives Messages `max_tokens` and Bedrock Converse `maxTokens`. Batch lines follow the same rule. Responses keeps `max_output_tokens` and rejects both Chat names.\n\nLocal transports translate the accepted maximum to upstream `max_tokens`. All local profiles reject strict function guarantees. Ollama rejects explicit tool choice; generic compatible rejects required/named choice. Anthropic Chat accepts leading system/developer text but rejects strict schemas, temperature above 1 and non-object tool arguments. Chat Completions and Responses maxima are optional, including for priced generation. When omitted, admission pins the selected price's positive output ceiling as both the reservation bound and the maximum sent upstream. Each failover attempt resolves its own price and cap. Invalid explicit maxima return 400 without dispatch; an unusable server default returns 503. Native Messages still requires `max_tokens`.\n\n```json\n{\"model\":\"company/smart\",\"messages\":[{\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":\"Hello\"}],\"max_completion_tokens\":32,\"stream\":false}\n```\n\n## Responses\n\nStateless native OpenAI text/function subset: `model`, string or nonempty item-list `input`, `instructions`, `max_output_tokens`, `temperature`, `stream`, `store:false`, flat function `tools`, supported `tool_choice`, and `text:{\"format\":{\"type\":\"text\"}}`; `user` and `metadata` as [client labels](#client-labels-logs-sessions-and-apps) only.\n\nMessage items support user/assistant/system/developer and text/input-text (assistant output-text also accepted). Function calls use `call_id,name,arguments` (string); results use `call_id,output` (string). Supported message/function-call items accept optional string `id` and `status` (`in_progress`, `completed`, `incomplete`); function-call outputs also accept explicit null for these two metadata fields. IDs/statuses are validated read-only metadata, never server-side lookups or authorization. Tool correlation uses `call_id`, not the item `id`. Assistant output-text accepts omitted or empty `annotations`; nonempty annotations/citations are explicitly rejected. This lets clients reuse JSON `output` or a terminal stream event's `response.output` in stateless followups without adding a maximum or `store:false`. SDK-only parsing extensions (such as `parsed`/`parsed_arguments`) are not wire fields and remain unsupported. Tools are flat Responses definitions, not nested Chat envelopes. OpenAI strict function schemas can be forwarded where supported.\n\nOmitted or null `stream` means non-streaming. Omitted, null or false `store` means stateless operation under the gateway's privacy subset, not OpenAI's provider-side storage default; `store:true` is refused before admission. Existing nullable supported options (`instructions`, `max_output_tokens`, `temperature`, `metadata`) treat null as omission. `tools` is optional but not nullable in OpenAI SDK 7.28; `tools:null`, malformed values and unknown/unsupported fields with null remain rejected. This does not enable hosted tools, reasoning, structured output or stateful followups.\n\n[208 more lines in file. Use offset=86 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":4,"timestamp":1793210004655},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:24.657Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_acb10bcd4b7741b19e8643bd06352b70|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236949438819dbf93a2c229fd390f","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! #102: browser-only configuration and live-authorized occurrence reads.\nuse super::*;\n#[path = \"scheduled_report_csv.rs\"]\nmod csv;\n\npub(super) fn routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new()\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}/runs\",\n            get(platform_runs),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}/runs\",\n            get(workspace_runs),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}/csv\",\n            get(platform_csv),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}/csv\",\n            get(workspace_csv),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules\",\n            get(platform_list).post(platform_create),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}\",\n            get(platform_run),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}/runs/{run}\",\n            get(workspace_run),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/report-schedules/{id}\",\n            get(platform_get)\n                .patch(platform_update)\n                .delete(platform_delete),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules\",\n            get(workspace_list).post(workspace_create),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/report-schedules/{id}\",\n            get(workspace_get)\n                .patch(workspace_update)\n                .delete(workspace_delete),\n        )\n}\n\n/// Unlike legacy metadata boundaries, foreign personal scopes are indistinguishable\n/// from absent workspaces (#282). Used by both middleware and handlers.\npub(super) async fn workspace_authority(\n    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,\n    u: &impl Actor,\n    ws: Uuid,\n) -> Result<String, ApiError> {\n    resources::platform_role_snapshot(tx, u.user_id()).await?;\n    let (kind, owner): (String, Option<Uuid>) = sqlx::query_as(\n        \"SELECT kind,owner_user_id FROM workspaces WHERE id=$1 AND disabled_at IS NULL AND (kind<>'personal' OR owner_user_id=$2)\",\n    ).bind(ws).bind(u.user_id()).fetch_optional(&mut **tx).await?.ok_or_else(missing)?;\n    if kind == \"personal\" && owner == Some(u.user_id()) {\n        return Ok(kind);\n    }\n    let admin: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(\"SELECT EXISTS(SELECT FROM effective_workspace_memberships WHERE workspace_id=$1 AND user_id=$2 AND role IN ('owner','admin'))\")\n        .bind(ws).bind(u.user_id()).fetch_one(&mut **tx).await?;\n    if !shared(&kind) || !admin {\n        return Err(denied());\n    }\n    Ok(\"workspace\".into())\n}\nasync fn authority(\n    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,\n    u: &BrowserPrincipal,\n    ws: Option<Uuid>,\n    write: bool,\n) -> Result<String, ApiError> {\n    if let Some(ws) = ws {\n        return workspace_authority(tx, u, ws).await;\n    }\n    let role = resources::platform_role_snapshot(tx, u.user_id()).await?;\n    if role != \"admin\" && (write || role != \"auditor\") {\n        return Err(denied());\n    }\n    Ok(\"installation\".into())\n}\n/// A single canonical acquisition, before any write/row lock. Shared authority\n/// locks intersect the exclusive revocation/suspension/disable locks, as #100.\nasync fn lock_authority(\n    tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>,\n    u: &BrowserPrincipal,\n    ws: Option<Uuid>,\n    recipients: &[Uuid],\n) -> Result<(), ApiError> {\n    let scopes = ws\n        .map(locks::Scope::Workspace)\n        .into_iter()\n        .chain(std::iter::once(locks::Scope::User(u.user_id)))\n        .chain(recipients.iter().copied().map(locks::Scope::User));\n    locks::shared(tx, scopes).await?;\n    authority(tx, u, ws, true).await?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\nstruct Create {\n    cadence: String,\n    timezone: String,\n    delivery_time: String,\n    #[serde(default = \"paused\")]\n    state: String,\n    cost_center_id: Option<Uuid>,\n    recipient_user_ids: Vec<Uuid>,\n}\nfn paused() -> String {\n    \"paused\".into()\n}\nfn present<'de, D: serde::Deserializer<'de>, T: Deserialize<'de>>(\n    d: D,\n) -> Result<Option<T>, D::Error> {\n    T::deserialize(d).map(Some)\n}\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\nstruct Update {\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"present\")]\n    cadence: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"present\")]\n    timezone: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"present\")]\n    delivery_time: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"present\")]\n    state: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = \"present\")]\n    recipient_user_ids: Option<Vec<Uuid>>,\n}\nfn validate(\n    cadence: &str,\n    timezone: &str,\n    time: &str,\n    state: &str,\n    recipients: &[Uuid],\n    config: Option<&settings::SignIn>,\n) -> Result<(), ApiError> {\n    if !matches!(cadence, \"weekly\" | \"monthly\")\n        || !matches!(state, \"active\" | \"paused\")\n\n[488 more lines in file. Use offset=151 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":2,"timestamp":1793210004655},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:24.657Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_2825409313de414ba95ca7977ced8b16|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236949438819da7fec5141af7889a","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! #83 slice A: browser-only, independent approval of manual member grants.\n//! All writes use the existing management transaction and canonical locks.\nuse super::*;\nuse sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\n\ntype RequestResult = Result<Json<Value>, ManagementError>;\n/// Common fields for every viewer: the requester's own `member`/`admin` and the two approver\n/// queues. `workspace_name_at_request` is a frozen submitted reference (UX fix, #83): it is\n/// captured once at INSERT and never updated, so it never discloses a later rename or a\n/// workspace becoming non-discoverable. It is not the live workspace name.\nconst PROJECTION: &str = \"jsonb_build_object('id',r.id,'requester',r.requester,'target_kind',r.target_kind,'workspace_id',r.workspace_id,'workspace_name_at_request',r.workspace_name_at_request,'requested_role',r.requested_role,'status',r.status,'reason',r.reason,'created_at',r.created_at,'expires_at',r.expires_at,'decided_by',r.decided_by,'decided_at',r.decided_at,'membership_grant_id',(SELECT membership_grant_id FROM access_request_applications a WHERE a.request_id=r.id))\";\n/// Approver-only: adds the requester's display name/email (requires a `JOIN users ru ON\n/// ru.id=r.requester` in the FROM clause). Workspace owners/admins already see this pair for\n/// any entitled user through the Members \"Add member\" picker (`members::candidates`), and\n/// Platform Admins/Auditors through `GET /platform/users`; this projection discloses nothing\n/// beyond what those existing, equally scoped reads already show the same viewers. It is used\n/// only for the workspace- and platform-scoped queues, never for the requester's own `mine`/\n/// `detail` reads, so a non-approver never receives another requester's identity.\n///\n/// The `CASE WHEN` guard below mirrors `members::candidates`' own WHERE clause exactly\n/// (disabled, cleaned, no email, or no current platform entitlement): a requester outside\n/// that picker's reach gets both fields redacted to `NULL` here too, so this projection never\n/// discloses more than that picker actually would for the requester's *current* state, not\n/// just their state at submission.\nconst PROJECTION_WITH_REQUESTER: &str = \"jsonb_build_object('id',r.id,'requester',r.requester,'requester_display_name',CASE WHEN ru.disabled_at IS NULL AND ru.cleaned_at IS NULL AND ru.email IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM effective_platform_roles er WHERE er.user_id=ru.id) THEN ru.display_name END,'requester_email',CASE WHEN ru.disabled_at IS NULL AND ru.cleaned_at IS NULL AND ru.email IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM effective_platform_roles er WHERE er.user_id=ru.id) THEN ru.email END,'target_kind',r.target_kind,'workspace_id',r.workspace_id,'workspace_name_at_request',r.workspace_name_at_request,'requested_role',r.requested_role,'status',r.status,'reason',r.reason,'created_at',r.created_at,'expires_at',r.expires_at,'decided_by',r.decided_by,'decided_at',r.decided_at,'membership_grant_id',(SELECT membership_grant_id FROM access_request_applications a WHERE a.request_id=r.id))\";\nfn conflict() -> ApiError {\n    ApiError(StatusCode::CONFLICT, \"Request is no longer applicable\")\n}\n\npub(super) fn routes() -> Router<Store> {\n    Router::new()\n        .route(\"/api/v1/access-request-targets\", get(targets))\n        .route(\"/api/v1/me/access-requests\", get(mine).post(create))\n        .route(\"/api/v1/me/access-requests/{id}\", get(detail))\n        .route(\"/api/v1/me/access-requests/{id}/withdraw\", post(withdraw))\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/access-requests\",\n            get(workspace_queue),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/access-requests/{id}/approve\",\n            post(workspace_approve),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/workspaces/{ws}/access-requests/{id}/deny\",\n            post(workspace_deny),\n        )\n        .route(\"/api/v1/platform/access-requests\", get(platform_queue))\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/access-requests/{id}/approve\",\n            post(platform_approve),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/access-requests/{id}/deny\",\n            post(platform_deny),\n        )\n        .route(\n            \"/api/v1/platform/workspaces/{ws}/discoverable\",\n            axum::routing::put(set_discoverable),\n        )\n}\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\nstruct Submission {\n    workspace_id: Uuid,\n    reason: Option<String>,\n    idempotency_key: String,\n}\n#[derive(Deserialize)]\n#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]\n\n[407 more lines in file. Use offset=71 to continue.]"}],"isError":false,"durationMs":2,"timestamp":1793210004655},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:24.657Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}

Personal details are owner-only; platform totals do not grant personal detail access. Shared members see their own activity unless an administrator or the object's visibility permits more; ownership checks can hide objects with 404. Auditor platform authority is read-only, independent User/membership entitlements remain usable. Credential families are exclusive, duplicate Authorization headers are refused, inference keys never authorize management, undeclared routes fail closed, and write scopes do not imply read scopes. BrowserAdminOnly includes Platform Admin even on GET. HEAD implicitly follows GET unless separately declared; no synthetic HEAD operations are listed.
