0001,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0.019079600000000002}},"stopReason":"toolUse","timestamp":1793210014351,"responseId":"resp_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae2369e9e30819d87787e846cd4079f","rawStopReason":"completed","durationMs":21978,"thinkingLevel":"high"},"timestamp":"2026-10-28T17:53:56.336Z","cwd":"/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"89dec0a1-8a4b-450","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_920a2ed7d70c4860befc3578ce6ecc99|fc_03c3c85627d3ad03016ae236b3f8d0819dae7e6101be14ef10","toolName":"read","content":[{"type":"text","text":"//! Mechanical inventory of production route registrations, independent of the policy table.\nuse std::{collections::BTreeSet, path::Path};\nuse syn::{\n    Expr, Lit,\n    visit::{self, Visit},\n};\n\ntype Inventory = BTreeSet<(String, String)>;\n\n// Evaluate only what is known in a production build (test=false). Unknown\n// feature/target predicates are included, so optional production routes count.\nfn production_cfg(meta: &syn::Meta) -> Option<bool> {\n    match meta {\n        syn::Meta::Path(p) if p.is_ident(\"test\") => Some(false),\n        syn::Meta::List(list) => {\n            let args = list\n                .parse_args_with(\n                    syn::punctuated::Punctuated::<syn::Meta, syn::Token![,]>::parse_terminated,\n                )\n                .unwrap();\n            let values: Vec<_> = args.iter().map(production_cfg).collect();\n            if list.path.is_ident(\"not\") && values.len() == 1 {\n                values[0].map(|v| !v)\n            } else if list.path.is_ident(\"all\") {\n                if values.contains(&Some(false)) {\n                    Some(false)\n                } else if values.iter().all(|v| *v == Some(true)) {\n                    Some(true)\n                } else {\n                    None\n                }\n            } else if list.path.is_ident(\"any\") {\n                if values.contains(&Some(true)) {\n                    Some(true)\n                } else if values.iter().all(|v| *v == Some(false)) {\n                    Some(false)\n                } else {\n                    None\n                }\n            } else {\n                None\n            }\n        }\n        _ => None,\n    }\n}\nfn test_only(attrs: &[syn::Attribute]) -> bool {\n    attrs.iter().any(|a| {\n        a.path().is_ident(\"cfg\")\n            && a.parse_args::<syn::Meta>()\n                .is_ok_and(|m| production_cfg(&m) == Some(false))\n    })\n}\n\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct Scanner {\n    prefix: String,\n    routes: Inventory,\n}\nfn literal(expr: &Expr) -> String {\n    match expr {\n        Expr::Lit(l) => match &l.lit {\n            Lit::Str(s) => s.value(),\n            _ => panic!(\"route path must be a string\"),\n        },\n        // The public logo is shared with its response metadata.\n        Expr::Path(p) if p.path.is_ident(\"LOGO_PATH\") => {\n            crate::management::branding::LOGO_PATH.to_owned()\n        }\n        _ => {\n            panic!(\"unresolved route path: declare a literal or teach the inventory to resolve it\")\n        }\n    }\n}\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct Methods(BTreeSet<String>);\nimpl Methods {\n    fn add(&mut self, name: &str) {\n        match name {\n            \"get\" | \"post\" | \"put\" | \"patch\" | \"delete\" | \"head\" | \"options\" | \"trace\"\n            | \"connect\" => {\n                self.0.insert(name.to_uppercase());\n            }\n            \"any\" => {\n                self.0.insert(\"*\".to_owned());\n            }\n            \"on\" | \"on_service\" | \"any_service\" => panic!(\"unresolved method registration\"),\n            _ => (),\n        }\n    }\n}\nimpl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for Methods {\n    fn visit_expr_call(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ExprCall) {\n        if let Expr::Path(p) = &*node.func {\n            self.add(&p.path.segments.last().unwrap().ident.to_string());\n        }\n        visit::visit_expr_call(self, node);\n    }\n    fn visit_expr_method_call(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ExprMethodCall) {\n        self.add(&node.method.to_string());\n        visit::visit_expr_method_call(self, node);\n    }\n}\nimpl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for Scanner {\n    fn visit_item(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::Item) {\n        let attrs = match node {\n            syn::Item::Mod(i) => &i.attrs,\n            syn::Item::Fn(i) => &i.attrs,\n            syn::Item::Impl(i) => &i.attrs,\n            _ => {\n                visit::visit_item(self, node);\n                return;\n            }\n        };\n        if !test_only(attrs) {\n            visit::visit_item(self, node);\n        }\n    }\n    fn visit_macro(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::Macro) {\n        let tokens = node.tokens.to_string();\n        assert!(\n            !tokens.contains(\". route (\")\n                && !tokens.contains(\". nest (\")\n                && !tokens.contains(\". route_service (\")\n                && !tokens.contains(\". nest_service (\"),\n            \"router registrations inside macros need inventory support\"\n        );\n    }\n    fn visit_expr_method_call(&mut self, node: &'ast syn::ExprMethodCall) {\n        if node.method == \"nest\" {\n            self.visit_expr(&node.receiver);\n            let prefix = literal(&node.args[0]);\n            let old = self.prefix.clone();\n            self.prefix.push_str(prefix.trim_end_matches('/'));\n            // Inline nesting is resolved. Named routers must not silently lose the prefix.\n            assert!(\n                !matches!(node.args[1], Expr::Call(_)),\n                \"resolve named nested router in inventory\"\n            );\n            let count = self.routes.len();\n            self.visit_expr(&node.args[1]);\n            assert!(\n                self.routes.len() > count,\n                \"unresolved nested router in inventory\"\n            );\n            self.prefix = old;\n            return;\n        }\n        assert!(\n            node.method != \"route_service\" && node.method != \"nest_service\",\n            \"service routes need an explicit inventory implementation\"\n        );\n        if node.method == \"route\" {\n            let path = format!(\"{}{}\", self.prefix, literal(&node.args[0]));\n            let mut methods = Methods::default();\n            methods.visit_expr(&node.args[1]);\n            assert!(!methods.0.is_empty(), \"unresolved method router for {path}\");\n            for method in methods.0 {\n                self.routes.insert((method, path.clone()));\n            }\n        }\n        visit::visit_expr_method_call(self, node);\n    }\n}\nfn scan(dir: &Path, scanner: &mut Scanner) {\n    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir).unwrap() {\n        let path = entry.unwrap().path();\n        if path.is_dir() {\n            scan(&path, scanner);\n        } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == \"rs\") {\n            let name = path.file_name().unwrap().to_str().unwrap();\n            if name == \"tests.rs\" || name.ends_with(\"_tests.rs\") || name == \"authz_matrix.rs\" {\n                continue;\n            }\n            let source = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();\n            scanner.visit_file(&syn::parse_file(&source).unwrap());\n        }\n    }\n}\npub(crate) fn inventory() -> Inventory {\n    let mut scanner = Scanner::default();\n    scan(\n        &Path::new(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\")).join(\"src\"),\n        &mut scanner,\n    );\n    scanner.routes\n}\n\nfn inventory_difference(actual: &Inventory, declared: &Inventory) -> String {\n    let missing: Vec<_> = actual.difference(declared).collect();\n    let extra: Vec<_> = declared.difference(actual).collect();\n    format!(\n        \"Missing declarations (method, template): {missing:#?}\\nExtra declarations (method, template): {extra:#?}\\nFor each route, declare it in apps/gateway/src/route_policy.rs\"\n    )\n}\n#[test]\nfn inventory_failure_names_missing_extra_and_contributor_action() {\n    let actual = [(\"GET\".to_owned(), \"/new\".to_owned())]\n        .into_iter()\n        .collect();\n    let declared = [(\"POST\".to_owned(), \"/removed\".to_owned())]\n        .into_iter()\n        .collect();\n    let message = inventory_difference(&actual, &declared);\n    for text in [\n        \"Missing declarations\",\n        \"Extra declarations\",\n        \"GET\",\n        \"/new\",\n        \"POST\",\n        \"/removed\",\n        \"declare it in apps/gateway/src/route_policy.rs\",\n    ] {\n        assert!(message.contains(text), \"{message}\");\n    }\n}\n#[test]\nfn every_served_route_has_an_exact_authorization_declaration() {\n    let actual = inventory();\n    let declared: Inventory = crate::route_policy::ROUTES\n        .iter()\n        .flat_map(|r| {\n            r.methods\n                .split_whitespace()\n                .map(move |m| (m.to_owned(), r.path.to_owned()))\n        })\n        .collect();\n    assert!(\n        actual == declared,\n        \"{}\",\n        inventory_difference(&actual, &declared)\n    );\n    let count: usize = crate::route_policy::ROUTES\n        .iter()\n        .map(|r| r.methods.split_whitespace().count())\n        .sum();\n    assert_eq!(\n        count,\n        declared.len(),\n        \"duplicate authorization declarations; {}\",\n        inventory_difference(&actual, &declared)\n    );\n}\n#[test]\nfn removed_installation_policy_requires_platform_authority_per_method() {\n\n[88 more lines in file. review completed; no files changed or Docker/database commands run.\n\n## 1. Gate 4: capture before decoding\n\n**Correction:** neither example fragment completes schemas: access-requests has 12 operation acknowledgements; scheduled-reports has 10; both have empty `schemas` maps ([access-requests.json:1–77](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/docs/openapi/management/access-requests.json#L1), [scheduled-reports.json:1–65](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/docs/openapi/management/scheduled-reports.json#L1)). Thus **502 slots remain**; `ErrorEnvelope` is the sole non-placeholder component ([artifact:4–95](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/docs/openapi/management.openapi.json#L4), [baseline:36–51](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management_openapi/mod.rs#L36)).\n\nCapture `(status, HeaderMap, bytes)` at:\n- Management `call`, before JSON fallback: [tests.rs:210–216](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/tests.rs#L210).\n- Governance `response`/`call`: [tests.rs:75–123](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/governance/tests.rs#L75).\n- Served-router `request`, currently status-only, and direct privacy responses: [authz_matrix_tests.rs:33–60,127–128](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/authz_matrix_tests.rs#L33).\n- Actual loopback HTTP before `.json()`: [authz_matrix_tests.rs:410–428](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/authz_matrix_tests.rs#L410).\n- OIDC redirects/cookies and public logo responses: [identity/tests.rs:1219–1260](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/identity/tests.rs#L1219), [branding_tests.rs:49–67](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/settings/branding_tests.rs#L49).\n\nAdd `responses.rs`: resolve explicit method/template; select documented status, headers and media type; validate JSON against a document-rooted schema; validate text/binary bytes separately. Assert envelope `code == actual_status`; distinguish empty bodies from JSON null; handle implicit HEAD. Reject incomplete referenced slots throughout covered areas.\n\nRun positive fixtures through an **executed per-area registry**; require its observed operation set to equal the completed operation inventory, including nonempty collections, redacted variants, legitimate redirects/empty responses and intentional 410s. Denials cannot count as positives. Preserve privacy assertions.\n\nInitial five-file task: new harness, management module visibility, OpenAPI module registration, management helper, served-router helper. Subsequent tasks: fragment + artifact + baseline + one/two fixture files.\n\n## 2. Gate 2: vectors first, binding still required\n\nRecommend **request vectors first**. Existing syn machinery inventories concrete JSON extractors, not reachable DTO semantics ([configuration_matrix_tests.rs:8–82](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/configuration_matrix_tests.rs#L8)); current cases retain successful controls before top-level unknown-field probes ([same:713–746](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/configuration_matrix_tests.rs#L713)).\n\nAdd nested unknown/missing/wrong-type/null/omission probes, comparing schema acceptance with real deserialization. Then implement bounded recursive DTO binding for ordinary structs/enums/imports, failing unsupported constructs explicitly. Custom `nullable`/`supplied`/`present` require vectors; syntax-only `Option` inference is insufficient ([policies.rs:15–65](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/governance/policies.rs#L15)). Vectors do not waive eventual gate-2 acceptance.\n\n## 3. Delivery order\n\nApproximate allocations partition all 502 slots; each slice comprises several ≤5-file tasks. Route inventory: [route_policy.rs:102–1393](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/route_policy.rs#L102).\n\n| Slice | Slots | Shapes; required DB fixtures |\n|---|---:|---|\n| Harness + auth/branding/invitations/me/targets | 44 | Redirects/cookies, binary logo/304, owner-private reads. Sessions/OIDC, invitation acceptance, populated notifications/models/exports. |\n| Remaining identity/access | 114 | Users/workspaces/members, access approvals, OIDC mappings, tokens, keys/service accounts; requester redaction, one-time secrets. Lifecycle, foreign-personal, Auditor and scope fixtures. |\n| Catalogs/setup | 88 | Providers/models/deployments, catalogs/grants, comparisons, configuration preview/history; dynamic JSON and PATCH omission. CRUD, history atomicity, grant visibility. |\n| Governance/pricing/reporting | 130 | Policies/routing/prices, costs/imports/reconciliation, usage, schedules/runs/CSV; money strings, nullable unknowns, admission identities. Seed ledger/holds/prices, redacted views, snapshots, CSV expiry and tombstones. |\n| Settings/alerts | 70 | Settings/logo writes, rules/events/webhooks; manually decoded bodies and secret-reference variants. Environment locks, storage-off, test throttles, privileged/redacted reads. |\n| Logs/files/batches/legal holds | 56 | Dynamic detail JSON, multipart/binary, creator visibility. Populated logs, uploads/downloads, cancellation, holds and hidden 404s. |\n\nShape evidence: [access_requests.rs:7–25](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/access_requests.rs#L7), [reports.rs:609–625](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/governance/reports.rs#L609), [webhooks.rs:18–30,174–242](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/alerts/webhooks.rs#L18), [files.rs:4–37](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/files.rs#L4).\n\n## 4–6. Follow-through and risks\n\n**#82:** three PRs: separate route/capability/error skeleton; JSON protocols plus SSE event validation; multipart/binary/realtime/jobs plus SDK contracts. Reuse offline validation ([validation.rs:105–116](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management_openapi/validation.rs#L105)), independent route inventory ([authz_matrix.rs:217–243](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/authz_matrix.rs#L217)), and loopback SDK fixtures ([sdk-contracts.mjs:5–21](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/tests/sdk-contracts.mjs#L5)).\n\n**#38 closes** only after exhaustive #81/#82 acceptance, verified releases, consumer readiness, and separately authorized publication—not skeleton completion.\n\nRisks: stub matrices cannot establish handler positives ([authz_admin_token_tests.rs:1–5](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management/authz_admin_token_tests.rs#L1)); generated path schemas prohibit refinement ([generator.rs:192–196,252–265](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/src/management_openapi/generator.rs#L192)); approved dependency proposal `0.33.0` differs from current `0.60.1`, requiring review reconciliation ([Cargo.toml:85–88](/Users/nicholascecere/projects/rust/open-model-gateway/apps/gateway/Cargo.toml#L85)).
